A part whose home settings paint one framework part (the settings' shared `css_element`) has a calibrated slot on its block; the part's first located element (the fact record's instance of its template key) becomes the measuring target of that slot on every node of the block, so the resting pass writes its style values. A part whose settings paint no part, or several, or whose element the record has no instance of, is a note. |
| `lib/fill-state-reason.mjs` | FR-48-81: why a state combination cannot be entered on the page (a token with no host, inside `:has()`, or never exposed to scripts), shared by the state reader and the code reader. |
| `lib/fill-state-pseudo.mjs` | FR-48-81: the values of a state look on a pseudo-element (`.card:hover::after`): its declared values from the matched rules DevTools lists under the element's `pseudoElements`, and its computed style as the render. |
| `lib/fill-logic-state.mjs` | FR-48-13, FR-48-81: the looks behind an `sc-if` logic state. For each state-map row reached by setting state keys (`reach: set-state`), the keys are set through the runtime's own `lib/intake-state.mjs::setDraftState`, every target's declared values and render are read, and the state is put back; the looks come back in `readStates`' shape (`states: [setting state]`, `logic: { id, ids, keys }`) so `fillStates` writes them. A state no vocabulary word names, or whose keys cannot be set, is a gap row with its reason. |
| `lib/fill-open.mjs` | FR-48-13, FR-48-34: Fill's state opener. A surface that exists only while a draft state holds (an intake skeleton root with `viaState`: a drawer, a mega-menu panel), or whose root renders closed (a drawer kept in the page off-canvas) while its handoff record is an open-able kind (`lib/manifest-build.mjs::OPENABLE`: menu, drawer, modal, accordion) and the surface's states name one of its states other than closed or default, is opened before it is read, through `lib/intake-state.mjs::setDraftState` and `::reachDraftState`. `stateOpener` (given `widthKeys`, `lib/state-reach.mjs::widthStateKeys` over intake.json `breakpoints`) takes the states from the handoff record (the record's states named by the surface's `surfaces.json` `states`, by the state's name or its walker key, `lib/manifest-build.mjs::walkerKey`, a state named open first, never closed or default, joined by the same state at another breakpoint: a record state whose keys other than its width bounds equal a bounded candidate's) and lists the `ways` `lib/state-openers.mjs::stateWays` builds from them, the builder intake uses: per state its plain keys (`via: 'state'`, only keys that are state keys of the live component, `facts.json` `state[<component>].keys`, never a render value, a width-derived or underscore key, a width bound, a description such as a placeholder, a non-width range or prose) and its `how_to_reach` setState calls (`via: 'reach'`, `lib/intake-reach-calls.mjs::reachCalls`), a state holding a description trying the calls first; a state with neither is a refusal. A way whose state carries width bounds over a key the draft writes from the width (`{"w": ">= 768"}`, `lib/state-reach.mjs::widthBounds`; an accordion's `{"acc": "0–3"}` is no bound) is offered only at the widths inside them (`waysAt`), and its calls run with the bound keys put back to the viewport's values after them. A record with a route (`withRoute`) is read on that hash route, because a component a route mounts has no live instance before it loads; the entry draft's component is the runtime's `Root` host. `openState(page, opener, width)` tries each way offered at the width until one opens and judges it: an instance reached and one or more of the way's state's `sc_if_tpl_ids` rendered (the verdict names the `state` and `via` that opened), else the state is refused with its reason (`refusalRows`: an UNMAPPED row on the surface root, property `(state opener)`). `openerSource` is the walker config's draft `open` function text: at the page's width it opens the way Fill opened there (`openedAt`, from the read verdicts), else the first way offered at that width, through `lib/intake-state.mjs::logicState` carried as source. `liveOpenerSource(opener, root)` is the live `open` function text: at the widths the draft side opens at, `openOwner` (in-page) clicks the control that owns the surface's live root (the nearest of the root and its ancestors with an id that a control outside it names in `aria-controls`: a nav drawer's `<dialog>` and the header burger; the shown control first, else a hidden one, whose own handler still runs), and leaves a surface already open as it is. `describeWays` is the run log's line (`lib/state-openers.mjs::describeWay` per way). Also exported: `usableKeys(keys, live, own)` (the per-key verdict, `lib/state-openers.mjs::judgeKeys`), `routeHash` (a record route as its hash), `renderedTpls` (the in-page count of the record's sc-if ids rendered), `ROOT_HOST` (the runtime's name for the entry component, `Root`) and `STATE_REFUSED` (the refusal row's property). `withRoute(url, route, routing)` gives a state-page export's route as the `#@state/page=<route>` fragment, and `readUrlOf` is the url Fill reads a surface at. |
| `lib/fill-opener-refusals.mjs` | The state refusals beyond the opener's own: `openerRefusals` takes the read widths' verdicts (`readDraft`'s `opener.widths`) and the motion sampler's (`sampleMotion`'s `opened`) and returns a `{ state, reason }` entry for `lib/fill-open.mjs::refusalRows` when a state opened at no read width, opened at only some (naming the widths not read), or could not be opened for the motion sample at a width (naming it), so no width is dropped without a row. |
| `lib/fill-state-code.mjs` | FR-48-81, R-47-4: the code read of a state no browser can enter. Combinations naming the same states are one cascade over the draft's stylesheet rules (`!important`, then specificity, then source order; `var()` resolved on the target); the look carries `read: "code"` and `rendered: null`, so the render cannot confirm it. |
| `lib/fill-prop.mjs` | One property of one element: which tiers differ from what the node shows, the slots a setting could paint, `clamp()` where a setting accepts one; every write is the resolver's. |
| `lib/fill-equivalents.mjs` | FR-48-8 step 5: a draft property no setting holds, written through a setting for another property on the element or its parent (the framework DB's `property_equivalents`), only under each row's condition. |
| `lib/fill-resolve.mjs` | Root-down resolution of a whole skeleton: walks the nodes, fills content (presence, words, links, media, handover) and each element, lifts rows, lists UNREAD. |
| `lib/fill-route.mjs` | A draft's hash route (`#/apply`) as the permalink of the page map's page for that route. |
| `lib/fill-resolve-state.mjs` | Fill's run state and per-node planning: inherited baselines, the divergence ledger view, spacing ownership per tier. |
| `lib/fill-ancestor.mjs` | FR-48-14b: a host block's part-wide setting written once. After the walk, every `no-setting` row on a child block's part (or on its root when the same draft element is also the part) is offered to the enclosing blocks through `lib/resolve.mjs::resolveViaAncestor` with this module's check in the hop's `ctx.loopBody` injection point: the setting is written once on the host when every measured instance of that part asks the same value at every width read, never when one asks another or the skeleton gave an instance no element; the write is judged by `lib/fill-prop.mjs::resolveProperty` against the host's calibrated default. A setting counts only when the framework DB places it on a part (`css_element`) whose calibrated home the child's part ends. Answered rows are settled (`via: 'host-write'`); the others record `ancestor` in `checks`; a size row and a held row the pass leaves record why under `skipped`. |
| `lib/fill-resolve-element.mjs` | Fill's resolution of one element: every property that differs, written through the resolver, with the not-plain, conflict and fluid records. |
| `lib/fill-spacing.mjs` | Spacing ownership per tier: a flex or grid parent's declared gap first, the render confirming it; otherwise rendered border-box gaps (equal within 0.5px). |
| `lib/fill-binding-group.mjs` | Binding grouping (FR-48-41): the written settings one `{{ }}` style binding feeds on two or more elements are listed once as a group (`groupBindings`), a binding written different values is split; `fill.mjs` runs it and writes `binding-groups.json` |
| `lib/fill-values.mjs` | Fluid fit and `clamp()`, the 16px breakpoint sweep against the SGS boundaries, and the sweep log. |
| `lib/fill-presence.mjs` | Visibility and variant settings from calibration `presence`, words from `text`, links from `link`. |
| `lib/fill-link-attrs.mjs` | A draft link's target, rel, id and aria-label (a picture's role="img" name to its image-alt setting), written to the block's own settings (block.json schema, supports.anchor, calibrated `target` entries) or raised as a named UNMAPPED row. |
| `lib/fill-media.mjs` | FR-48-39 (images): a draft `<img>` or `<figure>` reaches its block's image settings (URL, attachment id, alt, caption) through the media map; an image the map lacks is a named gap; a block whose DB rows name no image setting takes its block.json image trio (`schemaImageSlots`: a split hero's picture), and the skeleton's picture handover on that node is settled (`pictureSettles`); the painted-asset check. `stockEntries(map, site, key)` returns the media-map entries a stock preview names: the `stock: true` files of `site` whose asset is the preview's address without the query. |
| `lib/fill-list-content.mjs` | FR-48-82 (item arrays): a block holding a list (its one `content` array setting, or one child block named by `block_composition.accepts_allowed_blocks`) takes the draft's items, each field to the item key of its name, else of its role (`data/item-roles.json`, read as `ITEM_ROLES`; a key whose primary role is the field's first, a key naming the role later only when the item has none, so a team member's `name` takes a draft `title`), links through the page map, pictures through the media map; what no key holds is a named handover row; a held list is kept. `ITEM_ROLES_FILE` is the path of `data/item-roles.json` (shape `data/item-roles.schema.json`), the roles an item key or draft field can play in priority order (loading throws on a file that breaks the schema or names a role twice); `wordsOf(name)` splits a name into lower-case words; `fieldRoles(name)` returns every role the name's words name, in priority order; `keyRole(name)` returns the first of them; `matchFields(fields, keys)` returns `{ map, unmatched }`: each draft field's item key, a field taking the key of its own name first, else an open key whose primary role it plays; `listTarget(db, block, schemaOf)` returns where a block keeps its list (`{ kind: 'attr', attr, keys }` or `{ kind: 'children', child, keys }`), or null; `itemPicture(value, media)` returns the attachment a picture value names in the media map, or null; `itemAttrs(item, opts)` returns `{ attrs, rows }`, one item's settings and the fields it cannot hold; `withPictures(list, pictures, media)` gives an item whose value names no uploaded picture the picture the draft draws in its place; `listDecisions({ block, target, node, list, media, pages })` returns `{ writes, children, childWrites, rows, notes }` for one list block; `logicReader(folder)` returns a reader of a component's draft logic, read once per run; `listContentWrites({ nodes, flat, refOf, facts, inventory, idxFor, db, schemaOf, ... })` runs the list pass over every node with a draft element and returns `{ writes, children, unmapped, handover, notes }`; `applyChildren(flat, children)` places the children it decided, each list host's inner blocks becoming its items. |
| `lib/fill-list-items.mjs` | FR-48-82: the list the draft draws inside one element: an `sc-for` loop's copies from the rendered skeleton inventory, or every item behind a current-item display from the logic (`lib/intake-ast-eval.mjs`). `holeField(hole, tag)` returns the item field one template hole prints (`{ head, name, image }`), or null; `loopItems({ inventory, facts, rootKey, rootComponent })` returns the loop items the rendered inventory shows inside `rootKey` (the outermost loop whose copies print fields of its own variable), or null; `rootData(idx)` returns what the draft's `renderVals` returns, the template's root data; `currentItems({ facts, rootKey, rootComponent, idx })` returns the items of a current-item display inside `rootKey` (two or more objects an index the code moves picks between), null when it holds none, a reason when it holds several. |
| `lib/fill-fixture.mjs` | A calibration fixture's styling preconditions as Fill sees them: a property a precondition paints in the calibrated rest (the social-icons fixture's 1px border and background switch) is compared with its unstyled value, and a written setting carries the switch the fixture turned on for it. |
| `lib/fill-row-lift.mjs` | Fill's Site Info row lift: the generated icons' shared look (link box as the row's shape element, svg as its glyph element) written once on the `sgs/social-icons` row when every copy agrees; every value the row does not hold (a copy that differs, a property the row has no setting for) is read against the icon's own calibration and written on that icon's own setting, a square 50% box is the shape setting `circle`, and an icon with no such setting is raised by block. |
| `lib/fill-part-pairing.mjs` | A setting the DB places on a part of a block (`css_element` other than `wrapper`) is written only from the draft element paired to that part: a value read from another element (the nav root's `height: 100%` for the burger's size) is refused as UNMAPPED `unpaired-part`. A property that places children (align-items, justify-content, a gap, flex-direction or flex-wrap) read from the draft element laying out the children the part holds is paired to the part (`laysOutPart`: the draft's burger button lays out its bars itself, the block's bar-stack box holds them). |
| `lib/paint-state.mjs` | A block's paint states as Fill judges a node: `matchesPaintState` tests a state's `when` (inner blocks held or not, attributes set or unset) against a tree node, and `calibrationForNode` lays the first matching state's default paint over the block's, property by property per element and width, so an empty Floating layer is judged against the inert paint it renders, not the default instance's. `fillTree` reads every node's calibration through it (`st.calFor( name, rec )`). |
| `lib/fill-same-box.mjs` | Two elements of one block that calibration measures as the same box (a root and its content band): a layout keyword whose setting paints the band is written there, and a size declared on an inner element that is the root's content box goes to the root's setting with the root's own padding and border added. |
| `lib/fill-band.mjs` | The content band's width (Spec 02 `contentWidth`), which no finder reads because the draft has no band element: at 375, 768 and 1440 (1024 and 1920 read lengths only), the draft element's children span its content box (or are sized by themselves, do not stretch, or sit in a flex row, an aligned flex column or a grid) and the band must be at least its padding box, or its stretching children (block-level, in block flow or a stretching flex column) sit in a narrower centred column and the band is that column plus the padding; the node's default is left where it gives every width, else `full`, the cap, or the `normal`/`wide` preset the cap equals (the client snapshot's `settings.layout` sizes), a narrower tier only where the wider one does not hold it. The band setting is the block's DB max-width setting on its `content` slot at the slot calibration measured; the resolver confirms it and formats a px cap. Run per node by `lib/fill-resolve.mjs::fillTree` before the unread list, so a judged band setting is never listed unread. |
| `lib/fill-step-rungs.mjs` | A step between the read widths that a written per-device setting cannot hold (FR-48-50): the sweep's value against the tier read each written property came from, a scoped `@media` rule over each stretch they disagree next to a real step. |
| `lib/fill-state-motion.mjs` | A hover transform or start-padding change the draft's state look shows, as the hover record the motion writer takes: a block whose motion support is declared holds it as its sgsMotion hover layer, the transform as its channels and the padding change as `indent` (the px delta from the resting padding; timing from the property's own entry of the transition list). |
| `lib/fill-unread.mjs` | What Fill leaves unjudged on a measured node: style settings whose calibrated element no finder read, presence settings with no reading or no read element, text and link settings with no reading, and layout or mode enums calibration reads no presence for; never a setting the skeleton sets or Fill wrote. |
| `lib/brand-registry.mjs` | Reads the one brand and contact registry (`plugins/sgs-blocks/includes/data/brand-registry.json`) and derives which addresses belong to which brand, and which colour tokens are named for one (`brandNames`, `brandOfToken`: slug or Site Info name of three letters or more, label words, `aliases`) (scheme for phone and email, domain for socials); no platform table of its own. `reviewPlatformBlocks(db)` takes the framework DB (optional) and returns the review-platform blocks in it (`sgs/<brand>-reviews`; the roster is read, never listed); `paletteBrands(brands, blocks)` takes the registry brands and those blocks and returns the brands a palette token can be named for: the registry's, plus one per review-platform block whose brand the registry lacks, carrying `reviewBlock` (the block that paints its colour itself). |
| `lib/fill-handover.mjs` | The handover entry shape Fill declares, validated against the five shared owners and four kinds. |
| `lib/fill-page.mjs` | The page baseline for inherited properties from the theme snapshot (root typography and colour, heading and element styles, CSS initial values), which calibration deliberately does not record. |
| `lib/fill-entrance.mjs` | Samples a draft's entrances at 375, 768 and 1440 (delay, duration, first-frame pose, load or scroll trigger), sweeping down the page so scroll reveals play, and each element's endless animations as the code made them (timing, keyframes, box, `<marquee>` attributes, SVG animation elements): the motion writer's render check and the source of the values the code computes at run time. A surface that exists only while a draft state holds passes its `opener` and the page's state is set (through `lib/fill-open.mjs::openState`) before the probe reads, so the entrances are those of the open drawer or panel. |
| `lib/fill-motion.mjs` | The motion cloning writer (Spec 48 FR-48-78): each skeleton node's inventory motion records as its `sgsMotion`, on the root or the declared part a slot names, render-checked, token-snapped and validated before it is written. |
| `lib/fill-motion-element.mjs` | FR-48-78, FR-48-47, Spec 38A FR-38A-33: the motion layers one draft element makes on one target for `lib/fill-motion.mjs::motionWrites`: its entrance (render-checked at 375, 768 and 1440, token-snapped, preset-named), its hover state with the paint timing, and the effects and Additional CSS its endless animations make. `snapLayer` is the token snapping every layer of the writer goes through. |
| `lib/fill-motion-stagger.mjs` | FR-48-47, Spec 38A section 8: the stagger a draft's list entrance makes. A one-shot entrance played on each item of a list with a delay computed from the item's index (`Math.min( i * 26, 320 )`, `140 + i * 55`) is the `sgsMotion.stagger` of the block holding the items (offset, step, cap), with the items' own entrance as that block's root entrance; an index formula the helper cannot hold is a named unmapped row. |
| `lib/motion-layers.mjs` | One element's motion records as `entrance.base` (from and to, or `values.keyframes` for a record with more than two keyframes) and `hover.state` layers, and the match of a run-time-picked entrance to a measured pose. |
| `lib/motion-states.mjs` | Which published motion state or open part a draft's state-triggered animation or transition plays on (the state's words against the block's `motion_support`, the open words from the `states` table). |
| `lib/motion-set.mjs` | An SVG `<set>` that switches opacity or visibility once after load as the held entrance it can be, and the reason for any other. |
| `lib/motion-check.mjs` | The motion writer's render check: a sampled entrance against the draft's code for duration and delay (device overrides) and for the from-pose and easing (reported only). |
| `lib/motion-sample.mjs` | The sampled-pose check (FR-48-47): the pose the draft's code states at 0, half and all of an entrance's run (its keyframes, easing and direction), against the pose the probe read after pausing and seeking each animation there. |
| `lib/css-matrix.mjs` | A CSS transform list as the 4x4 matrix a browser draws it with, in the list's own order, so the render check compares the code's from transform with the transform the render drew; a percentage or unknown function is unreadable (null). |
| `lib/motion-clip.mjs` | A clip-path `inset()` as the entrance clip channel, one inset edge as `clipEdge`; other shapes are unsupported. |
| `lib/motion-shadow.mjs` | A box-shadow's parts (inset, offsets, blur, spread, colour), so a ring or an accent-line hover is told from a drop shadow. |
| `lib/motion-channels.mjs` | CSS values as the motion schema's channels: lengths, angles, transform and filter lists, a record's from and to poses, and the keyframe stops of a record that has more than two. |
| `lib/motion-effects.mjs` | The one effect writer (`writeEffect`): an effect slug and values as a layer of its panel's list, placed where the editor's Effects panel offers it (qualifying blocks, target level, part kind), every value checked against the DB's fields; FR-48-77's recognisers call it too. |
| `lib/motion-loops.mjs` | A draft's endless animations as effects: the smallest effect whose owned channels cover the loop and whose values reproduce it (marquee px/s from travel over loop time, ambient float, SVG pulse, Ken Burns), a `<marquee>` and SVG `<animate>` as loops, and a named Spec 38 gap for a loop no effect draws. |
| `lib/motion-loop-css.mjs` | A draft's endless keyframe animation that no motion effect draws, as the CSS of the block's Additional CSS box (`sgsCustomCss`): reduced-motion gated, `linear` over stops sampled from the whole-cycle easing curve (the Web Animations API eases once across the iteration; CSS eases each keyframe interval), the fewest stops whose error stays within 0.75 degrees and 0.005 scale. |
| `lib/motion-db.mjs` | The framework DB's motion facts, read-only: targets per block, fields per layer, presets, the effect registry. |
| `lib/motion-tokens.mjs` | Token references (the field types' token patterns read from the schema, the values from the client's snapshot) and the preset match. |
| `lib/motion-behaviour-settings.mjs` | FR-48-77: the script behaviours SGS expresses as block settings rather than motion layers: the header's scrolled shadow and sticky bar per tier, the open-status dot pulse, scrolling to an opened accordion row, the testimonial slider's autoplay and the pinned timeline's track, counter, focus and active year. |
| `lib/motion-effect-element.mjs` | The effect element of a Web Animation that runs on an element its script creates (a sheen band, a ripple circle): the declarations the script gives that element before it animates it, read from the inventory's call path and JS style-write records, for the sheen band's width and the ripple's diameter and colour. |
| `lib/motion-record-text.mjs` | The reading helpers the effect recogniser and the settings recognisers share: a record's properties, words, trigger and timing, and the number patterns its formulas are read with. |
| `lib/motion-tilt.mjs` | FR-48-77, Spec 38A FR-38A-41: the tilt recognisers. A script-written rotation from the pointer's position is Follow pointer, the pointer position written into custom properties for a highlight is its glare layer, and a Web Animation back to no rotation on pointer leave is its reset. |
| `lib/state-reach.mjs` | The width helpers of a state opener (`lib/state-openers.mjs`, intake's `openers`, Fill's `stateOpener`): `widthBounds(st, widthKeys)` (a comparison or range value of one of `widthKeys` as whole-pixel `{ key, min, max }` bounds: a breakpoint the viewport holds, never a value; any other key, an index range or a count, is no bound, and with no width keys nothing is), `fitsWidth`, `waysHeldAt(ways, width)` (the ways whose bounds hold at a width, Fill's `waysAt` and intake's `wayAt`) and `widthStateKeys(breakpoints)` (component to the state keys its thresholds compare, `state.w` or `effW (state.w)`, from intake's breakpoints). |
| `lib/fill-config.mjs` | Generates the walker config: one pair per node with a `draftRef`, `refPrefix: 'cr-ref-'` and the ledger path, via `pairs.mjs::configText`. A state-driven surface's draft side opens the same state Fill read (`lib/fill-open.mjs::openerSource`) in place of the click-text open, and its live side opens the surface's live root through the control that owns it (`lib/fill-open.mjs::liveOpenerSource`), so neither side of a pair is compared closed. Every state the surface maps is a walker state (`lib/walker-states.mjs`): each later one loads afresh (`from: 'rest'`) with its own draft and live action, a width-bound one carries its `widths`, and the mapped states Fill cannot open are listed under `unwalked` with their reasons. `WALK_WIDTHS` (375, 768, 1440, 1920) are the widths a generated config walks (the walker's standard run, Spec 48 §3.6 item 2). |
| `lib/fill-report.mjs` | `fill-report.md` and `fill-report.json`: UNMAPPED (each row keeps the `checks` and `unchecked` fields the resolver recorded), handover, writes, spacing, fluid, breakpoints, entrances, ledger, snaps. |
| `lib/css-families.mjs` | CSS shorthand families derived from mdn-data (edge, corner and axis families such as `padding`, `border-radius`, `gap`, `overflow`, `inset`, `place-*`; mixed shorthands such as `outline` known but never reached from a part): the one key normaliser for physical and logical spellings, the fold of parts into a shorthand value, and a paint completed both ways. |
| `lib/css-equivalence/index.mjs` | FR-48-86: the equivalence engine, consulted before any "no setting" verdict so a draft declaration reaches every setting that paints the same result whatever its property name. Five layers in order: spelling (`lib/css-families.mjs`), canonical values and the calibrated default paint (`values.mjs`, `effect.mjs::paintAt`; the paint of a part that calibration measured as the same box through `lib/fill-same-box.mjs::calSameBox`, an inline display compared blockified under the draft's flex or grid parent, and a state value that is the property's initial value where the block paints it at rest AND the draft itself rests at it (`input.rest`, per width; an unknown resting value holds nothing), which a state's shorthand resets to), inert values (`inert.mjs`), the setting whose measured effect equals the draft (`effect.mjs`), and a conditional `property_equivalents` row. `consult(input, ctx)` returns a write, a held reason (`EQUIVALENCE_HELD`) or a named home. |
| `lib/css-equivalence/keywords.mjs` | Layer 2 of the equivalence engine (FR-48-86), alignment keywords: `flex-start`/`start` and `flex-end`/`end` align to the same edge in a grid, and in a flex container unless `flex-wrap: wrap-reverse` (cross axis) or a `*-reverse` flex-direction (main axis) swaps it; an unread context keeps them apart. `lib/resolve.mjs::formatValue` reads an enum spelt `start`/`end` through it, with the draft element's own display, flex-wrap and flex-direction. |
| `lib/css-equivalence/values.mjs` | Layer 2 of the equivalence engine (FR-48-86): canonical values. Two values paint the same when their canonical forms are equal: a timing keyword is its `cubic-bezier()`, a time is milliseconds, a transform list its composed matrix, any other value the shared tolerance rule; a shorthand's value is projected onto one of its longhands. |
| `lib/css-equivalence/tolerance.mjs` | The one rule two computed values compare by (FR-48-86), shared by the equivalence engine, Fill's tier comparison (`lib/fill-prop.mjs::tiersDiffering`) and the live judge of UNMAPPED rows (`lib/confirm-live.mjs::judgeUnmapped`): a keyword is first read as the value the browser paints for it where the element's context decides that, then two lengths agree within half a pixel, except letter and word spacing, which compare exactly. An inline-level `display` is its block-level twin on a flex or grid item and on an out-of-flow box (blockification, `paintCanon`). |
| `lib/css-equivalence/inert.mjs` | Layer 3 of the equivalence engine (FR-48-86): values that paint nothing. A value is inert at a width when the property does not apply to the element there (judged from its own display and position and its parent's display), when the element is not rendered there, or when it is the property's initial value and calibration measured the block painting that initial value too; a case the context cannot decide is undecided, never inert. |
| `lib/css-equivalence/effect.mjs` | Layer 4 of the equivalence engine (FR-48-86): the setting's measured effect replaces its declared name. A draft longhand is matched, under each of its spellings, against every property, slot, reach, side effect and discovered value calibration recorded; a value is written only where calibration measured it, and `timingHome` answers which setting holds a timing longhand on a part. |
| `lib/fill-held.mjs` | The "already held" classifier: an UNMAPPED row the element already renders leaves the list under `covered`, `inherited` or `default` (`inherited` also holds a font longhand on a leaf that prints no text and paints no generated content, equal to what its parent renders; a value that differs from the parent, a font-size or an icon font's face, stays raised), and is listed under its reason in `fill-report.md`. |
| `lib/fill-census.mjs` | The Fill census: classifies every UNMAPPED and already-held row of a Fill run by its reason text into the named classes of `data/census-classes.json`; a row no class matches is a new gap kind. |
| `lib/census-mechanism.mjs` | The mechanism census library: walker rows de-duplicated by (width, state, element identity, kind, key, values) and run states that read the same collapsed (`walkerItems`); the Fill run's unmapped and already-held rows joined to them by (ref, slot or path, property, state) into sides `walker-only`, `fill-unwritten`, `fill-held` and `both` (`surfaceRows`); the block family from the framework DB read-only (`blockFamily`, the root of the parent chain; `rowBlockFamily` gives a slug the DB does not hold the `unregistered:<slug>` family and a row with no slug the `(no block read)` family), the property family from `lib/css-families.mjs` (`propertyFamily`), the tier pattern (`tierPattern`); and the signature and grouping across clients (`signatureOf`, `groupRows`; the parts in order are `SIGNATURE_PARTS`). A row with a missing part has no signature. Helpers: `blockLookup(db)` (the DB's slug to parent map), `refBlocks(tree)` (a Fill tree's ref to block slug), `fillRows(run)` (a Fill run's unmapped and already-held rows). `partParts(row)` reads a walker part or effect row's key (`<part name> <hover|press|rest>[:<property>]`) and sides into `{ state, family }`, the client's part name left out of the family (an effect row's family names the side that lacks the effect); null for any other row. `fillState(reason)` returns the interaction state a Fill row speaks for, read from its reason text. |
| `lib/fill-confirm.mjs` | FR-48-8, FR-48-36: the confirm record for every written value (declared against rendered draft by property type, the verdict; the rendered-live half is `lib/confirm-live.mjs`). |
| `lib/confirm-live.mjs` | FR-48-36: the rendered-live half of the confirm record. `fillLive(records, report, {stateMap})` pairs each record with its live element by node and element path in the walker report (never by words; a mispaired, missing or doubly-named element stays `open` with `liveWhy`), compares rendered draft with rendered live (R–L, lengths ±1px) and applies the verdict table (`liveVerdict`); `writeConfirmLive` writes `confirm-live.json` beside Solve's report. Run by `solve.mjs --fill-report <fill-report.json>`. |
| `lib/solve-rows.mjs` | Solve's reading of a walker report: open rows, writable groups, draft values per width, the state-conflict refusal, content rows (text, presence, link) and their resolution, the handover list, classification. |
| `lib/solve-report.mjs` | Writes `solve-report.md` and `solve-report.json` (the whole-page line, content and handover counts, the handover list, the issue and cause views, and a Hardcode table with the widths that differ and the winning rule). |
| `lib/solve-groups.mjs` | The report's two readings of the surviving style and hover rows: issues (one element, property and state, every width with its own draft and live values, and the widths that match) and causes (issues sharing a class, a property and the winning rule or the same values, every element listed with its widths). No row is merged away. |
| `lib/solve-journal.mjs` | FR-48-5: Solve's crash report. A write-ahead record of every setting written since the last judged walk, written before the tree file is saved and cleared once a walk has judged those writes, so a run that stops early leaves it naming the writes no walk judged; the next run reads it first and judges them against the walk they were computed from. |
| `lib/solve-loop.mjs` | R-47-9: the build, walk and write rounds. At most `maxRounds` write rounds, guard rounds (revert a named culprit, try one suspect, restore an innocent one) not counted; `maxRounds` 0 measures only. Every step is passed in. |
| `lib/solve-loop-body.mjs` | FR-48-14: Solve's half of the loop-body check. Loads the fact record, the surface's origin map and the served root component that `lib/loop-body.mjs::loopBodyCheck` needs on a real run; with any input missing the hop keeps its one-descendant rule and the reason is returned for the run log. |
| `lib/solve-summary.mjs` | The run summary line `solve.mjs` prints for the live judge: the confirm verdict counts beside the UNMAPPED rows held and raised. |
| `lib/winning-rule.mjs` | For Hardcode rows: the rule that wins on the live page and the rule carrying the draft value, read through Chrome DevTools' matched rules (read-only, headed unless `--headless`, rest state only). |
| `lib/references.mjs` | Reference blocks found from each block's render.php (linked placeholders, frames around another post's blocks, core template parts) and the surfaces lint that every printed post has a surface. `themeContentParts(file)` returns the theme's own content parts (theme.json `templateParts` outside the header and footer areas), which a template copy prints as the theme ships them. |
| `lib/entrance.mjs` | Entrance start: writes no motion between Spec 38A M6.6 and M7 (the only motion attribute is `sgsMotion`, written by the M7 route), so `entranceStart` always returns null and `groupRects` reads the live element's rect per width. |
| `lib/fill-diagram.mjs` | A rendered draft's dimension diagram measured into `sgs/diagram-dimension` settings (line ends, guide reach and overshoot, tick length, label anchor), from the SVG geometry API and the label's box, never the source text (R-47-4). |
| `lib/pairs.mjs` | Block pairing: words per block, their draft twins (repeated words planned apart), the partner choice (element, padded wrapper or text run), the keep-or-leave-out judgement (PAIRING_LIMITS) and the generated config's text. |
| `lib/origin-pairs.mjs` | The skeleton's origin pairs: each node's draft element against its block (`skeletonPairs`), a width variant scoped by its finder (`variantScope`), and a part the block renders whose draft element sits outside the block's own (`outsideSlotPairs`), with the slots it cannot pair (the node's own element, a key missing from the inventory, a width-variant node) in `left`.; `tplSelector(key, { inner })`, the document-wide selector for the element a tpl key names inside its own import host, or null when no selector names it alone. |
| `lib/pairs-page.mjs` | Block pairing's in-page collectors: tagged words, live block boxes and text runs, draft chains (repeated words placed nearest the sure ones), form controls by identity, hand pair elements, and the draft opened through its navigation. |
| `lib/pairs-recheck.mjs` | The width re-check of a pairing run: each kept draft finder is read again at the re-check widths, and a block whose finder fails at one is paired again there and keeps one joined finder or is left out. |
| `lib/identity.mjs` | Exact draft identity for pairing: structural alignment of a skeleton with a committed tree (never word pairs), the agree-or-mispaired judgement of the word matcher's element against the identity element, the in-page relation reader, and the element fingerprint (tag, class, style hash, ancestor tags, own-words hash, template line) with `realign`, which maps a previous export's keys to the next export's. |
| `lib/fact-key.mjs` | The two key levels of a draft element, built and read in one place: the template key `<dc file>/<tpl>` and the instance key `<chain>/<tpl>#<copy>`. No other module builds one with a template literal (`tests/fact-key.test.mjs`). |
| `lib/freshness.mjs` | Whether a saved record still describes the export on disk: `sources` (the sha256 of every draft, every `*.js` file at any depth, `README.md`, `surfaces.json`, `animations.json`, every file under `assets/`, `tokens/*.css`, and the route commit) and `assertCurrent`, which every reader of an intake record or saved inventory calls. |
| `lib/backlog-recheck.mjs` | FR-48-68: `recheckEntry(entry, env)` re-runs G3 to G6 (`lib/gate-chain.mjs`'s own gate functions) for every width of one backlog entry against the injected environment `{ db, cache(block), states, probes }`, and `recheckBacklog({ backlog, envFor, commit, today })` closes a row only on positive evidence from the row's own class that the gap no longer holds (F-setting: a setting now reaches the element or a trial now changes the property; F-hardcode: the winning-rule probe now names no SGS rule; F-block: a roster block now matches; F-behaviour: the flow now agrees on a fresh walk; a G5 reach closes only an F-setting); a walker state the state map does not map, a throw, a missing input or any other failure leaves the row open with the reason (evidence rendered by `lib/gate-evidence.mjs::renderEvidence`); a closed row is never re-run. |
| `lib/framework-backlog.mjs` | FR-48-68: the cross-client framework backlog. `BACKLOG_FILE` (`.claude/reports/framework-backlog.json`), `SITES_DIR`, `backlogKey` (`block\|attribute\|property`, the property the CSS property the gates read, `lib/solve-rows.mjs::cssProp`; each entry keeps the walker's raw key as `rowKey`), `readGapsFile` (throws, naming the file, when it holds no rows array), `findGapsFiles`, `readBacklog`, `writeBacklog` and `mergeBacklog({ backlog, sources, mergedOn })`, which only adds and refreshes; a row closes through `lib/backlog-recheck.mjs`, and a row that is not a well-formed F-class row, or whose property is no CSS property, is refused and counted. |
| `lib/census-conservation.mjs` | The conservation check of a Fill run: every property the draft declares on a paired part (the run's read set) must end written, held or unwritten. `READ_FILE` (`reads.json`), `READ_VERSION` (2: the run binding; a version 1 read set is refused), `STATE` (`rest`), `fileSha`, `longhandsOf` (a property and every longhand it sets), `outcomesOf`, `conservation({ reads, report, unmapped })` (also `widthlessHolds`: the conserved reads only an `alreadyHeld` row answers, which records no width), `groupFailures`, `readRowsOf`, `bindingProblem(raw, dir, report, root)` and `conservationRun(dir, { root })` (`checked`, `no-read-set` or `unbound`). Motion writers answer every transition and animation longhand of their element; a state look is judged by the state check, not here. |
| `lib/census-reads.mjs` | `readSetFile({ client, surface, run, inputs, reads, nodes, refs })`: the reads.json object of a Fill run, every longhand the draft's code declares on a measured part per read width, bound to the run; `inputHashes({ root, skeleton, draft })` (each file's repository path and sha256). |
| `lib/census-drops-report.mjs` | The conservation drops as Markdown: `runStatus(dir)` (a run folder with no `fill-report.json` is `incomplete`, one with no `reads.json` `no-read-set`; neither counts clean), `newestChecked(runs)` (the newest checked run per client and surface) and `dropsReport(dirs, stamp)`. |
| `lib/census-gap-class.mjs` | FR-48-65 classes for census rows: `gitFreshness({ repo, db })` (the DB-versus-code check per block), `baseClass(row)` (the Fill reason's class, `held`, or `unassessed`), `classifyRows(rows, { db, calFor, calFiles, freshness })` (the instrument gates then turn a row into its I-class; `gitFreshness` gives its evidence as the git and DB commands that produced it; a row whose Fill reason is itself I-calibration takes its evidence from `calibrationEvidence(row, cal, files)`: the absent slot-map file, the missing paint, or the paint key at the row's slot where no width holds a reading of its property, naming the paint's own readKey; none where the paint does hold it, and none for a row in a state other than default, whose state paint the cache does not hold, except an absent slot map) and `classCounts(rows)` (each `instrument` entry carries the row's structured `evidence`; an I-class row without it throws). |
| `lib/gap-classes.mjs` | The one place a finding becomes one of FR-48-65's classes: `GAP_CLASSES`, `I_CLASSES`, `UNASSESSED`, `HELD`, `UNCHECKED` (W-unchecked), `REQUIRED_CHECKS`, `gapClassOf`, `mapTriage`, `mapResolverGap(gap, row)` (a `no-setting` row is held when its `code` is `held:*` or its reason says `held (default)`, F-setting only when its `checks` hold all of `REQUIRED_CHECKS` and `unchecked` is empty, else W-unchecked; a row from an older run with no `checks` is W-unchecked), `withDiscovery`, `ancestorsOf`, `staleCheck`, `instrumentFinding` (a NULL-routed, mistagged, uncalibrated or stale fitting setting makes the row an I-class row; its `attribute` is the setting the failing G3 or G4 itself judged) and `classify`. An unknown triage class, reason or class name throws; nothing defaults to a class. |
| `lib/gap-input.mjs` | `gapInput({ issue, row, res }, ctx)`: one gaps.json input row from a Solve issue and its chain result; every field comes from the row's own data and a value the data lacks is null; `property` is the CSS property the row's walker key names (`lib/solve-rows.mjs::cssProp`, null when it names none) and `rowKey` the raw key; `draftId` is `ctx.draftIdOf(row)` (the origin-map join, `lib/draft-identity.mjs`) or null, never the walker pair, which keeps its own field, and `readAt` is the width the first row was read at. An I-class row's `attribute` is the setting the failing gate judged (`res.setting`). A class outside FR-48-65 is a chain defect and throws. |
| `lib/gate-evidence.mjs` | FR-48-66 evidence: `fromCommand(command, line)`, `fromFile(file, key, line)`, `isEvidence` (a command with its output line, or a file with a key; free text is not evidence) and `renderEvidence` (one readable line for the CLI). |
| `lib/instrument-debt.mjs` | FR-48-67: `debtPath(client, root)`, `debtRow(r, { surface, known })` (throws on a non-I-class row, one with no evidence or one naming no surface; a known item's id is `<surface>:known:<key>`), `debtRows`, `knownRows({ rows, inTree, surface })` (the known items, both kinds, kept only where the surface's tree holds their block), `unroutedKnown({ db, isDiscovered })` (the NULL `css_property` items of `data/instrument-debt-known.json`), `staleSlotMaps({ cacheDir, readKey, localHash })` and `writeDebt({ file, rows, surface, publishedOn })`, grouped by owner: a surface's run replaces its own rows, known items included, and keeps every other surface's. |
| `lib/rule-origin.mjs` | `ruleOrigin(rule)` gives `inline`, `custom-css` (a block's `sgsCustomCss` residual, by its `sgs-custom-css-<uid>` element or the doubled `.sgs-c-<uid>` scope once lifted into the consolidated sheet; never when the rule's stylesheet is WordPress-, theme- or SGS-owned, by its owner element id (core's ids, or any `sgs-` handle other than the residual's `sgs-custom-css-` element and the registry's `sgs-blocks-collected` sheet) or `sheet` href, whatever the selector) or `stylesheet`; `withOrigin(list)` tags each rule. |
| `lib/cite-emission.mjs` | FR-48-15: a canvas citation is canvas-settable only on an emission proof, never on PHP source text. `samePath(calibrated, elementPath)` (the same steps, and the same `:nth-of-type` index where both sides carry one), `calibrationEmits` (the setting's slots or reaches name the row's element by `samePath`; a sibling citation, elementPath null, is never proven by calibration), `measuredEmitsFrom` (a CONFIRMED confirm-canvas verdict), `emissionProof` (`calibration`, `measured` or null), `citedElementPath` and `hopCanvasSettable`. |
| `lib/delivery-bind.mjs` | Binds a delivery-map animation record's tree evidence to the block that renders its own draft element: `nodeElementKeys(node, rootComponent)`, `recordElementKeys(record)` and `boundNodes(record, facts)` giving `{ nodes, why }`. |
| `lib/delivery-state-files.mjs` | Whether a done `state:<surface>:<name>` key's commit changed that surface's files: `surfaceFiles(client, surfaceId, root)` (from `build/surfaces.json` and the intake skeletons), `commitFiles(hash, root)` and `stateCommitEvidence(key, commit, { filesOf, changedIn })` giving `{ ok, why }`. |
| `lib/draft-branches.mjs` | FR-48-12 item 2: a blank-draft row classed by the draft's `sc-if` branches. `draftIdOfKey`, `instanceOf`, `branchIndex`, `branchVerdict`, `classifyBlankDraft` (an unwalked branch is neither written nor a gap) and `walkedStates`. |
| `lib/draft-branches-io.mjs` | The files `branchIndex` joins for one surface: `rootComponentOf(intake, draftFile)` and `loadDraftBranches({ client, surface, buildDir, surfaces, loadFactsFn, vocabFn })`, returning `{ draftOf }` or `{ reason }` naming the missing or refused input. |
| `lib/wp-run-session.mjs` | One logged-in browser for a run that builds pages (`openRunSession(site, env, timeout)`; `openDetachedSession(site, env, timeout)`, the same browser as its own process for a run that starts its children synchronously, as `site-build.mjs` does; `closeStrayTabs`, `readEnv`): launched once with a per-site profile under `cache/` (the login survives between runs), exported as `SGS_CDP_URL` so every `scripts/wp-build-page.js` child attaches instead of launching and logging in itself; used by `calibrate.mjs`, `site-build.mjs` and `site-run.mjs`, so a Hostinger edge that blocks bursts of automated logins sees one session. |
| `lib/content-sync.mjs` | `readPhp`, `writePhp` (the `wp eval-file` code, its request as base64 in the code, content through `wp_slash`), `syncTargets(surfaces, only)`, `planSync(from, to)` → `{ posts, templates, same, problems }` for `content-sync.mjs`. `writtenMismatches(plan, mirror, target)` returns, after a write, the written posts, templates and parts (named `post 5`, `template single`, `part header`) whose md5 on the target is not the mirror's, or which the target no longer has. |
| `lib/walker-lint.mjs` | FR-48-12 item 1: the walker config lint as a route gate, using the walker's own `lintRun`: `lintWalker(walker, states)`, `walkerLintFault(surface, walker, states)` (null, else the message that stops the run) and `lintClient(buildDir)`. |
| `lib/walk-lint-gate.mjs` | FR-48-12 for the gate chain's walks: `walkLintGuard({ surface, walker, root, states, lint })` returns an async check `guard(state)` that lints the config for that state alone, as the walker lints the `--states` it walks (a fault in another state does not refuse; `guard()` lints `states`), keeps a pass per state and never a refusal (a fixed config is linted again), and rejects a faulty one with `lintRefusal({ walker, root, fault })`, an error of `gateClass` W-walker whose `evidence` is `{ file, key: 'lint', line: the fault }`. |
| `data/instrument-debt-known.json` | The known I-class items FR-48-67 names (`unrouted`: block and attribute), each confirmed against the framework DB by `lib/instrument-debt.mjs::unroutedKnown` before it is written. |
| `lib/gate-chain.mjs` | FR-48-64: the gates G0 to G7 (`g0Freshness` to `g7Recheck`, each taking its inputs as one argument and returning `{ pass, class, evidence }`) and `runChain(row, ctx)`, which stops at the first failing gate and returns `{ gate, class, evidence, gates, candidate, settings, setting }` (`setting`: the `{ block, attr }` a failing G3 or G4 judged, else null). G3 passes a routed fitting setting on the row's state before it considers a NULL-routed name fit; a NULL row's name is read against the DB's routed `css_property` values (`lib/name-fit.mjs::compoundSpelled`) and its state from the DB's `css_state` values (`nameState`). G0 includes the browser and Playwright version check against the cache. G2 takes `unread` (null, or why the walk could not read the declared value) and fails W-walker on a reason. An input builder's thrown `gateClass` names the failing class (G0: a node the surface tree lacks is W-route, a row naming no node W-walker; only an unreadable tree is I-stale). DB reads are read-only; emission, probe and re-check are injected functions (`lib/gate-probes.mjs` supplies the live ones). A G6 or G7 probe that throws a W-walker error carrying structured evidence (`lib/walk-lint-gate.mjs::lintRefusal`) fails W-walker with that evidence. |
| `lib/gate-probes.mjs` | FR-48-64 G5 to G7: `createProbes({ origin, db, states, openPage, walk, renderBlock, today })` returns `{ measure, probe, recheck, close }`. `measure` lists every rule that matches the live element and declares a setting's `css_property` (value independent) and reaches when one names the setting's block (`belongsTo`); `probe` runs the missing-setting trial (an SGS rule on the element, or a candidate setting written with a probe value through the block renderer), the hardcode read (the rule whose removal changes the computed value, found in the page and put back), the roster grep and the flow probe; `recheck` reads a fresh walk shared per state and width. Every rule it reads carries its origin (`lib/rule-origin.mjs`): a rule in a block's `sgsCustomCss` residual is the route's own write, never a framework rule, so it cannot make a gap look like a hardcode or a reach. A hover row is measured with the pointer on the element; a state a probe cannot reproduce, an absent element, rules that cannot be read and any missing input throw, so the gate fails with its own class. `liveTarget(config)` gives the live origin and the surface's own live page from a walker config's live url (an `SGS_LIVE_ORIGIN` override keeps the path). |
| `lib/gate-probes-page.mjs` | The in-page halves of the probes, passed to `page.evaluate`: `enumerateInPage` (the matching declaring rules, counting stylesheets and rules it could not read) and `winnerInPage` (the removal trial). |
| `lib/gaps-file.mjs` | FR-48-66: `gapRow` (one gaps.json row: id `<surface>:<issue key>`, class, owner, draftId (intake's draft id or null) beside the walker `pair`, `property` (the CSS property) beside `rowKey` (the walker's raw key), `readAt` (the width the values and evidence were read at), the eight gates with status and evidence, firstSeen, publishedOn, `killed: false` until the kill line is built, `framework` for the F-classes; refuses a passed gate with no evidence, evidence that is free text rather than a command and its output line or a file and a key (`lib/gate-evidence.mjs::isEvidence`), a missing gate, an unknown class or an impossible candidacy), `writeGaps` (drops rows a fresh walk closed, keeps firstSeen and other surfaces' rows, checks every row before touching the file), `gapsPath` and `OWNERS`. |
| `lib/read-template.mjs` | The template reader (FR-48-7, Spec 48A §3): one record per element of the runtime's annotated template, from the census's read: tag and real tag, every attribute under its real name, the static `style` and `style-<pseudo>` declarations as longhand pairs (expanded by the browser's CSS parser), each hole with its place and logic path, the template parent, real parent and parent chain, `sc-if`, `sc-for` and `dc-import` membership, ref, hooks, events and fingerprint. |
| `lib/read-logic.mjs` | The logic reader (FR-48-7, READING-DC-FILES §1): in the sandboxed page, at 375, 768 and 1440 set by resizing the viewport, the value each hole resolves to through the live instance's `renderVals()` (loop holes per item), for every element including those a width does not render; a component the entry never mounts is read in its own file; an unresolved hole is a gap row. The page is opened with its `setInterval` stopped (`lib/intake-sandbox.mjs::visit` `stopIntervals`), so a carousel index or countdown stays where it starts and two reads of one folder agree. |
| `lib/read-logic-statics.mjs` | FR-48-7: in the sandboxed page, each live logic instance's statics, getters and instance fields that hold arrays or plain objects (content arrays, menus, catalogues, form schemas) as `logic.statics` (name, source, kind, length, capped value, functions dropped and named; each entry read twice and kept only when both reads agree, a value that changed between them listed under the component's `dropped` with reason `volatile`; the viewport `width` the getters were read at; a node budget per component and per record, `truncated: true` when it is hit); each hole's value with a surface state's non-width `state_keys` applied through the instance's own `setState` (put back after every read, a read that throws caught and listed as a gap row), kept per element as `holesByState` only where it differs from resting (value, scope count or loop flag) and the element sits inside the state's surface (its roots' subtrees or its `sc_if_tpl_ids` members), the other changed elements listed once per state in `logic.stateSideEffects`; the paths under `assets/` those values name (a hole's path makes the hole's element a user, a static's is an `assets.logicRefs` row), so `lib/read-assets.mjs` marks the files used. |
| `lib/read-logic-conditions.mjs` | The width condition of each `sc-if`, read from the logic's syntax tree (`{ w: '>=', n: 1260 }`, `{ bp: 'm', is, when }` for a breakpoint token the logic derives from the width, `all` / `any`); the draft's logic is parsed, never run, in Node. |
| `lib/read-surfaces.mjs` | The surfaces reader (FR-48-7): each element's `surfaces.json` records (id, kind, parent, root, `closed_by`, `time_dependent`, `unresolved`; `states` are the record's states whose `sc_if_tpl_ids` hold it or an ancestor, `otherStates` the rest, each with `state_keys`, `animation_ids` and `in_sc_if`, all as the file states them), each surface's part roots and each page record's `blocks` (`pages`). |
| `lib/read-animations.mjs` | The animations reader (FR-48-7): each element's `animations.json` records, those that target it (`self`) or an element under it (`descendant`, by the parent chain the template reader holds), each with id, name, mechanism, group, status (`flagged` carries a `dead-code` or `no-op` status; the record is kept), `trigger` (type, detail, state keys), `breakpoints`, `routes`, `reduced_motion`, the target template key and whether it came from `template_tpl_id` or the `nearest_tpl_id` fallback. A record with no element, or whose element the template lacks, is listed in `unjoined` with its reason. Plain JSON, no draft code. No stage consumes it yet. |
| `lib/read-assets.mjs` | The assets reader (FR-48-7): each file under `assets/` with its intrinsic ratio (SVG `viewBox`, else `width`/`height`; PNG, JPEG, WebP and GIF sizes from the file header, no dependency), sha256, whether an SVG animates (`<animate*>`, `<set>`, `@keyframes`) and its `<g>` ids; and each template element's `assets` (`path`, `ratio`, `kind`, `sha256`, `animated`, `via`), matched by handoff-relative path from the element's own attributes, `url(...)` in its static declarations, or the path a project script (`<tag>.js`) maps its attribute value to (only for an attribute the script reads: its `observedAttributes`, else its `getAttribute` calls; looked up in a `Map`, string values only). A path a hole or static of the logic names makes the hole's element a user (`via: 'logic:<hole id>'`); a static's path with no element is listed in `assets.logicRefs`. A value with a `{{ }}` hole names no file. An `<image-slot>` keeps its placeholder as `alt` evidence. The record's `assets` lists `unused` files, `missing` references and `logicRefs`. No stage consumes it yet. |
| `lib/read-readme.mjs` | The README reader (FR-48-7): the handoff `README.md` split into sections by `handoff-schema/README.template.md`'s headings (matched by heading text; numbering, a trailing `(...)` note and `&` against `and` ignored; a heading that begins with a word `data/readme-heading-synonyms.json` accepts for a template heading no heading has by its own words is that heading), each section's text kept, with `judgementCalls`, `openItems`, `businessDetails` and `platformIntent` as lists of the bullets, numbered items and table rows under them (and under the deeper headings below them; "None" is no item; in the judgement calls and open items sections a sub-heading, bold line or `Label:` line naming a decision or something still open sorts the items under it, and a label line carrying text is itself an item), `blockHints` (a heading naming SGS or block mapping) kept as evidence only (FR-48A-8), `flagged` command-like lines (`lib/intake-prose.mjs::commandReason`) kept with their reason and never acted on (FR-48A-10), and `missing` template headings. One top-level `readme` object in the record, not per element. No stage consumes it yet. |
| `data/readme-heading-synonyms.json` | The words a README may use for a template heading (`headings`: whole words a heading begins with, an exact template heading winning and the first template in order taking a tie) and the label words that sort items into judgement calls and open items (`labels`); `lib/read-readme.mjs::loadSynonyms` refuses a key that is no template heading. |
| `lib/fact-record.mjs`, `data/facts.schema.json` | The shared fact record (FR-48-7, schema version 6): the template, logic (holes, `holesByState`, `stateSideEffects`, `statics`), surfaces, animations, assets (with `logicRefs`) and README readers joined into `sites/<client>/build/intake/facts.json`, one entry per template key with its instance keys per state (from the saved inventories), fingerprint and a `sources` header, plus the record's `gaps` (the README reader's, components with no live instance and failed state reads among them). The record holds nothing taken from the clock: two intakes over an unchanged folder write identical bytes. `loadFacts` refuses a stale or invalid record. |
| `lib/intake-facts-run.mjs`, `lib/intake-facts-worker.mjs` | `intake.mjs --facts <client>`: writes only `facts.json` from the export, the replay file and the saved inventories, through a secret-free worker. |
| `lib/approved-identity.mjs` | Which proposed row a Bean approval still names: an approval carrying a fingerprint is checked against the element its key names now, realigned to the one element it agrees with, or refused; the result carries every copy of that template element, and is `exact` only when a fingerprint was compared. |
| `lib/approved-check.mjs` | One approval measured against the rows the rules propose now: the approved block or action family and link rulings, checked against every copy of the template element, each copy that disagrees listed with its own key. |
| `lib/trial.mjs` | Try before write, the judgement: style uids and their mapping, rule differences, the render self-check, blocks that can never be trialled (usesContext, per-request classes), a write applied to attributes, and the verdict (toward the draft, no pair further away). |
| `lib/trial-page.mjs` | Try before write, the page side: the admin session and the block renderer, the live rules of a uid, CSS parsed by the page's engine, a trial applied and undone exactly, and pair measurement and scope. |
| `lib/trial-run.mjs` | Try before write, one round: logs in to the surface's site once, then per round re-reads the post's saved attributes, reloads the live page and tries each candidate write (render twice, self-check, CSS difference applied, pairs measured at every width, undone). Shared by `solve.mjs` (between a write round and the rebuild) and the replay `trial.mjs`. |
| `lib/issue-classes.mjs` | The one definition of a distinct open issue: the visual row kinds, the Solve classes and their order, the unmapped-state class and the issue key. Imported by both `lib/sweep.mjs` and `lib/triage.mjs`, so a surface's triage count equals its sweep count by construction rather than by two copies agreeing. |
| `lib/sweep.mjs` | Sweep library: the newest report per surface, issue rows per report (`wholePage`'s definition) and the site aggregate with shared rows counted once. |
| `lib/register-sweep.mjs` | Register sweep library: register tables as items, the eight A4 groups, section to surface mapping, verdict checking against the sweep and the pairings, the Sweep column writer and the per-group bundle. |
| `lib/triage.mjs` | Triage library: issues by `wholePage`'s key (the Solve classes, then the unmapped-state rows of `other`, in the sweep's order so the counts agree), the mechanical checks (fitting attributes including NULL css_property rows and discovered enums, roster extensions, enclosing calibration, consequence, transient, used value), the read-only resolver pass and the verdict order. |
| `lib/triage-source.mjs` | Triage's source pass (string search): a block's `render.php` mentions, its `style.css` rules for the element, and the PHP helpers its `render.php` reaches two hops deep (`sgs_*` functions, classes, required `includes/` files) that read the row's setting or emit the property, cited as `file::symbol`. |
| `lib/guard.mjs` | The regression guard: reverts a write calibration names, else tries one suspect at a time and lets the next walk decide. |
| `lib/ledger.mjs` | Ledger library: rules, matching, stale entries, accept migration, entries from report rows. |
| `lib/answer-sheet.mjs` | Answer-sheet library: row match keys (no pair name), walk and triage outcomes, wrong-write groups, per-surface scores and the baseline ratchet. |
