- `listIdentity(key, byKey, claimed)` → FR-48-10 item 1: the draft element a list block stands for, when its identity is one item of a run; `identityAdd({ add, hand, covered, wordRefusal })` → FR-48-10 item 2: whether pairing adds an identity pair for a block no generated or hand pair measures; a word-based refusal never removes it.

### `lib/fact-key.mjs`
- `parseInstanceKey(key)` → `{ chain, tpl, copy }` or null; `instanceKey({ chain, tpl, copy })` → the key text; `withCopy(key, copy)`; `copylessKey(key)` → the key with every copy index removed.
- `parseTemplateKey(key)` → `{ dc, tpl }` or null; `templateKey({ dc, tpl })`; `templateKeyOf(instanceKey, rootComponent?)` → the template key of an instance key (the root host takes `rootComponent`).
- `renameHost(templateKey, from, to)`, `renameRootHost(instanceKey, from, to)` → the key with its root host renamed (the served and intake key spaces).
- `templateKeyOfSelector`: The template key a selector string names: its last `data-dc-tpl="<n>"` in the root component's key space (a Site Info row node's draftRef is resolved inside the root's own template), or null when it names none or no root component is given.

### `lib/freshness.mjs`
- `sourcesOf(handoffRoot, { commit? }?)` → `{ files: { <path>: sha256 }, route: { commit } }`; `isSourceFile(path)` (a draft, any `*.js` at any depth, `README.md`, `surfaces.json`, `animations.json`, a file under `assets/` or `tokens/*.css`), `routeCommit()`.
- `changedSources(recorded, handoffRoot, current?)` → `[ 'support.js changed', 'x.dc.html added', 'y.dc.html removed' ]`; `current` is a `sourcesOf` result already taken, so a loop over many records hashes the folder once.
- `assertCurrent(record, handoffRoot, { label?, warn?, current? }?)` → `{ checked }`; throws `StaleRecordError` (`.changed` lists each file) when the record's `sources` disagree with the folder; a record with no `sources` warns once through `warn` and passes.

### `lib/read-template.mjs`
- `readTemplate({ sources, page? })` → `{ elements: { <template key>: record }, order, gaps }` from the census sources (`draftSources`); a record is `{ key, component, n, tag, realTag, attrs, style: { static, holes }, pseudo: { <pseudo>: { static, holes } }, holes: [ { id, where, expr, path, whole } ], parent, realParent, parentChain, membership: { scIf, scFor, dcImport }, own, ref, hooks, events, words, srcLine, fingerprint }`; `page` expands declarations to longhands (without it they stay as written, flagged `unexpanded`).
- `splitDecls(css)` → `[ [ name, value ] ]` split outside parentheses, quotes and `{{ }}`; `pathOf(expr)` → the dotted path of a hole or null.

### `lib/read-logic.mjs`
- `readLogic({ sandbox, entry, sources, template, widths?, surfaces? })` → `{ holes: { <template key>: { <hole id>: { <width>: { v: [ summary ], n, loop, err? } } } }, conditions, mounted, state, statics, staticsTruncated, holesByState, stateSideEffects, gaps }` (`surfaces`: the surfaces reader's `surfaces`, whose states' non-width keys are applied in turn); `planHoles(template)` → the holes to ask each component for; `askHoles(page, comps)` → a plan answered in the page's current state; `resizeTo(page, width)`; `READ_WIDTHS`.

### `lib/read-logic-statics.mjs`
- `readStatics({ page, components, used? })` → `{ statics: { <component>: { entries: [ { name, source: static | getter | field, kind: array | object, length, value, dropped?, truncated?, err? } ], functions, width, dropped?: [ { name, source, reason: 'volatile' } ], truncated? } }, missing, nodes, truncated }`; `CAPS` → the depth, item, key, node, text, per-component and per-record node caps.
- `stateJobsOf(surfaces)` → `{ jobs: [ { id: '<surface id>:<state name>', surface, state, component, keys } ], skipped: [ { id, reason } ] }` (no job for the resting state or a state with only width keys such as `>= 1260`; a state with a width key and a real key is a job with the real keys; a surface with no `dc`, a `props.x` variant, a selector key or a placeholder value such as `<field>` is listed in `skipped` with its reason).
- `readStateValues({ page, plan, jobs, widths, ask, resize })` → `{ <job id>: { component, surface, state, reached, widths: { <width>: { <hole key>: answer } }, errors } }` (a read that throws is caught, the state put back, the error listed); `holesByState({ elements, rest, states, widths, surfaces? })` → `{ holesByState: { <template key>: { <job id>: { <hole id>: { <width>: answer } } } }, stateSideEffects: { <job id>: [ template key ] } }`, only values that differ from resting (`v`, `n` or `loop`); an element outside the state's surface is listed in `stateSideEffects`. `valueGaps({ jobs, skipped, states, noInstance, truncated })` → the gap rows of the state and statics reads.
- `assetPathsIn(value)` → the set of paths under `assets/` the strings in a value name; `logicAssetPaths({ holes, holesByState, statics })` → `[ { path, by, key?, hole? } ]` for `readAssets({ logicPaths })` (a hole's path carries its element's `key` and the `hole` id).

### `lib/read-logic-conditions.mjs`
- `widthConditions({ source, scIfs })` → `{ conditions: { <sc-if key>: { value, width, via? } }, gaps, state: { keys, widthKeys } | null }` (`gaps` include the width reader's: a width basis it cannot determine; `state`: the logic's class-state keys and those derived from the width); `negate(condition)` (`{ bp, is, when }`: `when` is the width condition that gives the token and is never negated, `is: false` means the token does not hold; a partial `all`/`any` negates to `{ not, partial: true }`); `stateKeysOf(idx)` → the class-state keys.

### `lib/read-surfaces.mjs`
- `readSurfaces({ records, template })` → `{ surfaces: { <id>: { id, kind, name, dc, parent, roots, parts, states: [ { name, state_keys, sc_if_tpl_ids, animation_ids } ], closed_by, time_dependent: { value, reason } | null, unresolved } }, pages: [ { surface, route, blocks } ], byElement: { <template key>: [ { id, kind, parent, root, states: [ { name, state_keys, in_sc_if: true, animation_ids } ], otherStates: [ ...in_sc_if: false ], closed_by, time_dependent, unresolved } ] }, gaps }`. A state with no `sc_if_tpl_ids` (a hover, a scroll) is in every member's `otherStates`; an absent `animation_ids` is `[]`, an absent `closed_by`, `time_dependent` or `unresolved` is `null`. `joinFacts` stores `pages` at the record's top level.

### `lib/read-animations.mjs`
- `readAnimations({ records, template })` → `{ byElement: { <template key>: [ { id, name, mechanism, group, status, flagged, reach: 'self' | 'descendant', target, via: 'template_tpl_id' | 'nearest_tpl_id', trigger: { type, detail, state_keys? }, breakpoints, routes, reduced_motion: { behaviour, detail } } ] }, unjoined: [ { id, reason } ], records, gaps }`; an element holds a record once, `self` taking the place of `descendant`. `joinFacts` stores `byElement[key]` as the element's `effects`, the record's `animations: { records, unjoined }` and `counts.effects` (`records`, `joined`, `unjoined`, `elements`). A `records` that is not a list or a record that is not an object is a gap row; an empty-string id falls through to `nearest_tpl_id`; a missing `status` is `active`.

### `lib/read-assets.mjs`
- `readAssets({ folder, template })` → `{ files: { <path>: { path, kind, bytes, width, height, ratio, sha256, animated, groups } }, byElement: { <template key>: [ { path, ratio, kind, sha256, animated, via: 'attr:<name>' | 'style:<property>' | 'script:<file>' } ] }, alt: { <template key>: { source: 'image-slot', placeholder, hole, id } }, assets: { files, unused, missing: [ { path, by, via? } ], logicRefs: [ { path, by } ] }, gaps }` (`logicPaths`: `logicAssetPaths`' result; `via` also `'logic:<hole id>'`); `svgFacts(text)`, `rasterSize(buffer, kind)`, `fileFacts(folder, rel)`, `refPath(value)` (the handoff-relative path a value names, or null), `scriptMaps(folder, files)` and `scriptAttrs(text)` (the attributes a project script reads) are exported. `joinFacts` stores `byElement[key]` as the element's `assets`, `alt[key]` as its `alt`, the record's `assets` block and `counts.assets` (`files`, `elements`, `unused`, `missing`); `lib/freshness.mjs::isSourceFile` covers every file under `assets/` and every `*.js` file.

### `lib/read-readme.mjs`
- `readReadme({ folder })` → `{ present, title, sections: [ { heading, level, line, template, text, none, flagged } ], judgementCalls, openItems, businessDetails, platformIntent: [ { text, line, section, flagged? } ], blockHints: [ { text, line, section } ], flagged: [ { line, text, why } ], missing: [ template heading ], gaps }`; `TEMPLATE_HEADINGS`, `normaliseHeading(text)`, `templateHeadingOf(text)` (by the heading's own words), `templateHeadingsOf(texts)` (with `data/readme-heading-synonyms.json`), `labelKind(text)` (`openItems`, `judgementCalls` or null), `loadSynonyms(file?)` (refuses a key that is no template heading) and `splitSections(markdown)` are exported. `joinFacts` stores the result as the record's top-level `readme` (absent when the caller passes none); `lib/freshness.mjs::isSourceFile` covers `README.md`.

### `lib/fact-record.mjs`
- `buildFactRecord({ folder, client, entry, harPath, sources, widths?, inventoriesDir?, sandbox?, hashed? })` → `{ record, blocked }` (the record's `sources` are `hashed`, or hashed before anything is read, and its `state` holds each component's state keys and width keys); `joinFacts(...)` → the record from the readers' results (no browser; `logic.statics`, `logic.stateSideEffects`, `logic.truncated`, `assets.logicRefs` and the README reader's `gaps` included; no timestamp); `instancesOf({ inventoriesDir, folder, current? })` → the instance keys by template key (a stale inventory skipped and listed); `countsOf(elements, widths, gaps)`.
- `writeFacts(file, record)` → the counts, after validating against `FACTS_SCHEMA` (an invalid record is never written); `factsProblems(record)`; `factsFile(repo, client)`; `loadFacts(client, { repo?, folder?, warn?, current? })` → the record, refused when missing, invalid or stale (`StaleRecordError`); `warnMissingFacts(client, { repo?, folder?, warn? })` → true when the handoff folder exists and the record does not, warning once per client by name (false and silent for a client with no handoff folder); `FACTS_SCHEMA`, `FACTS_SCHEMA_VERSION`.

### `lib/intake-facts-run.mjs`
- `runFacts(options, { log?, widths? })` → the exit code of `intake.mjs --facts <client>` (manifest and replay re-check, then the worker `lib/intake-facts-worker.mjs`, which has no exports).

### `lib/approved-identity.mjs`
- `resolveApproval({ rowId, fingerprint? }, { rows, fingerprints })` → `{ row, copies, how: 'exact' | 'realigned' | 'unchecked', to? }`, `{ refused: why }` or `{ row: null }`. `copies` is every row of the same template element as `row` (each copy of a loop's item). `how` is `exact` only when the approval carries a fingerprint and the named element has a current one that agrees; with either missing, or a row id that is not a skeleton row, it is `unchecked`.

### `lib/approved-check.mjs`
- `wanted(approvedBlock)` → `{ kind: 'removed' }`, `{ kind: 'setting' }` or `{ kind: 'block', block, notLinked, source }`: what an approved outcome asks for.
- `aliasAbsorbed({ rows, fingerprints }, absorbed)` → adds, in place, the rows of a surface another surface absorbed (`rerunSkeletons`' `absorbed`: `{ <slug>: { into, root } }`, the rows whose `strip` is `root`) under the absorbed surface's row ids and fingerprints, which is what an approval names (`skeleton:<slug>:<key>`); `check-approved.mjs` calls it, so an approval of the top bar's rows finds them in the header that holds them now.
- `checkApproval(approval, { rows, fingerprints })` → `{ refused }`, `{ noRow }`, `{ settled }` (a client-built post settles it), `{ setting }`, `{ match }` (every copy agrees) or `{ mismatches: [ line ] }` (one line per copy that disagrees); each carries `how` and `to` from `resolveApproval`.

### `origin.mjs`
- (no exports) CLI only.

### `lib/trial.mjs` (reads block sources)
- `UID_RE`, `uidsIn(classes)`: a block's style uid classes. `mapUids(text, from, to)` → text with each `from` uid rewritten to the `to` uid of the same prefix.
- `ruleDiff(before, after)` → `{ added, removed }` rule texts. `selfCheck({ liveUids, renderUids, liveRules, renderRules })` → `{ ok, why }`.
- `untriable(block, blocksDir)` → why a block can never be trialled (usesContext; `wp_unique_id`/`microtime` in render.php), else null.
- `withWrite(attributes, write)` → the attributes with one write applied. `pairDistance(live, draft, blockX)` → width, height and in-block offset distance.
- `judgeTrial({ before, after, draft, blockX }, tol?)` → `{ verdict, keep, delta, worse }`: `keep` when the summed distance falls and no pair moves more than `tol` px away, `reject` when one does or the sum rises, `no-box-change` when no box moved (the rebuild judges it).
- `savedBlock(raw, ref)` → `{ name, attributes }` of the block whose className carries `ref` in a post's raw content (balanced-brace scan): the attributes the uid hashes.
- `candidatesOf(writes, round)` → the last write of each setting on each block in the round (its `after` holds the setting's whole value).
- `applyVerdicts({ tree, start, writes, gaps, blocked, results })` → `{ writes, gaps, rejected }`: each `reject` puts its setting back to its value in `start` (the tree before the round's first write; removed where start lacks it), drops its writes and blocks every group that wrote it as `trial-reject`; every other verdict leaves the write to the rebuild and the guard. Mutates tree, gaps and blocked.
- `anchorTops({ before, after, draft, anchors })` → the top offset anchor per width for `judgeTrial`, for the widths where the anchor pair was measured before, after and on the draft.

### `lib/trial-run.mjs` (Playwright; reads secrets and block sources)
- `draftBoxes(runDir, widths)` → `{ [width]: { [pair]: { box } } }` from a Solve run's `draft-cache-<width>.json`, first state.
- `isPhoneWidth(walkerCfg, w)`, `viewportOf(w)`: the walker's device profile (a full-mode config runs a width below 500px as an iPhone 13) and viewport (812px high below 500, 900 above).
- `openTrial({ envFile, envKey, walkerCfg, target, log? })` → `{ round(cands, draft, widths) → [ { ref, block, attr, group, ms, verdict, delta?, sumTol?, worse?, why?, restored?, pairs? } ], close() }`. `target` is the surface's build target: a post by id, or the active theme's template or template part by slug. Logs in once; each round re-reads the saved content (A = its saved attributes, which hold the last build, so the round's own writes are undone by construction) and reloads the live page once per device profile it needs. `pairs` holds each pair's distance to the draft before and after and its three boxes.

### `lib/trial-page.mjs` (Playwright pages)
- `readEnv(file)`, `adminSession(ctx, env, key)` → `{ base, api, close }`; `renderBlock(session, block, attributes)` → `{ status, html }`; `styleOf(html)`, `rootClassOf(html)`.
- In-page: `rulesInPage(token)`, `parseInPage(text)`, `applyTrialInPage([ css, remove ])`, `undoTrialInPage()`, `measureInPage([ resolveSrc, finders, blockSel ])`, `scopeInPage([ resolveSrc, finders, blockSel ])`. `cssOf(rule)` → CSS text with its at-rules.
- `anchorInPage([ resolveSrc, finders, blockSel ])` → in-page: the pair name a write on the block cannot move, to measure top offsets from (the last pair before the block that neither contains it nor sits inside it).

### `trial.mjs`
- (no exports) CLI only.

### `lib/pairs.mjs`
- `PAIRING_LIMITS`: a pairing is left out under 80% of the block's words matched, with a word from outside the block, with a box outside half to double the block's, or when it holds no padding where the block does and their content boxes match within `boxTolerance` (2px) (the padding sits on a draft ancestor).
- `paddedPartner(chain, liveBox, limits?)` → the partner from the draft chain (smallest element holding the twins, then its ancestors, nearest first): the first element, or, when its padding sits elsewhere, the nearest ancestor wrapping it with padding of its own.
- `wordsByBlock(liveRefs)` → Map ref → live word indices (every word inside the block, nested blocks included).
- `twinsByBlock(matches, blocks)` → Map ref → `{ live, draft }` (the block's words and their draft twins).
- `judgePairing(block, partner, liveRefsOfDraft, limits?)` → `{ ok, why }`.
- `twinPlan(draftIdx, words)` → `{ sure, repeated }`: element indices of the block's words whose text occurs once on the draft, and, per repeated text, every candidate element (draftChains places it nearest the sure words).
- `commonPath(paths)` → the deepest element path the given draft paths share, or null (a block of repeated words only, with no parent partner, anchors on its child blocks' partners' common ancestor).
- `wordMatch(texts)` → a regex source (flags `iu`) matching a text node holding any of the block's words as whole words; a text run takes only those nodes.
- `choosePartner(chain, block, liveRefsOfDraft, limits?)` → `{ partner, verdict }`: the element partner when it passes; else, when the draft element has the block's text but not its box, or shares its element with another block's words while the block's words are its own text, a text-run partner (`textRun: { direct }`) judged on both sides' text extents.
- `chooseControlPartner(chain, liveBox, limits?)` → `{ partner, verdict }` for a form-control block (no painted words): of the draft control's chain (the control, then each ancestor holding no other control), the element nearest the block's box within the size limits.
- `chooseGroupPartner(childPaths, groupBox, liveBox, limits?)` → `{ partner, verdict }` for a block whose draft element holds other blocks' words but whose children are paired: the group of its children's draft partners (`group: { paths }`), compared by union box only (the walker's `{ group }` finder, `paint.mjs::groupBox`).
- `reconcileHandPairs(hand, kept)` → `{ retarget, duplicate }`: a hand pair measuring a kept block's draft element on an element inside that block moves to the block root (`retarget`), and the generated pair it then duplicates is dropped (`duplicate`).
- `configText(handFile, surface, pairs, retarget?)` → the generated walker config's source (hand pairs named in `retarget` measure the given live finder).
- `liftExclusions(exclude, holdsSurface)` → the selector exclusions that hold the surface (a header, footer or drawer surface pairs its own words).
- `pairingState(cfg, name)` → the hand config's walker state that opens a panel surface on both sides (`pairs.mjs --state`), null without a name; throws when the state is missing or lacks a draft or live action.
- `rootFor(cfg, side, state?)` → the root the pairing collectors walk on one side: the hand config's `pairRoot[<state>][side]` for a panel that leaves the landmark it opens from (the live phone drawer, which `store.js::reparentToBody` appends to `<body>`), else `auto.root`.
- `partName(slot)` → a part pair's name suffix: the slot's last BEM element (`__burger`), with an `:nth-of-type` kept as `-<n>`.
## `confirm-canvas.mjs`

Live confirmation of the `canvas-settable` claims, read-only: it writes no tree, setting, page or stylesheet.

```
node scripts/computed-route/confirm-canvas.mjs [--candidates --client <slug>] <triage dir | families.json> <out.json> [base url] [only indices]
```

After a sweep, run it in candidates mode and write the result to `sites/<client>/build/qa/canvas-confirm.json`: it measures every family `canvasSettable` could ever cite, so a refuted citation's fallback is already measured and never fails open (Eye Care 2026-10-07: 1,795 families, 13,715 claims, 0 skipped stylesheets, one pass).

A claim classes a row W because another block declares a setting whose `css_property` covers the row's property and the reachability gate judged its emission to reach the row's element. Confirming that needs a value-independent test, because computed styles and CDP matched-styles cannot separate "the cited setting loses" from "the cited setting is unset". So this enumerates every rule in every loaded stylesheet that declares the property and whose selector matches the row element, and asks whether any of them is the cited block's. The walk recurses into `@media` blocks without checking whether they apply, so the test is generous to the claim and a refutation is conservative. Verdicts: `CONFIRMED`, `VAR-CHANNEL` (a matching rule reads a custom property the framework sets on the cited block), `REFUTED` (rules govern the row but none is the cited block's), `NO-RULE` (nothing declares the property for this element at all) and `ABSENT` (the row is not in the DOM on that page).

- `familiesFrom(triageDir)` → the claims grouped into families of (cited block, cited setting, row property), read from the triage reports so there is no separate hand step to go stale. A family is the unit the claim is made in: 168 rows collapse to 63 families, and surface is the wrong axis because a family spans surfaces. A resolver-hop citation has no `where` key and a canvas-roster citation has `ancestor` or `sibling`; the two are the same claim reached from two directions and are reported apart. `property` is the CSS property the row stands for (`lib/solve-rows.mjs::cssProp`: `painted-ground` is `background-color`), the key `measuredReachFrom` is looked up with and the property the stylesheet walk reads.
- `candidatesFrom({ client, triageDir, manifest, contextFor? })` → every family `canvasSettable` would consider for any issue of any `canvas: true` surface, found by calling it with a `ctx.measuredReach` that records each (block, setting, property, cited ref) and answers false, unioned with `familiesFrom`. Asking about every issue, not only today's canvas-settable rows, keeps the set independent of the confirm file it replaces. `contextFor(surface, recorder)` defaults to `triage.mjs::surfaceContext`.

Findings: `.claude/reports/2026-10-06-session-c2/CANVAS-SETTABLE-CONFIRMATION.md`.

- `collectContext({side, width, state?, root?, message})` → the message `pairs.mjs::runPairing` throws when a pairing collector fails: a failure raised inside the page carries no side, width or state, so a missing pair root read as a bare `page.evaluate` error four frames from its cause. A pair-root failure also names the root and that a state opener which did not fire leaves it absent; any other failure keeps its own text and only gains the side, width and state.
- `RECHECK_WIDTHS`: the widths a kept finder is re-checked at besides the pairing width (375, 768, 1920).
- `joinFinders(perWidth)` → one CSS selector list from a block's distinct per-width draft paths, in width order.
- `mergeWidthFinders(perWidth, resolvesAt, widths?)` → `{ ok, draft, drafts, why }`: one joined finder for a block whose draft partner differs per width, kept only when the joined selector resolves at every width to that width's own element, so it can never measure another element.
- `assertReachable({ url, blocks, words?, requires?, requiresFound?, notice? })`: throws on an empty live URL, no blocks, a missing `live.requires` element, or a failed or cancelled order notice, so an unreachable page fails loudly instead of writing an empty report.
- `chooseMediaPartner(live, draft, limits?)` → `{ partner, verdict }` for a block with no painted words but a media element: the draft media at the same place among its paired ancestor's media, left out when the two sides' counts differ.
- `pairingStates(cfg, names)` → the hand config's walker states for a multi-state pairing run (`pairs.mjs --state a,b,c`), `rest` meaning the page at rest.

### `lib/guard.mjs`
- `explains(w, r, cal)` → true when calibration ties write `w` to regressed row `r` (its own property, a calibrated side effect, or a discovered layout effect).
- `anchorRef(report, r)` → the ref of the pair a distance row (`y-from-<pair>`, `x-from-`, `right-from-`) is measured from, from the walk's live trace, or null. When the row's own node holds no write, the guard's suspects are the writes inside that node and inside the anchor pair.
- `ownSizeRow(r)` → whether a row is a node's own height or width box row (no path), which sums everything inside the node and so cannot judge one write.
- `landed(s, report)` → true when every row a setting was written from (matched by the write's group key) is closed in the report: the direct verdict on that setting.
- `settingsOf(writes)` → the writes grouped by node and attribute (writes to one attribute chain, so they are undone together).
- `suspectOrder(settings, calFor)` → layout-mode settings first, then settings writing a layout property, then the latest.
- `guardRound(base, report, tree, lastWrites, blocked, calFor, trials)` → the writes whose state changed (reverted, under trial, restored); settles last round's trials against the new walk first.
- `closeTrials(trials, blocked)` → settings still under trial when the run ends, reported as unconfirmed reverts.

### `solve.mjs` (runs `wp-build-page.js` and the walker)
- `WIDTH_GROUPS`: one walker per width, run in parallel (each with its own draft cache).
- `mergeReports(parts)` → one report from the per-width walks (runs in width order, errors by width).
- `WALK_FLAGS`: headless unless `SGS_HEADED=1` (Hostinger's edge 403s headless browsers after bursts of traffic; `pairs.mjs`, `calibrate.mjs` and `scripts/wp-build-page.js` honour the same switch, scrollbars hidden); every round's walk is lean (`--lean`: only the styles, boxes, hover end states and structure Solve reads) and reuses the run's draft reads (`--draft-cache <run dir>/draft-cache.json`).
- `USED_VALUES`: computed properties that are used sizes (`width`), written only as the draft declares them (a plain length or percentage at every width, from the walker's `declared` read), otherwise a `used-value` gap naming what the draft declares.
- `calibrationFor(block, site, dir?)` → the block's slot map with `site`'s default paint (through `withInnerRootAliases`) or null; `calibrationForSite(site, dir?)` → the `calFor(block)` function for one site.
- `withInnerRootAliases(cal, block)` → the calibration with every slot, reach and element path also answering to its form without a leading `.sgs-<slug>` step, when the cache holds that inner root as an element (a block calibrated with its optional wrapper on, measured on a page with it off); otherwise `cal` unchanged.
- `blockRenderSource(name)` → a block's `render.php` source, or null.
- `outsideOwner(node, { refs, source })` → who owns the content of a block whose words, elements or links come from outside the layout tree (`content-page`, `site-info`, `woocommerce-text`, `product-data`), read from the block's own source, or null. Evidence about the block, never a verdict on a row.
- `writeRound(report, tree, { db, snapshot, round, log, blocked, stateMap, calFor?, canvas?, ancestorHop?, ownerOf? })` → `{ writes, gaps, held }` (`held[groupKey]` lists a setting that already holds the draft value; `heldGroups(report, tree, opts)` reads it on a copy with its own snap log); only rows from mapped walker states are written. It hops to an enclosing ancestor last, writes content rows through calibration's `text`, `presence` and `link` entries, and reports `state-conflict` and `handover` gaps.
- `solveLoop({ maxRounds, build, walk, guard, write, save, blocked?, log? })` → `{ report, writes, gaps, rounds, lastWrote }`: the build, walk and write rounds with every step passed in; `maxRounds` 0 is measure-only (one build, one walk, never a write).
- `revertRegressions(prev, report, tree, lastWrites, blocked, calFor?, trials?)` → the writes the guard undid this round (`lib/guard.mjs::guardRound`).
- `wrongWrites(writes, reportAfter, stateMap)` → writes a later round reverted or that moved their rows further from the draft.
- `unpaintedBorder(report, g)` → a `not-painted` gap for a border colour row on a side the draft gives no border at any measured width (its value is the text colour carried by currentColor, so a written colour would paint nothing), else null.
- `settingRatio(wrong, writes)` → Spec 48 §3.3's ratio: wrong settings (one attribute on one node) over every setting written.
- `siteOverride(s, site, targets, readUrl)` → `{ surface, sshArgs, liveOrigin }` for `--site <calibration target>`: the surface built with that target's credentials (a mirror is a database copy, so the post ids and templates are the surface's own), `sshArgs` null for a local mirror (no remote host check), and the target's origin, which `walk` passes to the walker as `SGS_LIVE_ORIGIN` (`scripts/parity/lib/helpers.mjs::retargetLive` moves every live URL of the config to it). No site returns the surface unchanged.
- `envUrl(file, key)` → `WP_URL_<key>` from a secrets env file, or null.

### `fill.mjs` (runs Chromium, serves a local folder)
- `calibrationLoader(site, dir?)` → the block-to-calibration function for `site`: `solve.mjs::calibrationFor` over the route's cache, or over the cache folder `dir`.
- `parseMirrors(argv)` → `{ url: absolute file }` from repeated `--mirror <url> <file>`; throws on a lone url.
- `fillSurface(options)` → the report object (and writes `--out`): lints the skeleton, serves or opens the draft, reads it, resolves root-down, samples entrances and the sweep, and writes every output.

### `lib/draft.mjs`
- `contentType(file)` → the Content-Type for a file name.
- `resolveRequest(root, urlPath, index)` → `{ file }`, `{ redirect }` or `{ status }`; never a path outside `root`.
- `serveDraft(folder, { index, host })` → `{ url, port, close }` on 127.0.0.1 at an ephemeral port.
- `draftPageProblem(folder, index)` → why the folder serves no page at its root (no `index.html`, or the named `index` is absent), naming its `.dc.html` pages for `--draft-index`; null when it does. `assertDraftPage(folder, index)` → the folder, or throws that reason: Fill and `skeleton.mjs inventory` refuse such a folder, and `fill.mjs --serve` warns, because a reader opening the root matches nothing.

### `lib/fill-skeleton.mjs`
- `SCOPE_ATTR`: the attribute a node's draft element carries while its slots resolve. `scopeSelector(index)` → the selector for it.
- `finderProblem(f)` → why a value is not a walker finder, or null. `finderKind(f)` → `selector`, `text`, `textRun`, `group`, `js` or `tpl`, else null.
- `parseTplKey(key)` → `{ chain, tpl, copy }` for a `tpl` finder's `<import chain>/<n>#<copy>`, else null; `tplKey(parts)` is its inverse. The chain is `Root` then `<template>@<host stamp>#<host copy>` per nested import, so the same number in two imports, or in two copies of one import, never collides.
- `originMap(nodes, refs)` → `{ [cr-ref]: { tpl, fingerprint, slots? } }` for every node whose `draftRef` is a `tpl` finder; `fill.mjs` writes it to `<build>/<surface>.origin.json`.
- `scopeFinder(f, scope)` → the finder rewritten to resolve inside the scoped element.
- `skeletonNodes(tree)` → depth-first nodes with parent, children and targets.
- `skeletonProblems(tree)` → readable problems in a skeleton's draft keys.
- `cleanTree(tree)` → a copy without `draftRef`, `draftSlots`, `draftFingerprint`, `handover`, `siteInfoRow`, `childAttributes` and `childRefs`.
- `ROW_KEYS` → the Fill-only keys of a `sgs/social-icons` Site Info row. `siteInfoRow` is a list of registry brand slugs (absent: every registry brand in order); `childAttributes` is merged into every generated `sgs/icon`; `childRefs` maps a slug to the selector of the draft element it copies, resolved inside the row's own `draftRef`.
- `expandSiteInfoRows(tree)` → a copy where each such node holds `sgs/icon` innerBlocks (`iconSource: "brand"`, `brandName`, and `metadata.bindings.linkUrl` to Site Info's registry key); `fill.mjs` runs it after `skeletonProblems` and the lint.
- `hasRow(node)` → true for a `sgs/social-icons` node carrying any `ROW_KEYS` key.
- `ROW_GLYPH_SLOT`: a generated icon's glyph element path (`.sgs-icon__shape > .sgs-icon__svg`), read against the row's glyph element (`lib/fill-row-lift.mjs`).

### `lib/fill-fixture.mjs`
- `toggleStem(name, def)` → the stem an off-by-default boolean switch gates (`showBadge` → `badge`, `childIconShowBackground` → `childIconBackground`), or null.
- `fixturePreconditions(block, fixtures, schema, db)` → `{ attr: value }`: the fixture's attributes (with its first variant) that differ from the block's default and style it (a css_property, or a switch gating one).
- `taintedProps(block, preconds, schema, db, cal)` → `Map(LOOSE(slot) → Set(prop))`: the properties each calibrated slot shows only because of a precondition; a border width brings its style.
- `unstyledBaseline(st, block, cal, schema, slot, prop)` → the unstyled value (`0px` width, `none` style, transparent background, `none` image or shadow) Fill compares with for a tainted property, else undefined.
- `gateFor(st, block, cal, schema, attr, attributes)` → the fixture-precondition switch a written setting needs and the node does not hold, or null.

### `lib/fill-row-lift.mjs`
- `elementSlots(db, block, cal)` → every calibrated slot the block's settings of each element sit at (`{ element: [slot] }`): the settings of one element need not share a slot.
- `liftRow(st, rowRec, ref, cal, { fillElement, snapsOf })` → lifts the generated icons' shared look onto the row node: each icon's draft link is read as the row's shape element and its svg as its glyph element, at every slot the element's settings sit at, and `childIcon*` is written on the row only when every copy agrees; the rest is read against each icon's own calibration and written on the icon (`how: "row-child"`), or raised UNMAPPED by block. Never averaged.

### `lib/fill-same-box.mjs`
- `calSameBox(cal, a, b, axes)`: whether calibration measured two element paths as the same box on the axes (both by default) at every width.
- `layoutHome({ db, block, cal, prop, state, slot })`: the slot a setting for `prop` is written at: `slot` when a setting paints it, else the slot of a setting painting an element that is the same box, else `slot`.
- `innerToRoot({ rec, target, axis, reads, cal, widths })`: the root target and the per-width extra (the root's padding and border on the axis) when the draft element `target` is the root's content box, or null.

### `lib/fill-band.mjs`
- `bandSetting(db, block, cal)` → `{ attr, slot }`: the block's max-width setting on the DB `content` slot and the non-root slot calibration measured it painting, or null.
- `bandNeed(own, kids, declaredOf)` → one width's need: `{ min, why }` (spans), `{ exact, box, why }` (a centred column), or null (no drawn child).
- `bandValue(needs, current, sizes, format)` → `{ held }`, `{ value }` (a tier object) or `{ conflict }`.
- `capOf(value, sizes)`, `themeSizes(rawSnapshot)`: a band value's cap in px (Infinity for none) and the theme's `{ normal, wide }` sizes.
- `fillBand(st, rec, ref, cal)` → `{ attr }` when the node's band setting was written or held, else null.

### `lib/fill-part-pairing.mjs`
- `partHome(db, block, cal, element, except)` → the slot most of a part's resting settings are calibrated at, or null.
- `unpairedPart({ db, block, cal, attr, readSlot, prop, value })` → the reason a write read from `readSlot` does not belong to the part `attr` paints, or null (the part's own element, or calibration measured both as the same box).
- `exactHome(db, block, cal, element)` → `partHome` matched on the exact calibrated slot only: the part whose own settings calibration measured at that element.
- `laysOutPart({ readSlot, home, targets, readAt })` → whether the draft element read at `readSlot` lays out the children the part at `home` holds: the part sits inside it, no target sits at the part, and the targets directly under the part are its laid-out children (`layoutChildren`, each box inside its box) at every width it is read. `unpairedPart` also takes `targets` and `readAt( id, width )` for it.

### `lib/fill-state-motion.mjs`
- `MOTION_STATE_PROPS`: the state-look properties the motion layer holds (`transform`, and `INDENT_PROPS`: `padding-left`, `padding-inline-start`).
- `splitList(text)` → a comma list split outside parentheses.
- `stateHover(reads, target)` → `{ base, tiers, gaps }`: the widest width's hover record (a transform, and a start-padding change as a `padding-left` property from the resting px to the hover px; a delta of 0 writes none), the tablet and mobile looks that differ from it (a record, or null where the draft has none), and the reasons a width's transform cannot be held (a `var()` or `calc()` transform, a start padding that is not a px length, a base narrower than the widest width).
- `withRestPadding(records, reads, target)` → the records with a start-padding hover that has no resting value (the draft's `style-hover` names only the hover value) given `from` = the element's resting padding at the widest width (declared, else rendered; left as it is when not px), so `lib/motion-layers.mjs::hoverLayer` writes the delta; `lib/fill-motion.mjs::motionWrites` applies it to every element's own records.
- `stateMotionRecords(reads, target)` → the hover record a target's `:hover` transform and start-padding look makes (with the transform's own duration and easing, and `tiers`), or `[]`.
- `withStateHover(own, state)` → a target's hover records with the state read added: the properties no inventory hover record covers join that record (one hover layer per element), or stand as the state records when the element has none; `lib/fill-motion.mjs` calls it.
- `hoverDevices(record, baseLayer, layerOf, perDevice)` → `{ devices, unmapped }`: the hover layer's tablet and mobile overrides for a record with `tiers`; a width with no hover takes the neutral pose and a field held for all devices only (the easing) that differs is returned in `unmapped`.

### `lib/fill-read.mjs` (Playwright by path; imports `scripts/parity/lib/*`)
- `READ_WIDTHS`: 375, 768, 1440. `PRESENT`: the render-presence sweep series. `PROPS`: the properties Fill resolves.
- `loadChromium()` → Playwright, imported from `plugins/sgs-blocks/node_modules`.
- `requestAllowed(url, origin, external)` → whether the draft may load a url.
- `routeDraft(ctx, { origin, external, mirror, aborted? })` → routes a context's requests (mirror, allow, abort), adding each aborted url to `aborted` when given.
- `emptyReadError(width, count, aborted)` → the error `readDraft` throws when a read width resolves none of its targets (Spec 48 R-48-3): the width, the target count, the aborted requests and the `--allow-external` hint for a draft whose runtime loads from unpkg.
- `jobsFor(nodes)` → the collection jobs, roots before slots.
- `readDraft(options)` → `{ origin, widths, declared, sweep, states }`; `states[w]` is each target's state looks (`readStates`) at the read widths when `options.states` (the vocabulary) is given; `fill.mjs` passes `lib/db.mjs::statesVocabulary` and runs `lib/fill-state.mjs::fillStates` after `fillTree`.
- `FLEX_ITEM_PROPS`: the flex sizing properties read on the flex item a root is, never swept. `flexItemsInPage([ jobs, resolveSrc ])` → in-page: for each root job the flex item its element is (the outermost of the element and the wrappers above it that sit in a flex container, a wrapper counting only while it holds just the one below and paints nothing); `applyFlexItems(widths, jobs, items)` → the reads folded into the snapshots.

### `lib/fill-read-collect.mjs`
- `collectMany([ jobs, collectSrc, resolveSrc, paintSrc, props, pseudoProps, ... ])` → in-page: every job's pair snapshot with the walker's `collectPair`, plus the exact border box, whether the element takes part in flow, its own words and link, and a block's own text styles; the walker's collectors arrive as source text.

### `lib/fill-declared.mjs`
- `extraProps(declared, read)` → `{ prop: declared }` the code sets beyond Fill's list (motion timings, CSS-wide keywords and shorthands left out); `readExtras(page, jobs, extras, resolveSrc)` → their rendered values; `missingExtras(result, read, widths)` → the extras declared at some read width but not yet read at another, so readDraft reads them at every width; `isShorthand(prop)` (mdn-data).
- `usedValueDraft(declared)` → the plain declared length a used-value property takes, or null; `usedValueDrafts(draft, declaredAt)` → `{ draft, notPlain }` (the widths whose code states another form, which Fill names); `USED_VALUE` (height, the offsets, row tracks; width and the column tracks keep their own rules), `CSS_WIDE`.
- `unreadDeclared(declared, styles, read)` → the declared properties of the code that Fill reads in some layouts only (a sticky `top`) when the snapshot holds no value for them; `read` is Fill's own list.

### `lib/calibrate-probe.mjs`
- `probeState({ page, cdp, read, rootSel, props, mechanisms, maxUp })` → `{ read, how }` or `{ read: null, why }`; `probeMechanisms(state, rows?)` → the vocabulary rows a probe tries (`PROBE_REACHES`, prefix tokens left out); `applyInPage` / `undoInPage` (in-page); `changed(rest, read, props)`.

### `lib/state-vocab.mjs`
- `stateRows(db?)` → the `states` rows in vocabulary order (read once; throws when the table is missing); `forceable(rows?)` → the pseudo-class names `CSS.forcePseudoState` can force; `pseudoTrigger(pseudo, rows?)` → `press`, `hover`, `focus` or `state`.

### `lib/state-map.mjs`
- `stateMap({ surfaces, surface, intake, vocab, records })` → `{ surface, walkerStates: [{ walkerState, settingState, names }], shared, scIf, gaps }`; each `scIf` row is `{ id, component, record, class, reach }` with `reach` `walker` (`walkerState`, `settingState`), `set-state` (`keys`, `settingState`), `rest` (a state of the sc-if holds the component's initial values), `route`, `width`, `props`, `content` or `gap` (`reason`); `shared` are the setting states two or more walker states map to.
- `diffReasons(map, reasons)` → `{ missing, stale }`: the gap rows `stateless` does not state, and the surface's conflict entries naming a walker state the surface dropped or mapping it to another setting state.
- `namesState(walkerState, state)` → whether a walker state's name holds every state key's words; `vocabStateFor(state, vocab)` → the vocabulary state a draft state's keys stand for (a key word names a state, or a single-word token such as `--done`), or null; `initialValues(inv)` → Map `component.key` → the initial value the inventory states; `conflictKey(key)` → `{ ref, path, kind, prop }` of a conflict entry's key.

### `lib/state-map-io.mjs`
- `loadReasons(file?)` → `state-map-reasons.json` parsed (empty when absent), thrown when `reasonsProblems` finds any; `reasonsProblems(reasons)` → one line per conflict entry naming no `client`; `refusalsFor(reasons, client, surface)` → the conflict entries of that client's surface; `loadClient(client, { surfacesFile, intakeFile })` → `{ surfaces, intake }`; `REASONS_FILE`, `REPO`.

### `lib/walker-states.mjs`
- `walkerStates({ entry, record, facts, rootComponent, vocab, opener, widths })` → `{ states: [{ name, settingState, ways, widths, reach, skipAt }], refused: [{ state, reason }], component }`: every mapped state in order; `widths` null when it holds at every walk width; `reach` `'scroll'`, `'owner'` or null; `skipAt` the widths where the config's own `open` already applied the same record state.
- `stateDraftSource(state, component)` / `stateLiveSource(state, root)` → the config's action texts (the live one null when none is needed); `scrollPage(to)` (in page); `walkRefusalRows({ walk, id, nodes, flat, refOf })` → one UNMAPPED `(state opener)` row per refused state.

### `lib/walker-check.mjs`
- `checkWalkers(surfaces, buildDir)` → `{ faults, unwalked, checked }`: a fault per missing walker file and per mapped state the surface's full config (else its base config) neither walks nor lists under `unwalked`.

### `lib/state-check.mjs`
- `checkStates({ draft, live, liveRest, pairs })` → one row per pair, width, state and property the draft's state changes (`draftValue`, `liveValue`, `liveState`, `verdict` match / differs / unreadable, `tolerance`); `stateSummary(rows, draft)` → counts overall and by state, plus `unreachedDraft`. An element's looks of one state are merged per property (the later look wins) on both sides, and a look on a pseudo-element is its own state (`hover::after`).

### `lib/state-live.mjs`
- `stateGroups(cfg)` → `[{ name (null at rest), state, pairs }]`: the walker states the config's pairs name, each with its pairs (a pair without `states` is read in every state the config opens on both sides, rest included, any finder kind), a job id per side; `statePairs(cfg)` → the distinct pairs followed; `stateSkips(cfg)` → `[{ name, state, why }]`, the pairs not followed (no finder on a side, or a walker state the config cannot open on both sides), listed in the report as not followed; `wantedProps(draftRead, pairs)` → `{ liveJobId: [prop] }`, every property the draft partner's state looks change.
- `readSide(page, jobs, rows, want, log, logicRows)` → `{ states, rendered }`: one page's state looks at its current width, and the rendered resting value of each wanted property.
- `groupWidths(group, widths)` → the read widths a group's state is run at (its `widths` when it has them); `liveStateCheck({ browser, cfg, rows, widths, logicRows, log })` → `{ rows, summary, pairs }` (the `checkStates` rows, each with its pair name and `walkerState`); `surfaceStateCheck({ cfg, vocab, surfaces, surface, buildDir, logicRows, headless, log, run })` → the report part in its own browser, or why nothing was checked; the surface's sc-if rows (`surfaceLogicRows({ surfaces, surface, buildDir, vocab })`: the state map built from `<buildDir>/intake/intake.json`, none when intake has not run) go to `liveStateCheck`, so the draft's logic states are compared too; `run` is the check it runs (default `liveStateCheck`); `stateReport(check)` → `{ json: { summary, pairs, skipped, open }, markdown }`.

### `lib/fill-state.mjs`
- `COVERAGE_WRITES`: the coverage IDs whose register entry names this module as its writer, listed one per line and sorted; `coverageProblems` refuses a declared ID whose entry names another module and an entry naming this module that is not listed.
- `fillStates({ tree, nodes, reads, calFor, db, snapshot })` → `{ writes, unmapped, held, patch, patchEntries }`, writing into the tree in place through `lib/fill-prop.mjs::resolveProperty` with the look's state (a surface's open look with state null); `patch` is the theme-snapshot patch and `patchEntries` lists each key it set.
- `stateDrafts(reads, id)` → Map `state|prop` → `{ state, prop, perWidth, read }` (a combination joined by `+`; `read` is `"code"` when any width's value came from the stylesheet); `stateGap(state, held)` → why a look has no setting state, or null.
- `targetSnap(reads, target)` → the target's resting snapshot at the narrowest read width whose snapshot has a tag (a target hidden at 375 has none there), else the narrowest one; a focus look on an element with no tag and no role at any width feeds no theme ring.
- `SURFACE_BLOCKS` (`sgs/modal`, `sgs/nav-drawer`, `sgs/mega-panel`); `hiddenAtRest(reads, id)` → true when every read width found the target not visible.
- `interactionStates(db)` → the interaction state words of the DB's state vocabulary; `defaultStateLook(cal, state, rest, ctxAt)` → the block's own look in one state, per element, from calibration's state readings against the default read at rest; `stateContext(reads, id)` → the target's own context per read width; `holdDefaults({ node, ref, rec, target, cal, db, snapshot, reads, drafts, held, ... })` → holds the block's own interaction looks the draft does not have, as `{ writes, unmapped, held }`.
- `focusSharesHover` is re-exported from `lib/fill-state-focus.mjs`.
- `ancestorLook` → re-exported from `lib/fill-state-reach.mjs` for callers of the state pass.
- `motionHolds` → re-exported from `lib/fill-state-reach.mjs` for callers of the state pass.

### `lib/fill-state-tokens.mjs`
- `COVERAGE_WRITES`: the coverage IDs whose register entry names this module as its writer, listed one per line and sorted; `coverageProblems` refuses a declared ID whose entry names another module and an entry naming this module that is not listed.
- `tokenRoute({ state, prop, held, block, node })` → `"focus"`, `"press"`, `"link"` or null; `FOCUS_PROPS`, `PRESS_PROPS`, `LINK_STATES`; `pressVariant(block, node)` → the preset variant or null; `pickValue(perWidth)` → the widest read width's value.
- `createPatch()` → `{ patch, entries, conflicts, set(path, value, from) }`; `applyTokenLooks({ looks, sink, from, target, variant, owned })` → the UNMAPPED rows of the mappers' gaps; `ownedBySetting(cal, slot)`; `conflictRow(conflict)`.
- `takesRing(tag, role)` → whether an element of that tag or role takes the site focus ring. `BROWSER_RING`: the browser's own focus ring, which an element shows when the draft declares no focus outline. `ringLook({ drafts, reads, id, snap, focus })` → the focus look such an element shows (the browser's ring with a link's 1px offset, merged under the draft's own focus look). `restShadow(reads, id)` → the element's resting box-shadow; `ringOnlyLook(focus, browser, rest)` → whether a focus look leaves the box-shadow as it rests.

### `lib/fill-state-focus.mjs`
- `FOCUS_RESTS`: the `sgsFocusRests` setting. `focusSharesHover({ drafts, pseudo, prop, snap, attrs })` → the row's reason, or null, when a hover look written to a setting also paints keyboard focus and the draft has no focus look of that property on an element that takes focus. `lib/fill-state.mjs` re-exports it.
- `settleFocusRests({ rows, painted, db })` → `{ writes, unmapped }`: a node whose block carries `sgsFocusRests` and whose draft paints no focus look equal to its hover look gets `sgsFocusRests` true and its rows dropped; otherwise every row stays, its reason naming why.

### `lib/fill-state-script.mjs`
- `SCRIPT_TRIGGERS`: the React handler props that follow the pointer or focus, by the state they enter, with the DOM events the runtime listens for to call them and to end them. `scriptTriggers([ finder, resolveSrc, triggers ])` → in-page: the handlers that reach the target and its copies.
- `fireScript([ finder, resolveSrc, types, handlerProps ])` → in-page: fires events on the target and counts the elements shown outside the nearest element whose handler the state calls. `readScriptLooks({ page, cdp, jobs, resolveSrc, log })` → every script-driven look of every target at the page's current width, in `readStates`' `looks` shape.
- `scriptGapReason(script, state, gap)` → the unmapped reason of a script look no setting holds, named as a script look; `openSlots(cal, prop, slot, state)` → the other elements an open look of a property on a slot can be written to.

### `lib/fill-shop-parts.mjs`
- `SHOP_PARTS_FILE`: the record's file name; `loadShopParts(buildDir)` → the client's shop parts record, or null when it has none (a file that is not the record's shape throws, naming the file).
- `valueParts(record)` → the parts that carry a value for a block's settings, `[ { surface, part, block, writes: [ [ attr, value ] ] } ]`; `shopPartWrites({ record, nodes, flat, refOf, attrExists, writes })` → the writes for one Fill run, a value another writer put on the node that differs from the draft's raising a row.

### `lib/fill-timing.mjs`
- `COVERAGE_WRITES`: the coverage IDs whose register entry names this module as its writer, listed one per line and sorted; `coverageProblems` refuses a declared ID whose entry names another module and an entry naming this module that is not listed.
- `timingFacts(reads, id)` → `{ rest, enter, skipped }`: the resting rule's timings (`rest[prop]`, per width), each state rule's (`enter[state][prop]`) and the lists that name a time per property or compute it (`skipped`). `oneTiming(text)` → the one value a transition list states, or null; `TIMING_PROPS`.
- `timingWrites({ node, ref, rec, target, cal, db, snapshot, reads, held })` → `{ writes, unmapped }`: the resting timing to the setting with no state, a state's timing to the setting tagged with it (`how: "fill-timing"`), a state timing alone to the setting that times both ways when the block has none for the state (`"fill-timing-reverse"`); a different resting timing with no state setting is `timing-enter-differs`. `fillStates` calls it for every target, and `stateDrafts` leaves these properties to it.
- `timingDuplicates(rows)` → `{ unmapped, dropped }`: a resting timing longhand row the generic pass raised beside the state pass's own `timing-*` row for the same node, element and longhand is dropped, so each timing has one row, the state pass's (it names each property group); a state row is never dropped. `lib/fill-motion-settle.mjs::motionSettled` applies it to the surface's whole list.
- `timingCheck({ draft, live, pairs })` → rows `{ direction: "both" | "enter" | "leave", verdict: "match" | "differs" | "unreadable" }`; with only a state timing the live resting timing must equal the enter timing.
- `LIST_PROPS`: the timing longhands a transition list is split into, with their initial values. `transitionEntries(decl)` → the draft's transition per property, each longhand list matched by index and repeated when shorter; `propertyTimings(decl, prop)` → one longhand's timing per property grouped by equal timing; `isPaint(prop)`; `SHADOW`: the paint property the hover layer times on its own (`shadowDuration`, `shadowEasing`); `paintTimings(reads, id)` → the paint timing a target's resting rule gives at each width, per longhand (the shadow does not vote, unless it is the only paint property); `sameTiming(prop, a, b)` → whether a live timing is the draft's (times within 10ms, easings by canonical spelling).

### `lib/fill-state-apply.mjs`
- `applyToken([hostSel, reach, text, call, on, saved])` (in-page) → what to restore, or null; `renderedOf([finder, resolveSrc, props])` (in-page) → `{ prop: value }`.

### `lib/fill-state-code.mjs`
- `codeLooks({ id, blocked, rest, reasonOf })` → `{ looks, unreached }` for the combinations the page cannot enter; `cascadeCode(items)` → `{ prop: value }` or null; `specificity(selector)` → `[ids, classes, types]`.

### `lib/snapshot-tokens.mjs`
- `linkStateTokens(look, state, target)` → `{ key, state, value, gaps }`: `key` is `styles.elements.link['<:state>']` for `:link`, `:any-link` or `:visited`; `isThemeLink(target)` → `{ themed, why }`, the rule that decides whether a draft element is a plain content link the theme styles.
- `focusTokens(look)` → `{ focus, gaps }`: `focus` is null when the look declares no focus property, else the `settings.custom.focus` keys it sets (with `source: "draft"`); `pressTokens(look, variant)` → `{ variant, tokens, gaps }`, `tokens` holding `active-transform` and `active-shadow` only.

### `lib/fill-state-reason.mjs`
- `reasonOf(tokens)` → why the page cannot be put into a state combination: the first token with no host (a sibling combinator or a state inside `:has()`) or that is never exposed to scripts.

### `lib/fill-state-pseudo.mjs`
- `pseudoDeclared(cdp, finder, resolveSrc, pseudo, keepDocument)` → `{ prop: value }` the author rules declare on a pseudo-element (`::before`, `::after`, `::marker`, `::placeholder`), or null; `pseudoRenderedOf([finder, resolveSrc, props, pseudo])` (in-page) → the pseudo-element's computed values.

### `lib/fill-state-read.mjs`
- `readStates({ page, cdp, jobs, rows, rest, resolveSrc, log })` → `{ looks: { targetId: [{ states, family, selector, tokens, declared, rendered, read: "entered" | "code" }] }, unreached }`: every state look whose declared values differ from rest (`declaredDiff(rest, state)`), at the page's current width. `rows` is the state vocabulary (the framework DB's `states` table).
- `declaredDiff(rest, state)` → `{ prop: value }` for each property the state declares differently from rest.

### `lib/fill-state-combos.mjs`
- `stateCombos([jobs, rows, resolveSrc, hostAttr])` (in-page) → each target's state combinations with each token's host; `tokenSource(row)` → a vocabulary row's token as regex source; `readableRows(rows)` → the rows a page can enter (`READABLE` reaches) plus the unreachable ones.

### `lib/fill-shop-targets.mjs`
- `shopPartTargets({ record, nodes, facts, rootComponent, db, calFor })` → `{ added: [{ part, node, slot, finder }], notes }`: adds each located part's element as the target of its part's calibrated slot on the block's nodes, in place. `partElement(db, part)` → `{ element }` or `{ why }`; `instanceOf(facts, templateKey, rootComponent)` → the served-space instance key of the element's first copy, or null.
- `locatedElement(facts, locators)` → the template element every locator shares in its ancestry that is a real element, not an `sc-if` or `sc-for` wrapper: `{ key }` or `{ why }`.

### `lib/fill-logic-state.mjs`
- `COVERAGE_WRITES`: the coverage IDs whose register entry names this module as its writer, listed one per line and sorted; `coverageProblems` refuses a declared ID whose entry names another module and an entry naming this module that is not listed.
- `readLogicStates({ page, cdp, jobs, rest, logicRows, vocab, resolveSrc, log })` → `{ looks: { targetId: [look] }, gaps: [{ ids, reason }] }`: sets each `set-state` row's keys, reads the targets whose declared values change, restores the state. `logicGroups(logicRows, vocab)` → the rows grouped by component and keys, each with its setting state (the row's, else the vocabulary state the keys name) or a gap reason. `readDraft` calls it after `readStates` when given `logicRows` (the state map's `scIf` rows) and puts the looks in `states[width].looks` and the gaps in `states[width].logicGaps`.

### `lib/fill-confirm.mjs`
- `confirmRecords({ writes, nodes, reads, defects, rootComponent })` → one FR-48-36 record per written value and read width (declared, emitted, rendered draft, rendered live left for `confirm-live.mjs`, slot, D–R, tolerance, verdict).
- `compareDR(prop, declared, rendered, fontPx)` → `{ dr, type, tolerance }`; `propertyType`, `TOLERANCE`, `verdictOf(dr, defect)`, `draftIdOf(finder, rootComponent)`, `defectIndex(draftDefects)`.

### `lib/confirm-live.mjs`
- `RENDERED_TOL` (1, the rendered length tolerance in px); `compareRL(prop, renderedDraft, renderedLive, fontPx)` → `{ rl, tolerance }` (lengths ±1px); `liveVerdict(dr, fr34Passes, rl)` → `{ verdict, pending, reason }`; `livePair(report, record, width, rest)`; `restStates(stateMap)`; `fillLive(records, report, {stateMap})`; `writeConfirmLive(outDir, fillReportFile, report, opts)` → `{ file, counts }`.
- `imageLive`: The records with the live half filled: renderedLive, rl, rlTolerance, the verdict from the table and, where the table says so, pending. Records with no width (no target) pass through open. An image record's live half (FR-48-36, the image part): the painted image on the built page is the attachment Fill wrote (lib/fill-media.mjs::paintedImageCheck, which also holds the stated ratio) and carries the alt Fill wrote. media: { map, site }. An unreadable live image leaves the row open; it never confirms.
- `judgeUnmapped(rows, report, { stateMap })` → the `default` judge of Fill's UNMAPPED rows: a row no setting writes is held as `default` only when the built page already paints the draft's value on the row's element at every width the row states, read where the walker paired that element in a rest state (`livePair`) and compared by `lib/css-equivalence/tolerance.mjs::samePaint`; `writeConfirmLive` returns `unmapped: { held, raised }`.

### `lib/gate-chain.mjs`
- `GATES`; `g0Freshness(i)`, `g1PairAndPaint(i)`, `g2Intent(i)`, `g3Db(i)`, `g4Calibration(i)`, `g5Emission(i)`, `g6Probe(i)`, `g7Recheck(i)` → `{ pass, class, evidence }`; `PROBE_CLASS` (probe kind to F-class); `runChain(row, ctx)` with `ctx.inputs[gate](row, soFar)` building each gate's input.

### `lib/gate-probes.mjs`
- `createProbes(o)` → `{ measure(row, setting), probe(row, kind), recheck(row), close() }`; `belongsTo(selector, block)`; `probeValues(property)`.

### `lib/gaps-file.mjs`
- `gapRow(r, { surface, publishedOn, firstSeen })`, `writeGaps({ file, rows, surface, publishedOn })` → `{ file, rows, dropped }`, `gapsPath(client, root)`, `OWNERS`.

### `lib/resolve-declared.mjs`
- `declaredTiers(declaredForm, tiers, tierOf)` → `{ tier: declared }` for the tiers being written.
- `declaredUnit({ prop, decl, tiers, unit, attr, current, flat })` → the unit a unit setting switches to (every written tier declares it, it differs, no unwritten tier would be reinterpreted; never a per-tier unit object or flat siblings), else null.
- `declaredLength({ prop, declared, def, unit, forms, box })` → `{ value }` in the setting's form, or null to format as rendered. `resolveWeightKeyword(prop, declared)` → a declared `bold`/`normal` weight as `700`/`400`. `declaredUnit` also takes `defaultValue`, the schema default whose tiers count as held.
- `sizeKeyword({ prop, raw, def, unit })` → a size keyword as a setting's value: `{ value }` where the setting's emitter prints a bare keyword, `{ error }` where the property does not take it or this setting cannot print it, null where it is not a size property or keyword.

### `lib/fill-prop.mjs`
- `OWNED`: The settings a content writer decided from the draft for a tree node (an icon's source from its mark, `lib/fill-icon.mjs::iconDecisions`'s `decided`), keyed by the node object: `lib/fill-resolve.mjs::fillTree` decides every node's content before any node's look and sets them, and every resolver call on the node (`resolveProperty`'s `owned`, passed to `lib/resolve.mjs::resolveDiscovered` and `lib/css-equivalence/effect.mjs::discoveredWrite`) offers only the decided value of such a setting.
- `ownedCopy`: Registers a working copy of a node (`lib/fill-row-lift.mjs`'s read of one icon) as holding its source's decided settings; returns the copy.
- `COVERAGE_WRITES`: the coverage IDs this module writes (the CSS groups a draft header's Fill run wrote and confirmed, and every other property the register names this module as writing); `coverageProblems` holds each entry's write to this module, both ways.
- `TIER_WIDTHS`: 1440, 768, 375. `INHERITED`: calibration's inherited list, re-exported rather than copied. `CARRIED`: properties read from a text or layout carrier.
- `knownPaths(cal)` → every path a calibration knows for a block.
- `canon(prop, v)` → the value as painted, for comparison. `notPainted(prop, styles, snap, declared)` → whether the property paints nothing here (a 0s `transition-duration` too, where `declared` is known and lacks it).
- `defaultPaint(cal, slot, width, prop)` → the calibrated default paint, or undefined.
- `tiersDiffering(prop, draft, baseline)` → `{ width: value }` for the tiers that differ, carrying wider values down.
- `resolveProperty(ctx)` → `{ status: 'equal' | 'written' | 'gap', … }`, always through `lib/resolve.mjs::resolve`.
- `PX_TOL` (from `lib/css-equivalence/tolerance.mjs`); `gapPaintsNothing(prop, styles, snap)` → why a gap paints nothing on this element, or null; `calibratedContext(cal, slot, width)` → the context calibration read an element's default paint in, or null; `inOwnContext(prop, v, ctx)` → a baseline value as the value an unstyled element holds, so a `min-height` of 0px read off a flex item compares as `auto`.

### `lib/fill-equivalents.mjs`
- `equivalentRows(db, prop)` → the framework DB's `property_equivalents` rows for a draft property (none when the DB predates the table).
- `everyChildSame(kids, prop, draft, styleOf)` → whether every child was measured and asks the draft's value at each width.
- `rendersAt(draft, rectOf)` → whether the element renders at the declared px length at every width.
- `ratioOf(raw)` → the width over height a declared `aspect-ratio` states (`3741 / 848`, `auto 3741 / 848`, `1.5`), or null.
- `intrinsicRatio(widths, snapOf, declaredOf)` → whether the element states a ratio (the page-read `intrinsic.ratio` of its image or SVG, else its declared `aspect-ratio`) and its measured box keeps it within `RATIO_TOL` at every width.
- `CONDITIONS`: the conditions `fillEquivalent` evaluates (`every-child-same`, `render-matches`, `intrinsic-ratio`); a row naming another is skipped and named, never applied.
- `fillEquivalent(st, rec, target, prop, draft, hooks)` → after a `no-setting` gap, the write through a setting for another property on the element (`self`) or its parent (`parent`, once per parent, a later sibling `held`) with the row's `condition`, `{ status: 'gap', gap: 'unknown-condition' }` when only rows with an unknown condition matched, or null; writes carry `how: 'equivalent:<hop>'`. `hooks.rendered` is the property's rendered value per width before a used-value property was cut to its plain declared widths; an `intrinsic-ratio` row writes from it. An `intrinsic-ratio` row also offers the slots where the block's own setting for that property paints (a logo block's image), after the element's own slot.

### `lib/fill-resolve.mjs`
- `contentWidth(st, rec)` → the read width a node's content (presence, words, links, icon, image) is taken at: 1440 where the node is drawn there, else the widest read width it is drawn at (a width variant hidden from 768 keeps its link and icon).
- `fillTree(options)` → `{ writes, unmapped, alreadyHeld, notes, handover, spacing, fluid, held, snaps, settleHeld }` (`settleHeld(result)` re-runs the already-held classifier over the rows and writes every writer has added; fill.mjs calls it once before the report), filling the tree in place. Spacing ownership reads the declared values of, and applies its decided gap at, the element that holds the node's gap: the target at the calibrated slot of the block's `gap` setting when the skeleton gives one (a container's inner element), else the node's own element.

### `lib/draft-link.mjs`
- `resolveDraftLink(href, ctx, { file, handler })` → `{ kind, value?, route?, owner?, why?, dropped?, routes? }`: `address` (the page-map row's site address), `external`, `site` and `anchor` (the href as it is), `other` (a relative link that is no draft route), `none` (a `#` whose handler reaches no single page; `routes` names the pages a per-item handler reaches) or `unresolved` (`owner` `product-data` or `content-page`, and `why`). `ctx` null resolves every draft route to `unresolved`.
- `linkContext({ rows, routing, routeSlug, proposed, catalogue?, menuItems? })` → the resolver context; `draftLinksFor({ repo, client, routing?, proposedRows? })` → a client's context (null with no page map); `siteLinkResolver(ctx)` → `(href, e) => answer` for an inventory element; `linkWrite(answer, href)` → `{ value, handover }`.

### `lib/fill-route.mjs`
- `hashRoutePermalink(href, pages)` → `/<parent>/<slug>/` (or `/` for home and `#/`) for a `#/<route>` href whose route a `page` row of the page map carries, else null; `permalinkOf(row, pages)` → the permalink of one row through its `parent` chain.

### `lib/fill-resolve-state.mjs`
- `makeState(options)` → the run state built once for a Fill run; `planSpacing(st, rec)` decides each tier's gap owner and the margins the children give up; `baselineFn(st, rec, cal, prop, ownFontPx)` → `(slot, width) => value`, what the node shows with nothing written; `ledgerView(st, rec, ref, prop, draft, pseudo)` → `{ draft, rules, held }` over the divergence ledger; `alternateSlots(cal, prop, draft, apart)` (the other slots a property could paint: any carried property, and any other when `apart` names the slots the node's other targets read, never one that paints a nested block's element), `reachesChildBlock(slot)` (whether a calibrated slot path has a step named by another block's root class), `gapTarget(st, rec)`, `snapsOf(st, id)`, `draftOf(snaps, get)`, `valueText(draft)` (a readable value for a per-width map), and the shared `GAP_PROPS`, `slugClass`, `json`.
- `mergedRadius(drafts)` → the four equal corner radii as one `border-radius` draft, else null; `RADIUS_CORNERS` the four longhands.
- `ownPaint(cal, slot, w, prop, fs)` → the block's own paint of an inherited property at a slot, from calibration's `_own:<prop>` beside the element's font size `_fs`; a line height is carried as its ratio of the font size.

### `lib/fill-ancestor.mjs`
- `partWideWrites(st, { hop })` → `{ rows, settled, written }`: runs the part-wide pass over `st.hopRows` (the `no-setting` rows `fillElement` records), writing each host setting once and settling the rows it answers; `hop` replaces `resolveViaAncestor` for the negative control.
- `homesOf(cal, part)` → the host's calibrated paths (one per distinct form) whose steps end with a child part's own path; `[]` for the root.
- `instanceDraft(st, target, prop)` → what one measured instance asks for a property, `{ width: value }`, judged as Fill judges it (a value that paints nothing is left out, four equal corners are one `border-radius`, null when they differ).

### `lib/fill-resolve-element.mjs`
- `fillElement(st, rec, ref, cal, target, snaps, layer)` → writes every property of one element (or its `::before` / `::after` layer) that differs, through the resolver; `apply(st, rec, ref, target, prop, res, slot)` writes one resolver result and its fixture switch; `ratioWrite(...)` tries the fixed-ratio equivalent for a not-plain property; `unmapped(st, rec, ref, row)` records an UNMAPPED row.
- `declaredFluid`: The fluid value the code declares for a property when it declares the same clamp(), min() or max() at every read width ({ width: value }), else null: a value that changes with the width is a per-tier one.
- `settled(st, rec, ref, row)` → a value read that ends with nothing to write and nothing unwritten, with its `SETTLED` reason. `recordOwner(st, rec, target, prop, attr, direct)` → records the element that wrote a setting. `clashes(a, b, at)` → the places where two values of one setting both say something and differ. `ownerKeeps(st, rec, ref, target, prop, w, settingSlot)` → whether a write goes ahead over what another element of the node wrote to the same setting; the element the setting sits on wins over a value carried from another.
- `checksRan({ layer, alternates, res, eq })` → `{ checks, unchecked, alternates }` on a `no-setting` row: the checks that ran (`own-slot`, `calibrated-slots`, `equivalence`) and the one the part-wide pass runs after the walk (`ancestor`, listed under `unchecked` until `lib/fill-ancestor.mjs::partWideWrites` has looked at the row).
- `notPaintedWhy(prop, styles, snap, declared)` → why `lib/fill-prop.mjs::notPainted` judged a property inert on this element at this width.
- `answeredWidths(prop, draft, res)`: the read widths a written result leaves showing the draft's value (`answers`): a width whose shown tier the result writes with a value that paints the same, or that no written tier reaches; a fluid value answers every width it was fitted to.

### `lib/fill-spacing.mjs`
- `EQUAL_PX`: 0.5, the tolerance at which two rendered gaps are equal.
- `spacingOwnership(rects, { unmeasured, declared })` → `{ owner, count, decisions }`: a flex or grid parent that declares a gap on an axis owns it at the declared value when the render confirms it (every rendered gap at least the declared one under a `space-*` justify-content on that axis, otherwise within `EQUAL_PX`); otherwise the parent owns the gap only when every rendered sibling gap is equal. Each decision carries `source` (`declared` | `rendered`), `distributed` (the `space-*` value) and `disagree` (a declared gap the render did not confirm). `declared` is the parent's declared longhands at that tier (`lib/fill-resolve.mjs` passes `st.reads.declared[w]['<index>:']`).
- `declaredGap(declared, prop)` → the gap a flex or grid parent declares on one axis in px, or null (`normal`, 0, no layout, not a plain length).
- `decisionFor(ownership, prop)` → the decision for `row-gap` or `column-gap`, or null.

### `lib/fill-binding-group.mjs`
- `bindingOf(row)` → `{ where, expression, draftId }` for a census binding row (from its `detail` or its id), else null.
- `NO_FACTS` (`'no fact record'`, what `binding-groups.json` records as `skipped` when `facts` is null) and `noFactsWarning(file)` (the plain warning Fill prints naming the missing fact record).
- `groupBindings({ facts, nodes, writes, rootComponent })` → `{ groups, split, skipped? }`: a group is one style binding that fed the same setting the same value on two or more elements (`{ id, binding, attr, value, members, sections }`; each grouped write gains `group`); a binding written different values is a `split` entry, never a group. Elements are matched by the node's tpl finder and the census draft id.

### `lib/fill-values.mjs`
- `FLUID_WIDTHS`, `SWEEP_WIDTHS`, `SGS_BOUNDARIES`, `LENGTH_PROPS`, `FLUID_TOL`: the sample widths, the 16px sweep range, the SGS breakpoints, the properties swept and the 0.5px linearity tolerance.
- `fitFluid(samples)` → a line fit with its `clamp()` text, or null when the value steps.
- `acceptsClamp(calibration, attr)` → whether the setting's `forms` lists `clamp`.
- `applyClamp(writes, clamp, side)` → the writes with the per-tier values replaced by one clamp.
- `nearestBoundary(width)` → `{ boundary, offset }`.
- `breakpointSteps(prop, series)` → each step with its nearest SGS boundary.
- `sweepSteps(sweep, labelOf)` → every step of a sweep, labelled and ordered.

### `lib/fill-unread.mjs`
- `STRUCTURE_ROLES`: database roles of an enum with no CSS property that changes what a block renders (`layout`, `select-from-enum`).
- `structureRowsFor(db, block)` → the block's SGS-owned enums with no `css_property` in a `STRUCTURE_ROLES` role.
- `unreadOf({ db, block, cal, attributes, slots, written })` → `[ { kind, slot, settings, reason } ]`: kinds `element`, `presence`, `text`, `link`, `structure`. `fillTree` adds `node` and `block` and returns them as `unread`; `fill-report.md` lists them under Not read.

### `lib/fill-presence.mjs`
- `textSettings(calibration)` → the content settings the slot map's `text` key ties to an element: the settings `textDecisions` writes, and the ones `lib/content-coverage.mjs` claims for the measured route.
- `linkSettings(calibration)` → the address settings the slot map's `link` key ties to an element (an entry whose attr is `target` is no address): the settings `linkDecisions` writes.
- `presenceSettings(calibration)` → the settings the slot map's `presence` key names (a `<attr>=<value>` key names `<attr>`): the settings `presenceDecisions` writes.
- `COVERAGE_WRITES`: the coverage IDs whose register entry names this module as its writer, listed one per line and sorted; `coverageProblems` refuses a declared ID whose entry names another module and an entry naming this module that is not listed.
- `plainText(s)` → text without markup, whitespace collapsed.
- `presenceDecisions(input)` → `{ writes, unmapped, notes }` for visibility and variant settings, from calibration's `presence`.
- `textDecisions(input)` → the same for words, from calibration's `text`.
- `normaliseHref(href, origin, pages)` → the href as WordPress holds it, or null; a hash route (`#/apply`) is the permalink of the page map's page for that route (`pages`: the rows of `build/page-map.json`, passed to Fill as `pages`; `lib/fill-route.mjs::hashRoutePermalink`), null with no such page, and a bare `#` stays null. `linkDecisions(input)` → the same for links, from calibration's `link`; a setting bound to Site Info (`metadata.bindings.<attr>.args.key`) is still written and also returns a `handover` row, which `fill-resolve.mjs::fillContent` records as a `site-info` / `link` handover entry. A block whose `link` names one distinct path (the nested anchor `sgs/icon` calibrates as `.sgs-icon__link`) also takes the address at the draft's root slot when no slot names that path: the draft's own anchor, or the sole anchor inside the copied element, is that block's one link; a block with two link paths takes no root guess.

### `lib/fill-link-attrs.mjs`
- `COVERAGE_WRITES`: the coverage IDs whose register entry names this module as its writer, listed one per line and sorted; `coverageProblems` refuses a declared ID whose entry names another module and an entry naming this module that is not listed.
- `blockSupportsAnchor(slug)` → whether block.json declares `supports.anchor`.
- `linkAttrDecisions(input)` → `{ writes, unmapped, notes }` for a link's `target` (a boolean `...NewTab` or string `...Target` setting, or a calibrated `target` link entry), `rel` (a `rel` or `...Rel` setting; noopener and noreferrer are the block's own for a new tab), `id` (the `anchor` attribute, root element only, when `supports.anchor`) and `aria-label` (an `ariaLabel` or `...AriaLabel` setting; a picture's own aria-label, `imageName`, goes to the block's one image-alt setting, `imageAlts` from `lib/fill-media.mjs::imageSlots`, when read on the node's picture slot, `pictureSlot` from `lib/fill-media.mjs::pictureSlotOf`). A link's companion setting shares its address setting's prefix; a block with none gets an UNMAPPED row named `target`, `rel`, `id` or `aria-label`. `fill-read.mjs` reads the four from the draft element; `fill-resolve.mjs::fillContent` applies them; `scripts/parity/lib/links.mjs::compareLinkAttributes` compares them on the live render.

### `lib/fill-media.mjs`

- `mediaSettings(slots, schema)` → the settings `mediaDecisions` writes for a block: its one image slot's address, attachment id and alt, and a string `caption`; `[]` for a block with no slot or several.
- `COVERAGE_WRITES`: the image coverage IDs this module writes (`html:img`, `img.src`, `img.alt`, `figure`, `figcaption`); `coverageProblems` holds each entry's write to this module, both ways.
- `readMediaMap(file)` → `sites/<client>/build/media-map.json` or null. `imageSlots(db, block)` → `[{ alt, url }]` from the block's DB `image-alt` rows (`alt_companion_attr` names the URL setting). `draftPath(src, origin)` → the draft file an image address names, or null (data:, another origin). `mapEntries(map, site, path)` → the media-map files whose repo path is, or ends at a folder boundary with, that path.
- `idSetting(schema, urlAttr)` → the attachment id setting for a URL setting (`imageUrl` to `imageId`, else `<attr>Id`) when block.json declares it.
- `mediaDecisions(input)` → `{ writes, unmapped, notes }`: URL, id, alt and (from a figcaption) caption for a block with exactly one image setting; a held setting is kept; every miss (no map, not in the map, ambiguous, external, several image settings, a bare `<img>` on a block with none) is an `image` row in UNMAPPED and the draft's own address is never written. Called from `fill-resolve.mjs::fillContent`.
- `paintedImageCheck({ map, site, painted, id })` → `{ ok, reason }`: the painted URL (a WordPress size included, through `media-upload.mjs::paintedAsset`) is the attachment Fill wrote.
- `factImage`: What the fact record states about the image a draft element is (lib/fact-record.mjs: `elements[key].assets` holds the file's intrinsic ratio, `elements[key].alt` the placeholder an image-slot element carries as its alt): { alt, ratio, asset } for the element's template key, each null when the record does not state it. A placeholder that is a `{{ }}` hole is run-time text, so it is no alt.
- `pictureSlotOf`: The slot of the one picture a node holds (an image read or a picture's own name): the node's own element when it is one, else the single descendant slot that is one; null when none or two. `linkAttrDecisions` writes a picture's name to the image-alt setting only from this slot.
- `nestedImage`: The image a node holds: the node's own element when it is one, else the single image any one of its descendant elements (a nested slot) reads. snaps: { slot: the element's desktop read }. null when none or when two slots each hold one.
