- `mapPool(items, n, fn)` → `fn(item, index)` for every item, at most `n` of the call and the pool's slots in all at once, results in input order (a plain loop when the pool or `n` is 1 or inside a slot); `withSlot(fn)` → `fn()` run in one slot.

### `lib/intake-process.mjs`
- `ENV_ALLOW`: the variables an intake worker receives, with the four speed switches `INTAKE_POOL`, `INTAKE_SKIP_QUIET`, `INTAKE_PARALLEL_WIDTHS` and `INTAKE_PARALLEL_VISITS` (`tests/intake-sandbox.test.mjs` refuses an `INTAKE_*` variable the code reads that is missing from it). `secretNames(env)` → the names that may hold or reach a secret (`PWD` and `OLDPWD` excepted). `cleanEnv(env)` → the allowlisted copy.
- `assertSecretFree(env?)` throws listing every secret name present. `runIsolated(modulePath, args, { cwd, timeout })` → `{ code, stdout, stderr }` from a worker run with `cleanEnv(process.env)`; a non-zero exit throws.

### `lib/intake-prose.mjs`
- `PROSE_FILES`, `NOTE_FILES`, `COMMAND_VERBS`: what is scanned and the command verbs. `commandReason(line, { inShellFence, inFence })` → why a line is command-like, or null.
- `markdownRows(file, markdown)` and `noteRows(file, data)` → rows for one file. `commandLikeLines({ folder })` → `[{ rowId: 'prose:<file>:<line|json path>', file, line, text, why }]`.

### `lib/tokens.mjs`
- `parseTokenCss(text, file)` → `[{ name, value, file, selector, media, line }]`. `resolveTokens(tokens)` → `{ tokens, problems }`, each token with `resolved`, `chain`, `type`, `fallback` and `hex`; a cycle, missing reference or empty result is a problem with `resolved: null`.
- `readTokens(folder)` → `{ tokens, problems, files }` for `tokens/*.css` (a missing folder is a problem). `colourHex(value)` → `#RRGGBB[AA]` or null. `tokenType(name, resolved)` → `colour`, `gradient`, `length`, `font-family`, `font-size`, `shadow`, `easing`, `number`, `other` or `unresolved`.

### `lib/intake-palette.mjs`
- `proposePalette({ tokens, usage, slugs, problems })` → `{ rows: [{ rowId: 'palette:<token>', token, hex, resolved, chain, used, uses, role, roles, by, basis, confidence, why, wantedSlug?, sharedWith }], unused, oneOff, merges, tokens, vocabulary, problems }`; `usage` is `[{ hex, property, draftId, count, tag?, ground?, text? }]` plus the probe's `footer-ground` rows.
- `loadPaletteSlugs(path?)` → `[{ slug, name }]` from theme.json (`THEME_JSON` is the default path). `DEDUPE_DE` (1) and `NEAR_DE` (2).
- Colour maths: `hexToLab(hex)`, `ciede2000(labA, labB)`, `hexDistance(a, b)` (Infinity across different alphas), `dedupeColours(tokens)`.
- Usage: `summariseUsage(usage, hexes)`, `brandFamilies(colours, groupOf)`; `lib/intake-palette-usage.mjs::usageRoles(groups, uses)` → `{ roles: Map group → [{ role, why, weak? }], blocked: { role: hex } }`, `contrast(a, b)` (WCAG ratio), `chroma(hex)`, `SATURATED_C` (20).
- `lib/intake-palette-grounds.mjs`: `readGrounds([rootSelector])` (in-page) → `{ page, grounds, texts, footers }`; `groundUsage(probed, surface)` → the page and footer usage rows.
- Name rules, run in order through `NAME_RULES`: `ruleLinkHover`, `ruleLink`, `ruleHeading`, `ruleReserved`, `ruleOnGround`, `ruleSection`, `ruleFooter`, `ruleBorder` (a saturated colour is never the divider), `ruleStatus`, `ruleWhatsapp`, `ruleTextMuted`, `ruleTextInverse`, `ruleText`, `ruleSurface`, `ruleBrand`.

### `lib/intake-census.mjs`
- `rowId(row)` → `<kind>@<draftId>[/<discriminator>]`, one identity for static and runtime sightings; `assignIds(rows)` sets `row.id`, numbering repeats `~2`, `~3`.
- `mergeSightings(rows, sightings)`: a runtime sighting of a static row makes it `both`, any other becomes a `runtime` row. `markUnreached(rows, { kinds })`: a static-only row of a traced kind is `unreached: true`.
- `applyRuntime(rows, runtime)`: merges the runtime sightings, marks `unreached`, and leaves a row the trace cannot decide as `unreached: null` with its `traceGap`. `runCensus` takes `runtime: { visits, widths, harPath, harness }`.
- `regexCounts(text)` → METHOD.md §4's counts; `crossCheck(sources, rows)` → `{ table, gaps }`, a gap row per disagreeing category.
- `staticCensus({ folder, entry, harPath, sandbox?, timeout? })` → `{ rows, gaps, sources, counts, blocked }` (`blocked`: the aborted requests of the sandbox it opened). `runCensus({ folder, entry, registry?, root?, readers?, harPath?, sandbox?, census?, timeout? })` → `{ rows, gaps, problems, pass, sources, counts, runtime, blocked }`. Re-exports `draftSources`.
- A row is `{ id, kind, draftId, component, detail, source, unreached }` plus `reader` or `gap`; `draftId` is `<component>:<data-dc-tpl>` for a template construct and `<component or script file>:js:<line>:<column>` for code.

### `lib/intake-census-runtime.mjs`
- `TRACED_KINDS`: the kinds the runtime pass decides. `groupVisits(visits, entry)` → page groups from `file`, `file#hash` or `{ file, hash }`.
- `runtimeCensus({ folder, entry, harPath, visits, widths, census, timeout, harness })` → `{ sightings, perVisit, gaps, traceGaps, runtimeOnly, kinds, wallMs, blocked, usage }` (`usage`: every visit's `lib/intake-usage.mjs::collectUsage`, merged, the check on the file read).

### `lib/intake-census-trace.mjs`
- `traceInit()`: the in-page call wrappers and motion listeners; `collect(arg)` → one visit's evidence.

### `lib/intake-census-map.mjs`
- `buildModel(sources)`, `positionOf(model, offset)`, `codeTarget(row, model, rowsByComp)`, `callAt(model, offset)`, `countAt(coverage, component, offset)`: map a script hash and offset (coverage or a wrapper's call site) back to the static row; `CALL_KINDS`, `isCode(draftId)`, `splitId(draftId)`.
- `fnsNamed({ nodes }, name, how)`: the functions a name denotes in one program, `member` (a class method or function field, `this.<name> = <function>`, `this.y` and `this.y.bind(…)` followed to y), `local` (`function <name>`, `const <name> = <function>`) or `key` (an object property or method); `lib/intake-pagemap-flow-openers.mjs` asks for every kind.

### `lib/intake-census-template.mjs`
- `KNOWN_SC_TAGS`: the runtime's `sc-*` tags. `FONT_HOSTS`, `isFontUrl(url)`: the font hosts and font files a head load can name. `cutDraft(text)` → `{ template, logic, logicLine, logicColumn }`. `holes(s)` → the expressions in a string's `{{ }}` holes.
- `draftSources({ folder, entry, harPath?, sandbox?, timeout? })` → every reachable draft with its cut, its stamped template (each element with its `parent`, the nearest enclosing element's `data-dc-tpl`), how it was read (`entry`, `bridge`, `standalone`) and its helmet scripts; an import's component name is paired from `__dcTemplateSource` and checked against the rendered hosts (the runtime strips it from the annotated text). The list carries `blocked`, the aborted requests of a sandbox it opened itself (empty when the caller passed one).
- `templateRows(source, { handlerOf })` → the template rows of one draft. `decode(name)` → the real attribute name of a stamped one (`sc-camel-*`); `realTag(tag)` → the real tag (`sc-raw-*`). A stamped SVG element carries `svg`, its own-case name.

### `lib/intake-census-logic.mjs`
- `parseLogic(text, { startLine, startColumn, sourceType })` → the Babel tree; `walkAst(node, visit)`; `cssDecls(css)` (split as the runtime's `cssToObj` does); `motionKind(prop)` → `transition`, `keyframes` or null.
- `classMembers(cls)` → each member classified or `unknown`; `logicRows(source)` → `{ rows, gaps, model }`; `callSiteRows(ast, ctx)` → timers, observers, `.animate(` calls, listeners and motion written from code; `scriptRows({ component, text })` → the same for a helmet script file.

### `lib/capabilities.mjs`
- `REPO`, `CAPABILITIES_FILE`, `HINTS_FILE`, `groupSlug(group)` (an mdn-data property group's ID slug), `grammarFunctions(css)` (every function mdn-data's CSS grammar names); `buildInventory({ root?, dbFile?, registry?, css?, tags?, bcd?, oembed?, aria?, attributes?, hints? })` → `{ sections: { <name>: { side, source, status, entries: [{ id, label, count, examples, motion }] } } }`.
- `settingKind(row)` → `motion`, `styling`, `mode` or the attribute type; `dbSections(db)`, `codeSections(root?)`, `draftSections({ registry?, css?, tags?, bcd?, oembed?, aria?, attributes?, hints? })`; `snapshotKeyPaths(snapshots)`.
- `sectionOf(id)`, `allEntries(inventory)`, `mergeInventory(fresh, committed)` (an absent section keeps the committed one), `readInventoryFile(file?)`.

### `tools/presentational-hints.mjs`
- `HINTS_FILE`, `SOURCE`; `cssBlockHints(html)`, `proseHints(html)`, `bodyMarginHints(html)` → `[{ attribute, element, css }]` from each form the section writes hints in; `presentationalHints(html)` → one entry per attribute and element.

### `lib/intake-usage.mjs`
- `tally()` → a usage tally (id → `{ count, draftId, props? }`; `props` is the set of a CSS property group's properties the draft used, by standard name). `standardName(prop)` → the standard property browser-compat-data records a vendor or old name under (`-webkit-font-smoothing` → `font-smooth`). `cssUsage(text, tally, draftId, known?, context?)`, `declarationsUsage(text, …)` (a bound declaration names its property only), `elementUsage({ tag, attrs, svg }, …)`, `markupUsage(text, …, { svgFile? })`, `codeUsage(ast, tally, at, known?)` → each returns its parse problems, each naming its draft ID.
- `embedId(url, known?)` → `embed:<host>` (shortened to the closest inventory host; `embed:other` for a host the inventory does not name, when it holds that ID) or null; `cssInString(s)` → the CSS a string in code holds, or null; `cssValue(s, known?)` → whether a string is a bare CSS value.
- `RUNTIME_FILES` → the Spec 48A runtime files (`support.js`, `image-slot.js`) whose script text the file read skips. `cssUsage` leaves out `css-at:media` and the pseudo-elements inside `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` (SGS's own reduced-motion handling covers them).
- `draftUsage({ sources, folder, known })` → `{ usage, problems }`: the file read. `collectUsage()` (in-page, async) → `{ css, inline, elements, blockedSheets, foreignSheets, foreignElements }`; `mergeRuntimeUsage(acc, raw, visit)`; `runtimeUsage(raw, known?)` → the runtime read as a tally; `usageCheck(fileUsage, runtime)` → `[{ id, draftId }]` only the running page showed.

### `lib/clone-coverage.mjs`
- `CONTENT_SETTING_PREFIX` → `content-setting:`, the ID prefix of a per-setting gap row (`content-setting:<block>.<setting>`), which is not an inventory ID (`coverageProblems` holds it to `contentSettingProblems` instead of the inventory).
- `isContentSettingId(id)` → whether an ID is a per-setting gap row.
- `CONTENT_SETTING_OWNERS` → `Track S`, `Track F`, `Track W`: who closes a per-setting gap.
- `contentSettingId(block, attr)` → the row ID for a setting.
- `parseContentSettingId(id)` → `{ block, attr }` of a row ID, or null when malformed.
- `contentSettingProblems(id, entry)` → the problems of one per-setting gap row: it is a gap (no writer, check or exclusion), names its mechanism in `reason`, an `owner` and a `wave`.
- `COVERAGE_FILE`, `COVERAGE_SCHEMA`, `STEPS`; `loadCoverage(file?)` throws listing every schema problem.
- `statusOf(entry)` → `covered`, `gap` or `excluded`; `referenceProblem(ref, { root?, registry? })` → a sentence or null.
- `coverageProblems(inventory, coverage, { root?, registry?, baseline?, writes? })` → every gate problem as a sentence, including an ID a module declares it writes (`writes`, default `declaredWrites()`) whose entry is missing, excluded or names another writer, and an entry whose `write` names a lib module that does not declare it (checked against the declarations on disk, so only when `writes` is not passed); `declaredWrites(root?)` → `{ '<repo path>': [ ids ] }` from each `lib/*.mjs` file's `export const COVERAGE_WRITES`; `gapList(inventory, coverage)`; `seedGaps(inventory, coverage, note)` → `{ coverage, added }`.

### `tests/fixtures/framework-db.mjs`
- `fixtureDb(rows)` → the path of a temp DB built from the committed schema and filled with `rows`; `BASE_ROWS`.

### `lib/intake-registry.mjs`
- `REGISTRY_FILE`, `REGISTRY_SCHEMA`; `loadRegistry(file?)` throws listing every schema problem; `readerFor(entry, { root })` → the reader function, throwing on a missing module or function.

### `lib/intake-construct-readers.mjs`
- `readTemplateElement`, `readLoop`, `readImport`, `readBinding`, `readRenderKey`, `readGetter`, `readHelmetScript`, `readHelmetFont`, `readLink`: registry readers, `(row, { rows, sources })` → what the occurrence is read as, throwing when nothing can read it; `bindingTarget(where)` → `{ target, capability?, attribute? }` or null.

### `lib/intake-ast.mjs`
- `loadDraft(folder, file)` → `{ file, text, cut, tags, idx, error }` (a cut or parse failure is `error`, never a throw); `splitDraft(text)` → the template and `data-dc-script` logic.
- `scanTags(text, from, to)`, `attrOf(tag, name)`, `decodeEntities(s)`, `lineMap(text)`: template start tags and attribute values.
- `parseLogic(code, logicLine)` → `{ ast, code, parent, all, line(node) }`; `subtree(idx, node)`, `classMethod(idx, name)`, `enclosingFn(idx, node)`, `snippet(idx, node, max)`.
- `resolveName(idx, id)` (nearest scope, destructuring and parameters), `fnBinding(idx, fn)`, `callsOf(idx, binding)`.
- `evalStr(idx, node, depth, env)` → `{ values, partial, unresolved }`; `readBinding(value)`, `bindingKey(expr)` for `{{ }}` attribute bindings; `keyName`, `propName`, `isFn`, `isThisMember`.

### `lib/intake-routing.mjs`
- `loadExport(folder)` → `{ entries, pages, mounted, drafts }` (entries from `entry_drafts`, drafts reachable by FR-48A-3).
- `ADAPTERS`: `hash`, `state-page`, `files`, each `{ name, matches(exp) → { match, evidence }, read(exp) → { routes, links, gaps } }`. `pickAdapter(exp)` applies the precedence rule and throws naming the adapters and evidence on none or a double match.
- `readRouting({ folder })` → `{ adapter, routes, links, gaps }`; links carry `route`, `external` or `control`. `embeddedManifest(draftText, draftId)` → `{ manifest, gaps }`.

### `lib/intake-breakpoints.mjs`
- `draftBreakpoints(draft)` and `readBreakpoints({ folder })` → per draft `{ draft, basis, evidence, thresholds: [{ value, operator, variable, basis, expression, location }], gaps }`.
- `widthReader(draft, gaps)` → `{ keys, widthRef, widthish, sources }`, the draft's width readers (`lib/read-logic-conditions.mjs` uses `widthRef`); `numberOf(idx, node)` → a static number or null.

### `lib/intake-harness.mjs`
- `HARNESS_DEFAULTS` (clock `2026-01-05T10:00:00Z`, seed, `stepMs` 16, `timeoutMaxMs` 10000, `intervalTicks` 3, `rafFrames` 60, `scrollStepMs` 100, `settleMaxMs` 30000). `DISABLE_ITEMS`: the items only the negative controls may switch off.
- `harnessOptions(opts)` → the checked options (`skipQuiet`: on unless `opts.skipQuiet` is false or `INTAKE_SKIP_QUIET=0`; never recorded); `harnessSettings(opts)` → the `harness` object stored in each record and in `intake.json`.
- `quietSkip(q, limit, { stepMs })` → the clock jump across a quiet stretch: whole steps, at least one step short of the next due time `q` (null: none) and of `limit`, 0 under two steps.
- `bootStorageKeys(logicSource)` → `[{ store, key, expr, resolved }]`, keys the logic both reads and writes (from the tree).
- `preparePage(page, opts)` installs the paused clock and the in-page harness before navigation (refuses a context prepared twice); `gotoDraft(page, origin, file, { hash, timeout })` opens the draft and throws on a draft error; `draftErrors(page)` → `{ page, console }`.
- `ensureFonts(page, opts)` throws naming any face not `loaded`; `settleRest(page, opts)` → a summary of the rest-mode settle. `runClock(page, ms, opts)` runs the clock ms in stepMs steps with a real frame after each, the first always stepped and later quiet stretches jumped as `quietSkip` allows (the forced session's `nudge`; `INTAKE_SKIP_QUIET=0` steps every step). `drainTimeouts(page, opts, capMs)` runs the clock until no timeout and no frame callback is pending (intervals keep running), at most `capMs` of virtual time: the timers a forced event started finish before the state is put back (`lib/intake-defects-chain.mjs` calls it through the forced page's `drain`).
- `sampleTimes(duration)` → `[0, d/2, d]`; `targetAnimations(page, target)`; `sampleAt(page, { target, times, kind, read })` → the samples.
- `openHarness({ folder, index, harPath, file, hash, width, height, timeout, ... })` → `{ sb, page, options, settings, close }`, one fresh sandbox per run.
- `RAW_PAGE`: the key a wrapped page (`lib/intake-defects-force.mjs::bounded`) answers with the page it wraps.

### `lib/intake-harness-page.mjs`
- `harnessInit(cfg)`: the in-page init script (seeded random, storage, timeout tracking, interval and frame-loop caps, animation registry, frame wait, `quietMs` (virtual ms to the next possible change), hold, seek and font helpers).

### `lib/intake-reading.mjs`
- `READING_SCHEMA`; `READING_PROPS` (the walker's `DEFAULT_PROPS` without the synthetic `icon-*` entries).
- `readRecords(page, { surface, mode, width, state, t, rootSelector, props, harness })` → the records (refused without `harness`); `sortRecords(records)`; `toJsonl(records)`; `readingProblems(records, ajv)`.
- `readSurface({ folder, index, harPath, file, hash, width, mode, surface, state, sample, ... })` → `{ records, jsonl, settings, summary, url }`.

### `lib/intake-siteinfo.mjs`
- `COVERAGE_WRITES`: the coverage IDs whose register entry names this module as its writer, listed one per line and sorted; `coverageProblems` refuses a declared ID whose entry names another module and an entry naming this module that is not listed.
- `SYNC_SCRIPT`, `PROVISION_SCRIPT`: the two plugin scripts, run as commands only. `linkCandidate(href, where)` → a candidate from one link.
- `runSyncPrint({ draft, python })` (throws if `--push` is ever in its arguments) and `parseSyncPrint(text)` → the print's key/value pairs.
- `proposeSiteInfo({ folder, routing?, census?, syncPrint?, python? })` → `{ rows: [{ rowId: 'siteinfo:<key>[#n]', key, value, normalised, sources, valid, reason }], refused, gaps }`.
- `titleCandidates(titles)` → `[{ key: 'site_title' | 'tagline', raw, draftId }]` from the drafts' titles (`readHead(census).titles`), the part every title shares being the site title.
- `provisionKeys`: the keys the provisioning script fills (the brand registry's Site Info keys plus phone, email, address). `handoverTargets(key)` → `[{ owner: 'site-info' | 'wp-option', key }]` or null. `handoverFor(confirmedRows)` → `{ entries, notApplied }`, honouring `drop` and `edit:<value>` marks.
- `provisionDryRun({ site, handoverFile, script?, python?, timeout? })` → `{ table, lines, code, stderr, gaps }`, never with `--apply`; `parseProvisionTable(text)` → its table rows.

### `lib/intake-siteinfo-shape.mjs`
- `refusal(raw)` → why a value is refused (`{{ }}`, placeholder host, `#`) or null. `validate(key, raw)` → `{ valid, normalised, reason }` through `phoneShape`, `emailShape`, `socialShape`, `addressShape`, `hoursShape`.
- `DAYS`, `POSTCODE`, `parseHoursText(text)`, `socialBrands`, `socialKey(url)`, `brandByLabel(label)`.
- `locator(census)` → a function giving `<component>:<data-dc-tpl>` (or a file location) for a template or code position; `textRuns(draft)` → the template's text runs.

### `lib/intake-flags.mjs`
- `proposeFlags({ folder, census?, siteInfo?, stockHosts? })` → `{ rows: [{ rowId: 'flag:<kind>:<draftId>[:n]', kind, draftId, text|url, why, source, location }], gaps }`.
- `placeholderWhy(text)`, `isStockUrl(url, hosts)`, `readmePlaceholderLines(text)`.

### `lib/intake-skeletons.mjs`
- `SURFACE_KINDS`: the kinds that get their own skeleton (header, footer, drawer, menu, form, modal, global, page); other records are `parts` of a surface.
- `stripOptions(surface, allSurfaces)` → a header surface's `strips` reading option for `readInventory` (null for any other kind); `proposeSkeletons` reads a header first and drops a global surface whose root is a strip the header took (it is that header's row).
- `surfaceRoots({ folder, census, records? })` → `{ surfaces: [{ name, kind, component, rootTpl, route, selector, states, panel? }], parts, gaps }`; a menu record whose open states are tab panels gives one surface per panel, each with only that state.
- `tabPanels(record)` → the open states of a menu record that are its tab panels (each sets the same state keys to its own values; a closed or default state and a state setting no value are none), or `[]` (one open state, states over different keys, two with one value, or any other kind). `proposeSkeletons` copies a surface's `panel` into its intake.json entry.
- `proposeSkeletons({ folder, harPath, surfaces, client, db?, facts?, entry?, routing?, site?, shotsDir?, widths, repo?, harness?, log? })` → `{ skeletons, gaps, runs }`; per surface `{ surface, inventory, rows: [{ rowId: 'skeleton:<surface>:<key>', key, tag, words, block, confidence, low, reason, candidates, action }], problems }`; `runs` records each sandbox's requests, blocked hosts and proxy attempts.
- `openers(surface, census, widthKeys?)` (re-exported from `lib/intake-openers.mjs`) and `describeWay(way)` (re-exported from `lib/state-openers.mjs`): how a surface that does not render at rest is opened (per `surfaces.json` state, its plain state keys through `setDraftState` and the `<ident>.setState(<args>)` calls of its `how_to_reach`, `reachCalls`, run on the component's logic through `reachDraftState` (`via: 'reach'`), the reach first when a key holds a description rather than a value, `lib/state-openers.mjs::isDescription`; else the handler its `opened_by` names through `fireTrigger`); a state bounding a width key opens only at the widths inside it, applied at every such width with the key then set to the width; `proposeSkeletons` takes `census` and `openStates`, reads the width keys from the export's breakpoints, and each entry records `opened` (with `bounds` and `statesAt` for a width-bound opener) and any `readAt` width.
- `summaryOf(inventory)` → the inventory summary an intake entry keeps (element count and check counts); `rowsOf(surface, proposal, inventory)` → a proposal's rows as `intake.json` holds them (row ID, the finaliser's context, `rule`, `into`, `handover`, `builtPost`). `proposeSkeletons` also takes `inventoriesDir` (each surface's full inventory saved as `<surface>.json` for `lib/skeleton-rerun.mjs`) and passes each surface's kind, name and built post (`sites/<client>/build/skeleton/built-surfaces.json`) to `proposeSkeleton`.
- `contextOf(inventory, proposal, key)` → `{ parent: { draftId, tag, block } | null, siblings: [{ draftId, tag, block }], siblingCount, boxes: [{ width, x, y, w, h }] | null }`, the inventory context every skeleton row carries for the finaliser (null where the inventory held nothing); `SIBLING_CAP` (12) limits `siblings` to the nearest in document order.
- `unresolvedRows(skeletons)` → the kept rows with action `unresolved` or no candidate.

### `lib/intake-defects.mjs`
- `WIDTHS`, `STATE_KINDS`, `motionModifier(row)`; re-exports test 1 from `lib/intake-defects-binding.mjs`.
- `unreachedDefects({ folder, entry, harPath, census, visits, widths, onPage?, reads?, flags?, only?, force })` → test 2's verdicts; `onPage(session, at, into)` writes its reads into `into` (each width's Map is appended to `reads` in width order); `only` limits it to a set of census row IDs; `force` (`pseudo`, `state`, `events`, `props`, `deadCode`, `deadField`, `dataGate`, `reducedMotion`, `drag`, `coords`, `chain`: false) exists only for the negative controls.
- `proposeDraftDefects({ folder, entry, harPath, census, visits, widths, harness, flags? })` → `{ rows: [{ rowId: 'defect:<draftId>[:n]', draftId, censusId, component, test, kind, evidence, widths }], groups: [{ groupId: 'defect-group:<component>:<member>', component, member, rowIds, kinds, evidence }], notDefects, gaps }`.

### `lib/intake-defects-binding.mjs`
- `boundStyleRows(census)`, `classifyCompiled({ value, rule })` → `unresolved`, `dropped`, `resolved` or `no-rule`; `readPageBindings(page)`, `bindingVerdicts(...)`, `unresolvedBindingDefects({ pages, census })`.

### `lib/intake-defects-keys.mjs`
- `logicModel(source)`, `conditionKeys(...)` (the state keys a condition reads, with the literal values the code writes or compares), `patchesOf(...)`, `memberAt(...)`, `propsGating(...)` (whether a construct's gate reads `this.props.*`), `defectGroups(rows)`, `mountedProps(rows)` → Map(component → the prop names its parents mount it with, from the census's `dc-import` rows; FR-48-29).

### `lib/intake-defects-force.mjs`
- `FORCEABLE`, `PSEUDO_ELEMENTS`; `openForcedPage({ …, forcePseudoState?, drag?, coords? })`: one harness page per width with the forcing tools installed; the session adds `dispatchAt(row, index)`, `copies(row)` (the copies dispatchAt can fire on: all for a pointer event, the first for any other), `gestures()`, `snapshot(slot?)`, `restore(slot?)` and `changed(slot?)`.
- `timedOut`: a browser call that did not return within its bound. `unless`: a catch handler that answers a value for a failed call and rethrows a timed-out one. `bounded(obj, ms, name, doing)` → `obj` (a page, its clock, mouse or keyboard, or a CDP session) with every promise-returning method bounded to `ms`. `forcedVisits({ file, slice, width, open, visit, log })` → a page group's visits at one width; a visit whose call times out becomes a gap row naming its route, width and call, and the next visit opens a fresh page.

### `lib/intake-defects-gesture.mjs`
- `DRAG_TYPES`, `POINTER_TYPES`; `handlerInit` (an init script recording each listener with its source), `gestureIn({ op, … })` (in-page: fires recorded listeners or one template copy with drag gestures and pointer coordinates, delegated listeners on the elements their own `closest()`/`matches()` selectors name).

### `lib/intake-defects-chain.mjs`
- `CHAIN_SLOTS` (the named snapshot slots the pass uses and deletes when it ends); `chainPass(session, { triggers, patches, pending, check, rendered, pseudoPass, maxCopies?, budget? })` → `{ attempts, opened, exhausted, lost }`: two-step forced states (an opener, then each handler on what it rendered), state put back after each attempt; only fired attempts count against the budget; a route change is undone until the same components render (the URL re-opened with popstate and hashchange when needed); `exhausted` (budget hit with candidates pending) and `lost` (route not restored) become a gap on each pending row in `forceVisit`.

### `lib/intake-defects-deadfield.mjs`
- `deadFieldVerdict(source, off, census)` → `{ note, kind }` or null; `deadFieldWhy(...)` → its note. Why a construct whose guard needs an instance field written only in dead code (by `deadCodeVerdict`) or nowhere is dead; the kind is `unwired` when nothing writes the field or every write is unwired, `constant-guard` when any write sits behind a constant guard. Writes counted: assignments, destructuring targets, for-in/of left sides, `++`/`--`, `Object.defineProperty`/`defineProperties`, `Reflect.set`/`defineProperty` on `this`; a `this` alias, a same-name write on any other object, or an unreadable key makes it null.

### `lib/intake-defects-static.mjs`
- `deadCodeVerdict(source, off, census)` → `{ note, kind: 'constant-guard' | 'unwired' }` or null; `deadCodeWhy(...)` → its note; `definedTag(...)` → the custom element a script defines; `prototypeOnlyWhy(...)` → why a construct sits in flagged placeholder or prototype code, or null; re-exports `dataGatedWhy` from `lib/intake-defects-datagate.mjs`.
- `DEAD_KINDS` (`unwired`, `constant-guard`, `data-gated`: the `deadKind` a dead-code not-a-defect entry carries; only `unwired` is listed in review.md); `isUnwiredEntry(entry)` → true for unwired dead code, by its `deadKind`, else (a record without one) `isUnwired(note)` over `UNWIRED_PHRASES`, the endings of the unwired reasons' notes.

### `lib/intake-defects-datagate.mjs`
- `dataGatedWhy(census, { row, target? })` → `{ note, kind: 'data-gated' }` or null: the row sits inside an sc-if whose loop-item field is false for every item of the list the draft's own constant data gives (writes after creation followed), or is code reached only through a plain selector whose every matched element sits inside one. Refused on a non-constant list or field, an item where the field is true or unreadable, a census without stamped `parent` links, a bound matched attribute or a string naming the selector's attribute, class or id.

### `lib/intake-missing.mjs`
- `INSTRUMENT_DEBT`, `BEHAVIOURS`; `mainFlowOf(...)`, `draftIdOfKey(key)`, `skeletonMissing(...)`, `bodyEvidence(...)`, `behaviourOf(...)`, `heldBy(...)`, `censusMissing(...)`.
- `coverageMissing({ skeletonRows, census, coverage, usage? })` → rule 3's rows: one per capability ID the draft uses (`html:<tag>`, `construct:<kind>`, and every ID in `usage`) that the coverage map does not cover (`from: 'coverage'`, with `count`, and the entry's `wave` for a gap or `excluded: true` for Bean's exclusion; an ID marked `runtimeOnly` says the file read missed it).
- `longhands(prop)` → the longhands a property sets, by mdn-data's `computed` list, recursively; `unreadProperties(props)` → the properties of a set that Fill does not read (`lib/fill-read.mjs::PROPS`; a shorthand counts as read only when every longhand is, a custom property always). In `coverageMissing` a `css-group:*` gap with recorded `props` raises no row when Fill reads every property the draft used, and otherwise names the unread ones ("partly supported" when some are read).
- `proposeMissingFeatures({ skeletonRows, census, facts, registry?, coverage?, usage? })` → `{ rows: [{ rowId: 'missing:<…>', draftId, construct, line, mainFlow, why }], held }`; rule 3 and `lib/intake-head.mjs::headMissing` run only when `coverage` is passed.
- `FILL_READS` → what Fill reads and carries: its list without the context reads (direction, column-count, column-width), which it reads only to judge other values.

### `lib/intake-head.mjs`
- `HEAD_WAVE` (`site and surfaces`). `fontUrlFamilies(href)` → `[{ family, weights, italic }]` a Google Fonts address loads ([] for another host).
- `readHead(census)` → `{ fonts, preconnects, metas, titles, unhandled }` from every source's stamped helmet elements; `headMissing(census)` → one missing-features row per unhandled kind (`missing:head:<what>`, `from: 'head'`, `count`, `wave`).

### `lib/head-check.mjs`
- `tokenFonts(css)` → the font loads a design system's `tokens/typography.css` declares (its Google Fonts `@import`s), in `readHead`'s `fonts` shape: the declaration `head-check.mjs` checks when the export has the file. `loadedFamilies(head)` → the families loaded, merged by name; `snapshotFamilies(snapshot)`; `fontCheck(head, snapshot)` → a sentence per loaded family, weight or italic the snapshot does not serve.
- `pageHead(html)` → `{ charsets, viewport, title, description }`; `headCheck(head, html, { siteTitle? })` → a sentence per thing the live head lacks.

### `lib/media-stock.mjs`
- `stockKey(url, hosts?)` → a painted stock URL's identity (scheme, host and path without the query; null for a credit link, an http URL or another host); `stockDownloadUrl(key, width)`; `stockSurvey(repo, client)` → `{ painted: [{ key, width, paintedAt }], unpainted: [{ key, at, reason }] }` from the draft's `*.dc.html`; `stockImages(repo, client)` → its `painted`; `downloadStock(fetchImpl, url, hosts?)` → `{ bytes, type, ext }` or a throw; `signatureMatches(type, head)`; `stockFileName(key, ext)`; `stockReview(client, map, unpainted?)` → the markdown of `stock-review.md`.
- `MAX_STOCK_WIDTH` → the widest stock image requested, 2400 pixels; `DEFAULT_STOCK_WIDTH` is 1600.
- `STOCK_TYPES` → the accepted image content types mapped to their file extensions.
- `sha256Of(buf)` → the hex SHA-256 of a buffer.

### `lib/media-upload.mjs`
- `IMAGE_TYPES`, `sha256(buffer)`; `mediaAssets(repo, client)` → the repo-relative images to upload; `manifestSums(repo, client)` → handoff path → sha256 from the export's `MANIFEST.json` (an export asset whose bytes differ from its entry, or that has none, is refused); `siteCredentials(repo, site)` → `{ url, auth }` from `.claude/secrets/<site>.env` (refused unless `safeSiteUrl(url)`: https, or http to localhost); `readMap(file)`.
- `uploadMedia({ repo, client, site, map, creds, fetchImpl?, apply?, log?, survey?, stock? })` → `{ map, uploaded, skipped, sanitised, wouldUpload, failed, stockFound, stockUnpainted }` (the map is returned, not written); `paintedAsset(map, site, src)` → the map entry a painted URL shows, or null.

### `lib/shop-parts-read.mjs`
- `parseTemplate(source)` → the element tree; `drawerRegions(root, L)`, `buyboxRegions(root, L)`, `filterRegions(root, L)` → the region elements (`L` is `lib/shop-parts-logic.mjs::logicOf(source)`); `unitsOf(region, { file, dc, L, sources, listingKeys, addKeys })` → `{ kind, tag, text, aria, placeholder, type, on, href, conds, file, dc, tpl, rowN, facts }` units (`tpl`: the `data-dc-tpl` of the element the unit was found on); `readShopSurfaces({ records, sources })` → per kind `{ kind, record, file, units, problem }` (a surface the reader cannot find by its structure carries a `problem` and no units).

### `lib/shop-parts-logic.mjs`
- `logicOf(source)` → the parsed logic or null; `valuesOf(L, expr, scope?)`; `effectOf(L, expr, scope?)` → `{ sets, closes, resets, toggles, appends, writesStore, readsTarget }`; `isMoney(L, node)`, `moneyBinding(L, expr)`, `emptinessBinding(L, expr)`, `countBinding(L, expr)`; `selectionReads(L, expr)` → the state keys choosing a list binding's items.

### `lib/shop-parts.mjs`
- `RULES` (rule name → structural test over a unit's facts); `loadVocab()`, `resolveVocab(vocab, { attrs, files, fixtures? })` → the vocabulary with each setting pattern resolved against the DB's attribute names, each part or template home checked against its file, ids unique, rules known, and (with fixtures) each entry reaching a unit of some draft (throws `ShopPartsError` listing every stale entry); `matchSurface(kind, units, vocab)` → parts `{ id, label, home, write, by, units, locators, sample }`; `locatorsOf(units)` → the template keys of the units' elements (`<id>:hint` parts are gap rows); `missingRow(...)` → FR-48-34's row; `buildRecord({ client, surfaces, vocab })` → `{ client, surfaces, counts }`.
- `VOCAB_FILE` → the URL of `data/shop-part-vocab.json`, the shop parts vocabulary.

### `lib/shop-fixtures.mjs`
- `fixtureDefs(client)`, `skuFor(client, kind)`, `META_KEY`; `imageChoice(repo, client, site)`; `requireSafeCreds(creds)`; `requireWooCommerce({ creds, fetchImpl? })` (refuses a site whose REST index lists no `wc/v3`); `SIZE_ATTRIBUTE` (the global `pa_size` choice and its axis from `catalogue-read.mjs::variesByOf`); `planFixtures({ client, creds, fetchImpl? })` → per fixture `exists` or `create`, `convert` (its choice is still product-local) and the variations still missing; `planFixtureVariesBy(client, wp)` → each size term's variesBy state; `ensureImage(...)`; `applyFixtures({ repo, client, site, creds, fetchImpl?, wp })` → `{ created, imageUploaded, products, variesBy }` (each product read back; `wp` is a wp-cli runner, required).

### `lib/shop-fixtures-variesby.mjs`
- `planVariesBy(wp, attributes)` → per term `{ taxonomy, term, termId, current, axis, action }` (`write`, `has` or `missing`); `applyVariesBy(wp, attributes)` → the same rows from before the write, after writing the axis to the terms with none and reading again; `VariesByError`.

### `lib/shop-fixtures-wp.mjs`
- `wpFetch(wp)` → a `fetch`-shaped function that dispatches a request inside the site through `wp eval-file -`.
- `ShopFixturesError` → the error class every refusal of the shop-fixture plan throws.

### `lib/page-map-names.mjs`
- `MEGA_TITLE_SUFFIX`; `megaPanelTitle(label)` → the panel row's title; `megaPanelLabel(title)` → the menu item label.

### `lib/site-build-map.mjs`
- `LAYOUT_TYPES` (post type → active-pointer area), `SHOP_TYPES`, `POSTLESS_TYPES` (`product`, `product_cat`: rows that build a template and make no post); `rowKey(row)` → `<postType>:<slug>`; `readPageMap(file)` → rows (refuses a layout the write-back cannot reproduce, or two rows with one key); `writePageMap(file, original, ids)` → the written rows; `writeMenuMap(file, site, menus)` → `build/menu-map.json` with the menus the site build made (owner equal to slug) by name, for the site.
- `parentRow(rows, row)`, `orderRows(rows)`; `boundMenu(kind, menuNames)` → the page-map menu a layout surface binds (a drawer with no drawer menu binds `header`); `menuSurfaceKind(name, surfaces?)` → the layout surface kind a page-map menu feeds (the menu named like a kind, else the manifest surface of that name by its `trees/sgs_<kind>/` folder, else the kind its name starts with: `footer-shop`, `drawer-menu-links`) or null; `placeholderTree(row, menuIds)`, `treeFor(repo, client, row, menuIds)` → `{ tree, source }`; `pageTreeFor(repo, client, row, menuIds)` (a `shop` row's page takes a placeholder; its tree builds the template) and `templateTreeFor(repo, client, row)`; `treeHash(tree)`; `wpType(row)` (a `shop` row is a `page`), `hasPost(row)` (false for the rows that make no post), `builtTemplate(row)` (the template a shop row builds, its own or the type's default).

### `lib/manifest-build.mjs`
- `PREFIXES` (post type → key prefix), `KINDS` (post type → handoff record kind); `surfaceKey(row)` → the manifest key; `walkerKey(name)` → the walker-state key (trailing parenthesised qualifier dropped); `settingState(state, vocab)` → `scrolled`, `shrunk` or `current` when the state's name has that word and the vocabulary holds it, else null (open, closed and the press look are a surface's own reach, never a setting state here); `pageKeyOf(route)` → the `page_key` a route names (a `<name>.dc.html` file route gives `<name>`, query string and fragment ignored; any other route is its own key); `draftFor(record, drafts)` → the entry draft whose base name is the record's `dc`, else the first; `matchRecord(row, records)` → `{ record }` or `{ reason }` (never by position); `statesOf(record, vocab, ctx?)` → `{ states, pointer, dropped, noContext }`, an open-able surface keeping a state named open or expanded or one that sets a real state key (`ctx` is `facts.json` `state[<dc>]`: a key that is not a state key of the draft's logic, or that the logic derives from the width, is derived; with no `ctx` the key names `R`, `item` and `bp` decide and `noContext` lists those states); `panelStates(row, states)` → a mega menu panel row's states narrowed to the one its title's label names (`page-map-names.mjs::megaPanelLabel`, matched by `walkerKey`), any other row or a label naming none unchanged; `buildManifest({ client, site, rows, records, intake?, facts?, target, entryFile?, entryFiles?, vocab, existing? })` → `{ entries, unresolved, unmatched, pointer, dropped, disagree, skipped, added, stale, noIntake, noFacts }` (each entry carries `intakeSurface`, the slug of the intake skeleton with the matched record's id, and `tree` as `trees/<postType>/<slug>.json`; an entry with no intake skeleton is listed in `noIntake`), throwing when two rows give one key.

### `lib/key-space.mjs`
- `SERVED_ROOT`: `Root`, the served runtime's name for the entry draft's root host. `isServedInventory(inventory)` → true when its element keys start with `Root/`. `servedKey(key, rootComponent)` → the served-space key of a root-host key (`<rootComponent>/<n>#<copy>` becomes `Root/<n>#<copy>`); any other key, an import chain's included, comes back unchanged. `servedDecisions(decisions, rootComponent)` → one surface's client decisions with `servedKey` applied to each key. `servedMotion(keys, rootComponent)` → a set of `<dc>/<tpl id>` motion keys with the root component renamed to `Root`.

### `lib/site-build-menus.mjs`
- `desiredMenus(rows, linkText?, surfaces?, menuLinks?)` → `{ menus: { name: [item] }, sections: { megaRowKey: pageRowKey }, problems, notes }` (each item `{ key, kind, rowKey?, postType?, label, title, parentKey, order }`); a menu's link text is `linkText[menuSurfaceKind(name, surfaces)]`, and a header menu with no drawer menu beside it takes the drawer's text where the header has none; `menuLocation(name, registered)` → a location or null.
- `addressOf(url)` → a URL's path (no trailing slash) and query with the origin dropped, for matching a menu item's stored address across hosts; a `menuLinks` entry (`menuLinksFor`'s answer) becomes an item of kind `link` (`label`, `title`, `url`) at its slot.

### `lib/site-build-menu-links.mjs`
- `LINK_TYPES` → the row types whose menu entries are links to a product or category (`product`, `product_cat`).
- `menuLinksFor({ rows, ctx, catalogue, site })` → `{ links: { <row key>: [{ menu, position, parentItem, text, url }] }, pending: [{ rowKey, menu, text, why }] }`: each product row's menu entries resolved through `draft-link.mjs::resolveDraftLink` (`ctx`, from `draftLinksFor`) and the product's recorded permalink on `site`; a category row's entries are always pending.
- `menuLinksOf({ repo, client, site, rows })` → `menuLinksFor` over the client's page map, routing and catalogue read from disk.

### `lib/site-build-labels.mjs`
- `labelRoutes(drafts, front?)` → a Map from each static `label` beside an `href` in the drafts' object literals to its route (null when two routes claim it; a bare `#` href is a disclosure and names no route); `readLinkTextReport({ repo, client, front? })` → `{ text: { <surface kind>: { <route>: <link text> } }, conflicts: [message] }` from `facts.json` and the handoff drafts (`{ text: {}, conflicts: [] }` when either is missing; two lists on one surface giving one route different text carry no entry and a conflict message); `readLinkText(args)` → its `text`; `readSurfaces(repo, client)` → the client's `build/surfaces.json` (`{}` when absent).

### `lib/skeleton-carried.mjs`
- `sourceMaps(folder)` → a Map from a property key to the image paths a script maps it to; `logoFile({ e, maps, view?, rootComponent? })` → the draft image an element draws (an `<img>` src, else the one file a source map names for a static attribute value), the tag and attributes taken from the fact record's entry for the element (`view` is `recordView`'s result; an element of a served inventory is found under `rootComponent`), the rendered tag and attributes only where the record has no entry; null when there is none; `mediaEntry({ map, site, repo, folder, file })` → `{ id, asset }` for the one attachment whose source sha256 is the file's bytes (a path is never matched by its tail), or `{ reason }`; `carriedIds({ repo, client, site, rootComponent? })` → `{ logo(e, view?), menu(kind) }`. A carried id wins over a hand-set decision value (the row's `stale` note names the unapplied value); a `{ reason }` from `logo` is reported on the row (`carriedMissing`) and in the proposal's `problems`.

### `lib/site-build-state.mjs`
- `ROW_META`, `TREE_META` (the post meta keys), `SECTION_META` (a mega panel's section page id), `MENU_META` (the term meta a menu it made carries), `CONTENT_META` (the hash of a post's saved content), `TEMPLATE_OPTION` (`sgs_site_build_templates`, each template copy's tree hash), `TEMPLATE_CONTENT_OPTION` (each copy's saved-content hash), `isWooDefaultContent(content, roleDefault)` (empty, a `[woocommerce_*]` shortcode, the role's own default content, or WooCommerce-only block markup when the role has no known default), `WOO_ROLE` (each WooCommerce page option's role), `WOO_OPTIONS` (the four WooCommerce page-id options); `statePhp(rows)` → the PHP; `readState(runPhp, rows)` → the state object.

### `lib/site-build-plan.mjs`
- `PHASES`, `PERMALINKS`; `planBuild({ rows, state, repo, client, frontRoute? })` → `{ actions, problems, notes, options }`; `tally(actions)` → `{ creates, changes, adopts }`.
- `wooContentRole(row)` → `'cart'`, `'checkout'`, `'my account'` or null: a page WooCommerce renders itself (its template, or WooCommerce's own page name). `planBuild` takes such a page over but never rebuilds its content, and `site-run.mjs` skips its Fill, build and walk (Bean 2026-10-26: the page content keeps WooCommerce's Cart and Checkout blocks and account shortcode or block; the draft's layout goes into the site's own `cart`, `checkout` and `page-my-account` template copies with WooCommerce's block in the form region, Spec 48 FR-48-56 and FR-48-87, not built).

### `lib/site-build-apply.mjs`
- `wpBuildPage(repo, site)` → `(target, tree) => id`; `applyPhase(actions, phase, { wp, build, onId, log?, refs? })` → steps run.

### `lib/site-build-options.mjs`
- `siteOptionsFile(repo, client)`; `readSiteOptions(repo, client)` → `{ <option>: { records, value } }`; `promoteSiteOptions({ repo, client, from })` → `{ file, changed, options }`; `optionsPhp(names)`, `readStoredOptions(runPhp, desired)`; `planSiteOptions({ desired, stored })` → `{ actions, options, notes }`; `withSiteOptions(plan, { repo, client, runPhp })` (called by `site-build.mjs` after the planner).

### `lib/intake-state.mjs`
- `setDraftState(page, { component, keys })` → instances set (0 = not mounted); `reachDraftState(page, { component, calls, keep? })` → instances that ran every `{ ident, args }` call as `<ident>.setState(<args>)` with their logic bound to `ident`, each key `keep` names put back to its value before the calls (0 = not mounted or a call threw); `logicState` (the in-page function itself, exported so a walker config carries it as source); `snapshotDraftState(page, slot?)`, `restoreDraftState(page, slot?)`, `draftStateChanged(page, slot?)` → counts (`slot` names a separate snapshot).
- `readDraftState(page, { component, key })` → the value of state key `key` on each mounted instance (an empty list = not mounted).
- `fireTrigger(page, { selector } | { component, draftId }, event)` → false when nothing is rendered; `listenerInit` (an init script), `fireListeners(page, types)`, `LISTENER_TRIGGERS`.

### `lib/state-route.mjs`
- `openStateRoute(page, { route, key = 'page', component?, timeout? })` → `{ ok, reached, reason }`: sets `{ [key]: route }` on the live logic of `component` (default: the runtime's `window.__dcRootName()`), settles, and checks the logic holds the route and the runtime still renders.
- `applyStateRoute(page, { timeout? })` → the `openStateRoute` verdict for the route the page's address carries (`#@state/page=<route>`), `null` without one; throws `state route <route> was not reached: <reason>` when not reached. `moveToHash(page, hash)` does the same for a visit's hash and assigns `location.hash` for any other.
- `stateRouteHash(route)` → `'@state/page=<route>'` (`''` for the entry route `*`, `/` or none); `parseStateHash(hash)` → `{ key, route }` or null; `isStatePage(routing)`; `ROOT_HOST`, `PAGE_KEY`, `STATE_HASH`.

### `lib/intake-inventory.mjs`
- `EFFECT_KINDS`, `TRIGGER_KINDS`; `inventoryCensus({ folder, entry, routing, census, harPath })` → the census widened to every page file of a one-file-per-page draft (`{ rows, sources, gaps, blocked }`).
- `inventoryCore(census, { routing?, notDefects?, props? })` → `{ model, anim, surf, occurrences }`; `occurrences(census, model, anim, surf)` → `[{ occurrence, kind, role: effect|trigger|context|surface, recordIds, why? }]`.
- `inventoryBase({ census, folder, routing?, notDefects?, generated? })` → the unsampled inventory, built once; `withSamples(base, samples?)` → `{ animations, surfaces, occurrences, propVariants, samples, problems, gaps, meta }`; `buildInventory({ census, folder, routing?, notDefects?, samples?, generated? })` is the two in one call; `validateInventory(inv)` → master-schema problems; `reconcile(inv, { census?, jsWriters? })` → an occurrence with no record or a count naming a missing record, and with the census a counted row with no occurrence, an occurrence count that differs from the counted rows plus writer sites, and a trigger kind whose `CONTEXT_ONLY_MIN` (3) or more occurrences all fall back to `context`.
- `readTheirs(folder)` → Claude Design's `{ animations, surfaces }`; `readInventory(row, { rows, sources })` → the registry reading `{ recordIds, role, why? }` or null (the inventory is built once per pair of rows and sources lists); `inventoryStage(job, ctx)` → the intake stage's parts.

### `lib/intake-inventory-anim.mjs`
- `CATEGORIES` (the master schema's counted categories as census-row tests); a code-built style string's K record names the template elements holding its hole as `element.nearest_tpl_id`; `animationRecords(census, { notDefects? })` → `{ records, census: Map(row id → record IDs), sites, counts, unresolved, meta }`; `keyframeProperties(stops)` → one `{ property, from, to, keyframes? }` per property an `@keyframes` rule animates (a K record carries them after its `animation-name` row).

### `lib/intake-inventory-tpl.mjs`
- `elementStyles(element)`, `describeElement(comp, element)`, `traceOf(rows, { pseudo? })`; `templateRecords(census, add)` → TR, PS, K and RM records through `add`; `animationRecord(add, row, element, source, opts)`; `applyReducedMotion(records, rmRecords, metaOf)`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-code.mjs`
- `codeModel(census)`, `textOf(prog, node)`, `offsetOf(prog, draftId)`, `lineOf(node)`, `nodeAt(prog, off, re)`, `ancestors(prog, node)`, `declaratorsOf(prog)` (Map name → initialised declarators, built once per program or logic model), `memberOf(prog, node)`, `functionName(prog, node)`, `eventTrigger(event)`; the node tests `isFn`, `isThis`, `prop`, `globalName`.
- `triggerOf(model, comp, node)` → `{ type, detail, state_keys?, holes?, delay, via, members, called }` (`holes`: the renderVals key names a value built in code is passed to the template as); `reducedMotionOf(prog, node)` → `{ behaviour, detail }`; `literalOf(node)`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-target.mjs`
- `targetOf(model, comp, expr)` → `{ tpl, comp, selector, rule? }`; `constValue(prog, node)`; `waapiEffect(prog, call)` → `{ properties, timing, frames }`; `writerSites(prog)` → `[{ node, mechanism, target, properties }]`.
- `animatedTarget(model, comp, call, path)` → the element a Web Animations call animates on a call path; an element created in code is named by the element it is inserted into.

### `lib/intake-inventory-calls.mjs`
- `callsOf(p, fn)` → the calls that run a function node; `callPaths(p, node, reads, depth)` → one `Map(function -> call)` per chain of callers, followed outward while a call's arguments read its own function's parameters; `resolveArg(p, n, path, depth)` → the expression a parameter stands for on a path; `insertedInto(p, el, path)` → the element a code-created element is inserted into, or null.

### `lib/intake-inventory-csstext.mjs`
- `cssTextProperties(p, n)` → the properties a `cssText` expression sets, as `{ property, to }` or `{ property, formula }`, or null when the expression's declarations do not name their properties in literal text.

### `lib/intake-inventory-dead.mjs`
- `constantReturn(p, node)` → true when an early return on a constant makes the node unreachable; `emptyLoop(p, node)` → true when the node sits in a loop over a list every write to leaves empty.

### `lib/intake-inventory-trigger.mjs`
- `triggerOf(model, comp, node, seen)` → `{ type, detail, state_keys?, delay?, via, members, called }`: the trigger a code path runs on, `called` false when the code sits in a member nothing calls, wires or references.

### `lib/intake-inventory-keys.mjs`
- `stateWriters(prog)` → Map(member → state keys written); `routeKeys(prog)`, `viewportKeys(prog)` → the keys a router or the viewport writes; `timeReads(prog)`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-css.mjs`
- `cssDecls` (re-exported), `ms(time)`, `splitTop(text, sep?)`, `parseTransition(value)`, `parseAnimation(value)` (the iteration count, `infinite` included, of a template style, a helmet rule or the quoted text of a style-object string), `cssBlocks(text)` → `[{ media, selector, decls }]`, `sameSelector(a, b)`, `reducedMotionMedia(media)`, `matchSelector(elements, selector)` → `{ tpl, rule? }`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-surfaces.mjs`
- `surfaceRecords(census, { model, anim, routing?, props? })` → `{ records, counts, unresolved, scIf, componentSurface, keySurfaces }`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-sample.mjs`
- `SAMPLE_WIDTHS`; `sampleTargets(records, meta)` → the interval and frame-loop J records with a template target; `sampleWriters({ folder, index, harPath, file?, hash?, targets, widths?, harness? })` → `{ samples: [{ recordId, width, t, values }], gaps }`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-diff.mjs`
- `norm(value)`, `pairKey(record)`, `routeKey(route)`, `scIfKey(key)`; `diffAnimations(ours, theirs)`, `diffSurfaces(ours, theirs)`, `diffInventory(ours, theirs)` → `[{ rowId, draftId, text, field, ours, theirs, why }]`; a row ID, and a record row's `ours` or `theirs`, is built from the pairing key, never a record ID, so inserting a record changes no other row.

### `lib/intake-props.mjs`
- `declaredProps(text)` → `{ props }` or `{ error }` from the `data-props` of `<script data-dc-script>`; `propVariants({ folder, census })` → `{ props: [{ component, prop, editor, options, default, mounted, variants, unused, unusedOptions, gates }], gaps }`.

### `lib/intake-review-carry.mjs`
- `withUnappliedMarks(previous, markdown, { day })` → `{ intake, carried, acceptAll }`: the previous intake with each unmarked row given its review.md mark, the rows carried, and the sections whose Accept all is ticked.
- `writeProposedPageMap({ pageMap, confirmed }, text)` → `{ written }`: a plain run writes the proposed `page-map.json` only while `page-map.confirmed.json` (beside `intake.json`) does not exist, so it never overwrites a confirmed map; `writeConfirmedPageMap(files, text, today?)` is `--apply-review`'s write of the confirmed map and that record.
- `earlierIds(row, movedFrom)` → every row ID a row's element had before re-runs moved it, earliest first. `carryOver(previousIntake, intake)` → `{ review, changed, realigned, refused }`: a mark follows its element, never its number: a skeleton row is paired with the previous row that is the same element (by fingerprint, the same proposed block), any other row with the previous row of the same ID, and a mark on a row whose element changed or is gone is listed in `refused`. `lib/intake-review.mjs` re-exports it.

### `lib/intake-review.mjs`
- `ROW_TYPES`, `SECTION_TITLES`; `MARKS` (Appendix C's mark values per row type; the first is what "Accept all" sets) and `markShapes`; `DEFAULTED` (the row types whose blank Mark cell takes the first value: flag, not-a-defect); `safeRowId(id)` (`|`, a backtick, CR and LF written as `%7C`, `%60`, `%0D`, `%0A`, so every row ID survives the table); `UNWIRED_NOTE` (the reason cell of every not-a-defect row); `markProblem(rowType, mark)` → why a mark is refused, or null.
- `HASH_FIELDS`, `VOLATILE`, `sha`, `contentHash(rowType, row, draftId)`: the content hash of exactly what a row shows.
- `reviewRows(intake)` → `[{ rowId, rowType, draftId, contentHash, shows, proposed, note, acceptAll, needs, cause?, causeReason? }]` (`needs` is why a row needs reading, ranked by `NEEDS_ORDER`; a seen-but-unsupported missing-feature row, `from: 'coverage'`, takes `acceptAll` `gap:<wave>`, or `difference` when its capability is excluded, and no mark when the coverage map does not hold it; `cause` is the standing rule or client-built post that settled a skeleton row); `byCause(skeletonRows)` → `[[heading, rows]]`: the rows needing a decision by need and proposed block, then one group per rule or built post headed by its reason, then the confident proposals (`CONFIDENT_HEADING`), never one group per page; `readingCounts(intake, changed?)` → rows needing reading against rows accept-all covers, per section; page-map rows are `page:<postType>:<slug>`, notices `notice:prose:<file>:<line>` and `notice:host:<host>`.
- `renderReview(intake, { changed? })` → the `review.md` text (changed rows from `intake.review` when no list is passed); `parseReview(markdown)` → `{ sections, rowMarks, rowSection, problems }`.
- `applyReview(intake, markdown, { today })` → `{ intake, problems, missing }`; `isoDay(date)`. `carryOver(previousIntake, intake)` → `{ review, changed, realigned, refused }`: a skeleton mark whose row id now names another element, or is gone, follows its element by fingerprint (same proposed block) or is listed in `refused`.
- `unwiredRows(intake)` → the `notDefects` entries of the unwired dead-code kind as `not-a-defect` rows (`not-defect:<censusId>`, default mark `not-a-defect`); `notDefectLine(intake)` → the header line counting every other not-a-defect entry per reason (FR-48-34).

### `lib/intake-ast-eval.mjs`
- `ref(node, env)` → `{ node, env }`, a node paired with its loop environment; `returnsOf(idx, fn)` → a function's returned nodes, `propRefs(idx, node, key)` → where a property's value comes from.
- `resolveRefs(idx, node)` and `elementsOf(idx, node)` → the data a value or a loop iterates over; `staticTruth(idx, node, env)` → true, false or unknown for a simple filter condition.
- `evalDeep(idx, node, env?)` → `evalStr`'s result with loop variables followed; `loopEnvs(idx, node)` → one environment per loop item; `oneString(result)` → the single string or null.
- `trackWrites(idx)` → the same index with `writes` (values `o.k = v`, `o[k] = v`, push, unshift, splice and `Object.assign` add to an object or array literal the resolver reaches, which `propRefs`, `elementsOf` and computed reads then include) and `unknownWrites` (writes whose target it cannot reach); an index never passed through it reads only what each literal declares.

### `lib/intake-pagemap.mjs`
- `PAGE_MAP_SCHEMA`; `classifyRoute(text, { prices })` → `{ postType, template, collection, hint }` from a route's words: a product category, a single product, a post or a page; `hint` is the shop, cart or checkout its words name (never taken on its own).
- `proposePageMap({ folder, routing?, census?, surfacesHint? })` → `{ rows, linkMap: [{ from, href, kind: row|external|control|gap, slug?, error? }], gaps, routeSlug, menuItems: [{ menu, region, file, items: [{ label, targets, position, parentItem, children }] }] }` (`menuItems` are the draft's own link text and hrefs behind every menu entry; `surfacesHint` defaults to the export's `surfaces.json`; `null` skips the cross-check).
- `pageMapProblems(rows, ajv?)` → schema problems, duplicate slugs and parents naming no row.
- `drawerRepeatsHeader(menu, megaLabels)` → true for a drawer-region draft menu whose top-level items are each a header mega item's label.

### `lib/intake-pagemap-owners.mjs`
- `ownedSurfaces(surfaces, rows, endpoints, routeSlug)` → `{ rows, parts: [{ row, part }] }`: the surfaces that become rows, and each other surface as a part of its owning row (null for the site chrome).
- `wooSlugs(rows, accountPages)`, `shopTitle(row, label)`, `wooPages(rows, surfaces, used)`: WooCommerce's slugs, the shop row's menu label, and the cart, checkout and my-account rows a shop client always has; `checkoutEndpoints(rows, route, pageOfTag, home, routeSlug, facts)` → the routes reached only from checkout that the checkout's submit leads to or that show an order reference (`facts` from `commerceFacts`), removed from the rows.

### `lib/intake-pagemap-commerce.mjs`
- `commerceFacts(exp, routeKeys, links)` → Map( route → { payment, orderLines, password, listing, reaches, submits, orderRef } ): what each routed page shows, read from its template and logic.
- `commerceKinds(rows, facts, gaps)`: gives each route row its shop, cart or checkout shape from the manifest's declaration (`row.declared`) or the structure; a word hint (`row.hint`) the structure does not bear out is a gap row. `declaredKind(type, kind)` → `'shop' | 'cart' | 'checkout' | null` from a manifest page's `wp.type` and `kind`.

### `lib/intake-pagemap-hints.mjs`
- `crossCheck(code, hint)` → `{ surfaces, gaps }`: the code surfaces with their `surfaces.json` pairings (by kind, a hint modal or form with a code choice flow, place and name), then the hint-only records, and one `surfaces-disagree` gap row per surface one side lacks.

### `lib/intake-pagemap-regen.mjs`
- `runPageMap(o, { log })` → exit code: `intake.mjs --page-map`, writing nothing when the regenerated map (or the intake record holding it) does not validate; it also puts the proposal into intake.json's `pageMap` and renders review.md again (`syncIntake`), so `--apply-review` confirms the regenerated map.
- `syncIntake(intake, proposal, { reviewText, day })` → `{ record, problems, refused }`: the saved intake record with `proposal` (the regenerated rows before marks and ids) as its page map and its review rows carried as a re-run carries them (review.md's unapplied marks, then every mark whose row is unchanged); `{ record: null }` for no record or a palette-only one.
- `pageMapMarks(intake)` → Map( rowId → mark ) of the page-map review rows; `applyMarks(rows, marks)` → `{ rows, applied, unapplied, refused, lost }` (`drop`, `edit:<field>=<value>` for an `EDITABLE` field; an empty or `null` parent or template clears it; `refused` lists edits of other fields, `lost` each dropped row's parts). `lostIds(current, rows, marks)` → the current rows holding an id the new map loses, except those dropped.
- `carryIds(rows, current)` → the rows with the current map's ids by row key; `mapChanges(before, after)` → `added <key>`, `removed <key>` and `<key>: <field> <before> -> <after>` lines.

### `lib/intake-pagemap-surfaces.mjs`
- `codeSurfaces(exp, routeOf, routeKeys?, links?)` → `{ surfaces, menus, formComponents, accountPages, pageOfTag }`: the CPT surfaces found in the code (each with `page`, an overlay also with `nav`, `orderLines` and `reaches`, the route keys its own links lead to), their menus, the route keys whose pages hold a password field, and `pageOfTag(draftId)` → the page holding an element id. `isChoiceFlow(node)` → two or more pick lists (no tabs or disclosures) and no `<form>`. `showsOrderLines(text, L, node)` → a loop inside the element binds a currency amount. `pageOf(d, node, routeKeys)` → the route whose page holds a template node. `KIND_POST_TYPE`: surface kind to post type.
- `elementTree`, `innerText`, `descendants`, `ancestors`, `kebab`, `stem`: template tree and naming helpers.
- `isChoiceFlow(node, isFilter?)` → whether an element is a choice flow: two or more pick lists that are no filter (`isFilter(list)`, the shared `narrowsRenderedList` rule) and no `<form>`.

### `lib/intake-pagemap-mega-slots.mjs`
- `megaSlots({ menu, openers, anchorOf, itemOf, newMenu })` → Map(opener → `{ menu, position }`): inserts each opener the header menu lacks at its document place (`itemOf`), makes a menu with `newMenu()` when the header has none, renumbers the items; a null opener (a panel with no single opener) gets no slot.

### `lib/site-build-menus-step.mjs`
- `supersededItems(rows, state, linkText?, surfaces?)` → `item-delete` actions; `menusStep(plan, superseded?)` → `{ actions, options, unresolved }`; `runMenus({ planFor, apply, wp, applyPhase, log?, error? })` → exit code.

### `lib/intake-spec-sync.mjs`
- `SPEC_FILE`, `DATA_DIR`: the spec and the route's `data/` folder. `LABELS`: each Appendix C label with its schema file and shape (`body`, or `items` for the page-map array).
- `specBlock(specText, label)` → the parsed JSON block after `**<label>:**`; throws naming the label when there is none.
- `constructKinds(specText)` → the construct kinds Appendix C lists for the registry.
- `syncProblems(specText, label, schema)` → each disagreement between the spec block and the schema file (wrapper keys ignored), prefixed with the label.
- `intakeProblems(specText, schema)` → each intake-record key missing from the schema's `required` or `properties`, and each extra required key.

### `lib/motion-schema.mjs`
- `REPO`, `MOTION_SCHEMA_FILE`: the repo root and the schema path. `loadMotionSchema(file?)` → the parsed schema. `annotationKeywords(schema)` → every `x-sgs-*` keyword the schema uses (registered with ajv).
- `RANGE_UNITS`, `RELATIVE_UNITS`: the base-unit conversions (ms/s, px, deg/turn/rad) and the units a ranged field refuses. `rangeProblem(range, value)` → the error text for a value outside an `x-sgs-range`, or null (tokens and `calc()` are not range-checked).
- `motionValidator(schema?)` → the compiled ajv validator (cached per schema). `formatError(e)` → one ajv error as `path: message`. `schemaErrors(doc, schema?)` → every schema error. `checkMotion(doc, schema?)` → `{ valid, errors, refusals }`.
- `enumerateFields(schema?)` → every field with its `x-sgs-field` annotation (panel, field type, CSS property, state, per-device), the list the DB motion-field rows are seeded from. Re-exports `motionRefusals`, `elementRefusals` and `elementLayers`.

### `lib/motion-validate.mjs`
- `SELECTOR_MAX_LENGTH`, `SELECTOR_ALLOWED`, `SELECTOR_DENIED`; `selectorProblem(selector)` → the reason a custom-target selector is not scoped to the block root, or null.
- `customTargetErrors(doc)` → the custom-target rule failures; `validateMotionWrite(doc, schema)` → `{ valid, errors, refusals }` (the schema's errors and refusals plus the custom-target errors); `assertMotionWritable(doc, schema)` throws one Error listing every problem and returns the document when it is writable.
- `verdictProblems(verdict)` → every problem of a verdict as text: its errors, then each refusal's text (a refusal is a string or `{ id, text, layers }`); `lib/fill-motion.mjs::writeNode`, `lib/fill-behaviour.mjs` and `assertMotionWritable` print it.

### `lib/motion-refusals.mjs`
- `elementLayers(element)` → `[{ list, path, effect, layer }]` in paint order. `layerLabel(schema, entry)` → the layer's display label. `slotClaims(schema, entry)` → the paint slots a layer claims. `matchedValues(entry, m)` → the rule side's value paths this layer sets (in values or a device override; with `nonZero`, a zero amount is unset). `sideLabel(schema, entry, m)` → the label a refusal names the layer by for that side (a `valueLabels` label when every matched value maps to one). `matches(schema, entry, m)` → whether a layer meets one side of a refusal rule.
- `elementRefusals(schema, element)` → the refusal texts for one element. `motionRefusals(schema, doc)` → `{ <target>: [texts] }` for every element of a document.

### `handoff-repackage.mjs`
- `parseArgs(argv)` → `{ folder, project, entries, generated, dryRun }` or `{ error }`. `main(argv, log?)` → the exit code.
- `readOldManifest(text)` → `{ rows: [{ path, bytes, sha256 }], runtime }` from an old `MANIFEST.md`.
- `proveUnchanged(root)` → `{ problems, notes }`: a problem is a file changed since export or a folder that cannot be proven unchanged.
- `repackage(root, { project, entries, generated, dryRun?, schemas? })` → `{ problems, notes, actions }`; writes nothing when `problems` is non-empty or `dryRun` is set.

### `lint.mjs`
- `routeFiles(root)` → every route file, relative.
- `exportsOf(src)` → exported names.
- `lintFolder(root)` → problems (README index, scripts imports, a file naming the plugin's `node_modules`).
- `lintTree(tree, db, label?)` → problems (core style, native_wp).
- `lintSkeleton(tree, db, label?)` → problems (style values; and a brand `sgs/icon` that types its own brand's address in `linkUrl` with no `metadata.bindings.linkUrl`).
- `registerIds(markdown)` → the item ids a fix register holds (table first cells, comma-split, and ids leading a bullet).
- `HOUSE_RULES`: ledger rules that are house rules (`touch-target`, `accessibility`): their entries cite no register item.
- `lintLedger(entries, ids, label?)` → problems: an entry (not a house rule) whose `register` ids are missing or not in `ids`.
- `lintSurfaceLedger(surfacesFile, registerFile?, repo?)` → `lintLedger` on `<build>/qa/divergences.json` against `registerFile`, else the register `qa/ledger.config.json` names (from the repo root); a ledger with entries and no register, or a missing register file, is a problem.

### `lib/fill-behaviour.mjs`
- `declaredAttr`: Whether the framework DB declares the attribute on the block (read-only). `db` is the handle the caller already holds (the default opens the framework DB once). A DB that cannot be read declares nothing: the write is refused, never assumed.
- `hasAttrOf`: The attribute check of a framework DB handle, the shape `hasAttr( block, attr )` the writers take.
- `behaviourIndex`: The recognised behaviours of the records, keyed for placement: those a record names by template element are found through `recordsByElement`; those a script finds by a `[data-*]` hook (no template element of their own) are held by the elements that carry the hook. Returns { hosted: [ { r, b } ], hostsOf(key) } where hostsOf gives the hosted ones for a template element key.
- `stripKeysOf`: The template elements that scroll away above a pinned header: when the draft pins one element (a sticky header that also shadows on scroll), its earlier siblings that are not pinned themselves scroll off the page while the bar stays, as do their descendants. A Set of template keys.
- `applySettings`: Writes a node's block settings from its settings behaviours (the behaviours whose block declares each attribute, a framework DB query); `colour` values equal to a palette colour are written by their slug. A behaviour no settings of this node's block belong to is reported. Returns the items it wrote or reported.
- `applyStrip`: The header row that scrolls away above the pinned header bar: `rowHideOnScroll` on a `sgs/site-header-row` whose element is one of the strip keys, and `rowAnimate` off where the block declares it and `stillAtRest` holds.
- `stillAtRest`: The resting transition-duration of a target at every width it renders at (the code's declaration, else the rendered value) when every entry is 0 and no state look in `reads.states` declares or renders a non-zero transition or animation duration; a width the finder found nothing visible at is skipped, while an unread width, a failed declared read or no rendering width gives null.
- `place`: Puts a finished layer into the document where writeEffect placed it. An element's effect list holds each effect once (the motion schema's unique-effect limit), so a layer whose effect the list already holds is not added: the layer already there stands, and the return names it with the fields where the second layer differs ({ placed: false, layer, differs }); otherwise { placed: true, layer }. `lib/fill-motion.mjs::motionWrites` and `applyBehaviours` both place through it.
- `sharedLayerRow`: The row or note for a layer `place` did not add: the same values are one layer shared by both records (a note); different values are a named row, because the element holds the effect once and the first record's values stand.
- `applyBehaviours`: One node target's behaviours. `items`: [ { r, b } ]; `finish( effect, id )` writes and checks one effect (writeEffect, preset, token snapping); `written` maps a record id to where its layer went (a control's target); `controls` collects the controls for the second pass. Pushes to out.writes, out.unmapped, out.notes and out.site.
- `applyGlobals`: The behaviours that belong to no element: the draft's route fade (a site option) and the ones SGS already does (a scroll reset on a route change, the page scroll lock of a modal drawer, a back-to-top press). Each is reported once.
- `draftCssOf`: The draft's CSS text for the pointer-tracked reveal's consumer check: the draft file(s), which hold their styles inline (`style` attributes and `<style>` blocks). [] when the draft is served from a URL.
- `applyControls`: The second pass: each control sets values on the layer of the record it controls (the marquee's hover rate). The merged layer is validated on its own before it is kept, so a control value the schema refuses names the control instead of failing the whole node's document; the row carries the controlled record's node (where its layer, or its own row, sits).

### `lib/fill-current-item.mjs`
- `CURRENT_PATH`: The loop variable's property names a draft uses for "this item is the current route".
- `currentItemGroups`: The current-item groups of a fact record: [{ ifKey, itemKey, markKey, component, itemN, markN, path }]. Only an sc-if inside an sc-for, whose single hole is a current-like path, answered both true and false across the loop, with an element child.
- `groupOfJob`: The group a read job (a target with a `{ tpl: '<chain>/<n>#<copy>' }` finder) is the item of, or undefined.
- `currentSelectors`: The two selectors of a group: the copy where the condition holds and a copy where it does not.
- `readCurrentItems`: Reads the current-item looks at the page's current width: { looks: { [jobId]: [look] }, gaps: [{ ids, reason }] }. groups: currentItemGroups(facts); jobs: lib/fill-read.mjs::jobsFor. The comparison copy is read here, so the look is the current copy against a non-current copy of the same item, never against a target that may itself be the current copy.

### `lib/fill-form-schema.mjs`
- `formSchemaOf`: The form schema the fact record holds for a component: { component, entry, steps } or null. A component whose statics hold no array of steps with named fields has none.
- `schemaFieldOf`: The schema field a rendered control is: its id or name ends with the field's name (`ta-tradingName` is `tradingName`).
- `schemaChipsOf`: The schema field a group of choice chips is: the choice field whose options are exactly the chips' labels.
- `conditionsFromSource`: The conditions in a component's script: { <field name>: { field, op, value } \| { reason } }. The script's tests are read as `const x = ['a', 'b'].includes(v.f)` (an either-or list), `const x = v.f === 'a'` (equals) and `const x = v.f === false` (an unticked box); a field's `show: x` (or `show: !x`) names the test it waits on. One value is a condition the form blocks hold (`conditionalField`, `conditionalOperator`, `conditionalValue`); a list is not.
- `linkParams`: The query-string names the draft's links hand to a page (`?team=Sales&role=Driver`): every `href` in the fact record's templates, static or a hole's literal part.
- `savesProgress`: Whether the draft keeps a form's answers in the browser: the intake flags (lib/intake-flags.mjs) name a `localStorage` write in the component's logic.
- `requiredCopyOf`: The validation copy in a component's script, read from its syntax: per-field `if (!v.name) errs.name = '…'` lines and the shared per-type `f.req` line. Returns { fields: { <name>: { required?, tooLong? } }, byType }.
- `copyFor`: The copy one rendered control shows: { required?, tooLong? }; a schema field the draft does not require gets none.
- `schemaFieldByName`: The schema field whose name is `name`: { field, step }, or null when no field of the schema has that name.

### `lib/fill-form.mjs`
- `isFormFieldBlock(slug)` → whether a block is a form field (`sgs/form-field-*`), the blocks `formFieldWrites` fills.
- `FORM_FIELD_SETTINGS` → the settings `formFieldWrites` can write on a form field block.
- `FORM_BLOCK_SETTINGS` → the settings Fill's form pass writes on `sgs/button` (the submit flag), `sgs/form`, `sgs/form-step` and `sgs/form-review`.
- `formSettings(block)` → the settings Fill's form pass writes for a block (`lib/content-coverage.mjs` claims these for the role-writer route).
- `attrDef`: The attribute definition of a block by framework-DB query: { default } or null when the block holds no such attribute.
- `formFieldWrites`: The writes, UNMAPPED rows and notes for one field block. block: its slug; attributes: what the node holds; el: the draft control's `snap.form`; field: the schema field it is ({ field, step }) or null; param: the link parameter names of the draft; condition: { field, op, value } \| { reason } \| null.
- `formReadback`: Reads a written value back against the draft fact it came from; returns the problems (empty when they agree).
- `formWrites`: Fill's form pass. nodes/flat/refOf: the skeleton nodes, their tree nodes and the ref function; reads: readDraft's result; facts: the fact record (or null); flags: intake's flags; db: the framework DB; source: { component: script text } for the draft's conditions ({ component: script text }, see formSources); component: the surface's own component, whose schema is used (none named reads the first schema in the record); idxFor( component ): the parsed logic of a draft component (`lib/fill-list-content.mjs::logicReader`), from which a multi-step form's button words are read (`lib/fill-form-buttons.mjs`); buttonRules: `rules.formSteps` (read from the decisions file when omitted). A linked reference block (`formIsLinked`) and the blocks inside it are never written. Returns { writes, unmapped, notes, settled }: `settled` is one row (reason `row-held`, property `(linked form)`) for each node on or inside a linked reference block that the draft read, naming the linked form.
- `componentOf`: The component whose template a control comes from: the innermost import in the chain of its `tpl` finder that is one of the candidates (the script that validates it), or null when the finder names no template.
- `formSources`: The scripts of the draft's components that hold a form schema or required-field copy: { component: text } read from `<folder>/<component>.dc.html`; a named `component` reads that component's schema's script, none the first schema's.
- `schemaControl`: The control a schema field stands for when the page never rendered it, in the shape of the draft read's `snap.form`: its name, its autocomplete token and its options as the block takes them (choices for a block holding `tiles`, else options whose value and label are the draft's option).

### `lib/fill-form-buttons.mjs`
FR-48-57: the words of a multi-step form's own buttons. The draft's Next and Back buttons the skeleton absorbed (the form component's buttons outside any loop that `navRole` names) give the `sgs/form` node's `nextLabel`, `submitLabel` and `prevLabel`; `formWrites` calls it on a form node that holds `sgs/form-step` children.
- `stepRules`: `rules.formSteps` of `data/skeleton-decisions.json`.
- `trueOnLast`: Whether a test holds on the last step: true or false for a comparison with `<list>.length - 1` (`===`, `==`, `>=`, `<=`, `<`, `>`, `!==`, `!=`, either way round, through names and `!`), else null.
- `holeWords`: The words one hole path shows, followed through the object `renderVals` returns: `{ plain }`, `{ rest, last }` (a value chosen by a step test: the last step's and the others') or `{ reason }`.
- `buttonWords`: The words one button shows: its own text, or the one text hole the fact record reads as a string (an element hole, an icon, is no words); static text beside a hole, several holes or an unread hole is a `{ reason }`; no words is null.
- `stepButtonLabels`: `{ labels, notes }`: the Next button's words give `nextLabel` (and `submitLabel` from its last-step words), the Back button's one set of words `prevLabel`; copies that disagree, unreadable words and a Back button that changes words by step write nothing and are notes.
- `stepButtonWrites`: The writes for the form node: each label that differs from what the node reads as now (its value, else the block default); a label the block holds no setting for is a named UNMAPPED row.

### `lib/fill-icon.mjs`
- `ICON_SETTINGS` → the settings `iconDecisions` can write: `iconSource`, `brandName`, `iconName`, `iconSvg`, `iconFill`, `iconPosition`.
- `iconSettings(schema)` → those of them the block declares, or `[]` for a block with no `iconSvg`; the guard `iconDecisions` writes by.
- `KSES_FILE`: the plugin's `includes/helpers-svg-kses.php`, whose allowlist the writer parses.
- `LUCIDE_FILE`: the plugin's `includes/lucide-icons.php`, the Lucide glyphs a stroke mark is matched against.
