- `BRAND_FILE`: the plugin's `data/brand-registry.json`, the brand glyphs a mark is matched against.
- `allowedSvg(file)` → the sanitiser's allowed tags and attributes `{ tag: Set(attribute) }`, parsed from the PHP source so the writer keeps exactly what the page keeps.
- `signature`: The shape signature of a mark: each drawing element's tag and geometry, so the same drawing matches however it is painted.
- `lucideSignatures`: { signature: icon name } for every Lucide icon the block ships, read from includes/lucide-icons.php.
- `brandSignatures`: { signature: brand slug } for each registry brand whose glyph is its own SVG, and { slug: signature } the other way round.
- `markKind`: The kind of mark: 'stroke' (every drawing element is stroked and unfilled), 'filled' (none is stroked, every one is filled) or 'mixed'; and whether it is drawn the way Lucide draws (stroke width 2, round caps and joins).
- `iconDecisions`: svg: the element's read ({ markup, position }) or null. schema: the block's settings. attributes: what the node already holds. href: the address the mark links to in the draft (or ''), the evidence a Site Info brand row is that brand.

### `lib/fill-logo-size.mjs`
- `logoSizeSettings`: { width, max, unit } setting names a block has for a logo's size, or null.
- `logoSizeWrites({ nodes, flat, refOf, reads, db, unmapped })` → `{ writes, unmapped, notes }`: a logo whose one width setting cannot hold the draft's per-width sizes (the coded gap row) is written as its per-device size setting instead.

### `lib/fill-dimensions.mjs`
- `SIZE_FAMILY`: the size properties (height, width, min-*, max-*) with their kind (exact, min, max) and axis; the logical forms (block-size, inline-size) map onto them.
- `physical`: the physical name of a size property (`min-block-size` is `min-height`).
- `sizeOf`: { kind, axis } of a size property, or null.
- `MIN_TARGET`: 24: the px an interactive element's written height or width never goes below (WCAG 2.2 AA 2.5.8).
- `dimensionWrites({ nodes, flat, refOf, reads, db, unmapped, calFor, snapshot, scope, entries, date })` → `{ writes, unmapped, notes, ledger }`: a block's floor or ceiling the draft does not declare is written at the draft's rendered size, a declared size in a dimension with no setting goes to the block's minimum when the content fits (else the row stays with the reason), and an interactive size under 24px is written as 24px with a `touch-target` ledger entry.
- `contentExtent({ nodes, reads }, rec, target, axis, w)` → the content extent inside an element along an axis at a width, or null. `NATURAL_SIZE`: the child width (`auto`) that holds a non-shrinking item at its natural size under `fixed` child sizing.

### `lib/fill-regap.mjs`
- `TIER_OF`: the tier name of each read width.
- `rowTarget`: the skeleton node and target an unmapped row names, or null.
- `rowValues`: a row's per-width values; a width the row omits takes the draft's rendered value there.
- `rectAt`: the draft's rendered box of a target at a width, or null.
- `reresolve`: runs the generic resolver (lib/fill-prop.mjs::resolveProperty) on a changed per-width draft for a gap row's element, with no baseline.
- `commit`: writes a resolver result into the node and records each write.
- `perNode(rows)`: a row UNMAPPED raises once for several nodes (`nodes`, `lib/fill-row-lift.mjs::liftChildren`) as one row per node, so a pass reads and writes each node it names.
- `regroup(rows)`: joins the rows a pass leaves back into one row per look and reason.

### `lib/fill-map.mjs`
- `zoomOf`: The zoom a Google Maps embed address asks for (`z=15`, or `zoom=15`), a whole number from 1 to 20; null when it names none.
- `mapDecisions`: embeds: { width: { src, filter, h } } the iframe the draft paints at each read width (lib/fill-read.mjs `embed`; a width the element is not shown at is absent). schema: the block's settings. attributes: what the node already holds.

### `lib/fill-motion-account.mjs`
- `accountedIds`: The record ids a finished run has already accounted for: the layers it wrote (`used`), the layers a control or behaviour placed (`written`), and every record id an UNMAPPED row, a note (`<id>: ...`), a write's property or a site option names.
- `makeHomeOf`: Where a record sits on the surface: `homeOf( record )` is { n (the skeleton node), slot } or null for a record whose element is not a target. nodes: skeletonNodes(); keyOf( finder ): the inventory key of a target's element; idx: lib/fill-behaviour.mjs::behaviourIndex.
- `silentRows`: The UNMAPPED rows for the on-surface records nothing accounted for (see makeHomeOf); refOf( node ): the node's ref.
- `READING_PROGRESS_SETTING`: The page's reading progress bar: a record the effect recogniser reads as a page-scroll progress bar whose element is not on the surface (the skeleton removes the thin fixed bar: rules.readingProgress). Nothing in the tree can hold it; the Floating UI reading progress setting does, so the handover names the setting and the record for site build.
- `readingProgressHandover({ found, homeOf })` → a handover row naming `READING_PROGRESS_SETTING` for each page scroll-progress record no surface holds.
- `motionWidths(n, doc, reads)` → the read widths a node's sgsMotion write answers: the widths its root element was read at, where the document gives the root a layer state of its own, else none.

### `lib/fill-preset.mjs`
- `PRESET_KEYS`: the looks compared against a button preset variant: `[ key, css properties, state ]` per row.
- `presetBlocks`: The block settings that hold a preset variant, by framework-DB query: { block: attr } for every block with an `inheritStyle` setting whose values include the preset slugs.
- `matchPreset`: The preset variant a target matches at every read width, or null. presets: settings.custom.buttonPresets.
- `presetWrites`: Writes the matched variant onto every preset block whose draft element matches, and drops the per-instance values the resolver wrote for the properties the variant sets. nodes: skeletonNodes(); flat: tree nodes by index; refOf: tree node to ref; reads: readDraft's result; rawSnapshot/snapshot: the client's theme snapshot (raw, normalised); writes: Fill's writes. Returns { writes (the list without the dropped ones, plus the variant writes), notes }.
- `TRANSFORM_CHANNELS`: the hover-state fields that paint the CSS `transform`. `presetVariants(db, presets)` → `{ block: { attr, variants } }` for every block with an `inheritStyle` setting, its variants the snapshot's preset names that setting's values include. `dropPresetMotion({ lifted, flat, writes })` → removes the hover transform channels from the per-instance motion of every node whose painted preset variant holds the draft's hover transform, so the preset is the one writer of the lift.

### `lib/fill-safe-area.mjs`
- `safeAreaSides`: The sides a declaration asks the safe-area inset for.
- `fixedPart`: The value of a declaration with every safe-area inset taken as 0px, when it is a sum or a maximum of px terms (`calc(16px + env(...))`, `max(16px, env(...))`, `env(...)` alone); null for a form it does not evaluate.
- `safeAreaRead`: declared: the draft's declared text for one property; rendered: what the draft paints (a px length).

### `lib/fill-scrollbar.mjs`
- `splitScrollbarColour`: A computed `scrollbar-color` ("rgba(231, 215, 104, 0.75) rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)") as { thumb, track } colours, or null for `auto`.
- `isDark`: Whether a ground colour is dark (relative luminance under 0.4); a transparent ground is not a ground.
- `scrollbarTokens`: items: [{ from: { node, block, slot, index }, color, width, ground, thumbPart, trackPart }] read from the draft's scrollers. snapshot: lib/normalise.mjs::loadSnapshot. site: the client's current settings.custom.scrollbar (or undefined).
- `scrollbarPatch`: The scrollbar tokens of a whole surface: the scrollers read at the widest width, once each, as a patch of the client's theme-snapshot.json. Returns { patch, entries, notes, unmapped }.
- `mergePatch`: Two snapshot patches as one (the keys are disjoint paths of the same object).

### `lib/fill-state-defect.mjs`
- `droppedStateDecls`: The defect rows of intake's `draftDefects` (groups hold `rows`) that name a dropped state declaration: [{ draftId, pseudo, prop, declared }].
- `holeValue`: The single string a hole resolved to at one width for one copy of its element, or { gap }. `answer` is the fact record's `{ v: [ summary ], n, loop }`; a loop's list holds one summary per copy, in order.
- `addDefectLooks`: Adds the code looks of every dropped declaration to `reads.states[width].looks[jobId]` (in place) and returns { listed, gaps }. reads: lib/fill-read.mjs::readDraft's result; jobs: [{ id, finder }]; facts: the fact record; draftDefects: intake.json's; rootComponent: the entry draft's component (lib/fill-confirm.mjs::draftIdOf). A look already read for the same state and property at a width (the page did paint it) is kept as read.

### `lib/draft-defects.mjs`
- `OWNER_DEFAULT`, `FIELDS` (every field an entry holds); `defectRows(draftDefects)` → the defect rows of intake's `draftDefects`; `defectKey(row)` → the key a defect keeps across re-runs.
- `buildDraftDefects({ draftDefects, listed, owner, date })` → the list entries for one intake, `listed` being Fill's `addDefectLooks` rows (what the code intent was built as); `mergeDraftDefects(fresh, earlier)` → the list a re-run writes, each entry keeping the dates and owner of the earlier one; `validateDraftDefects(list)` → why a list is invalid, as strings.

### `lib/draft-defects-file.mjs`
- `defectsPath(client, repo)` → the file path; `draftDefectsFile({ client, repo, owner, listed, date })` → `{ client, takenOn, defects }`; `writeDraftDefects(file, { client, repo })`.
- `requestText(file)` → the request text for entries not yet sent, grouped by owner; `markSent(file, date)` → the file with `date` stamped on every entry that has no sent date.

### `lib/fill-width-gate.mjs`
- `TIERS`: the device tier boundaries, 768 and 1024.
- `SNAP_TOLERANCE`: 5%: a width threshold this close to a tier snaps to it, with a `breakpoint-snap` ledger entry.
- `HIDE_ATTR`: the conditional-visibility settings per tier (`sgsHideOnMobile`, `sgsHideOnTablet`, `sgsHideOnDesktop`); each is checked against the block in the framework DB before it is written.
- `TIER_RANGES`: The width range each tier covers: [ start, end ).
- `holds`: Whether a width condition holds (shows its content) at width `w`.
- `shownRanges`: The ranges [ lo, hi ) of widths from 0 at which a condition shows its content, merged.
- `intersect`: The overlap of two lists of ranges.
- `complement`: The complement of a list of ranges within [ 0, Infinity ).
- `snapThreshold`: FR-48-50 step 1: the tier a threshold snaps to, with its distance in px, or null (no tier within 5% of the tier, or the threshold is the tier). The nearest tier wins.
- `shownAt`: The widths a template element is shown at, from the width conditions of its enclosing sc-ifs: { shown, via } or null when no enclosing sc-if is gated by width.
- `hiddenRanges`: The snapped hidden ranges of a shown list: { hidden: [ [ lo, hi ] ], snaps: [ { draft, tier, distance } ] }.
- `mediaRule`: The scoped @media rule hiding a range (width in whole px; the rule `&` is the instance, includes/custom-css.php).
- `collapseAttributes`: The setting each block draws its own alternative below (the nav's burger under its collapse point), as the skeleton rules name it: data/skeleton-decisions.json `rules.navigation.collapseAttribute`, { block: attr }.
- `ownBreakpoint`: The numeric collapse setting a block holds, by framework-DB query: the attribute the skeleton rules name for the block, when the block holds it as a number; null for any other block, whatever other breakpoint-like numbers it holds.
- `tierRanges`: the width ranges of the tiers a list of read widths (375, 768, 1440) stands for, merged.
- `maskHiddenVariants`: a width-variant instance's reads at the widths it is hidden at become missing, before any pass, so two instances measured on one draft element never stand side by side as two children. `shownOfNode`: the widths a skeleton node's content is shown at: its enclosing sc-if's range, or for a width-variant instance (`widthShown` on the variant, `widthVariants` on the default instance) the range of its own element, the default instance being shown where no variant is.
- `scopedCssPuts`: the `sgsCustomCss`, scope key and label writes that append a scoped rule to a node; empty when the node holds the rule already. The one writer of a scoped rule.
- `snapEntry`: the `breakpoint-snap` ledger entry of one snapped threshold, numbered after the entries held.
- `widthGateWrites`: Writes the width gates of every skeleton node. nodes: skeletonNodes(); flat: tree nodes by index; refOf: tree node to ref; facts: the fact record (null: nothing written); db: the framework DB; scope: the surface; entries: the ledger's entries (the snap entries continue its numbering). Returns { writes, unmapped, notes, ledger } in the shapes lib/fill-resolve.mjs uses; `ledger` holds the new snap entries.
- `unite(a, b)` → the union of two lists of ranges. `placedChildWidening(nodes, facts, rootComponent)` → the width gate of every gated skeleton node as `Map( tree index -> { shown, via } )`, a wrapper's shown range widened to hold every block placed in it (`lib/skeleton-part-slots.mjs::placePart`), and a child that relied on the wrapper's gate keeping it.

### `lib/fill-width-ladder.mjs`
- `isLadderRow`: whether an unmapped row is a one-value-every-width gap, a value the setting's enum does not hold, a keyword a per-device setting cannot take, or a property whose setting paints another element of the block (never a size property).
- `effectOf`: what a value does in its element's context, as one canonical word (`normal` justify-content in a flex container is the start value; `space-between` with one visible child is too).
- `widthLadderWrites({ nodes, flat, refOf, reads, db, unmapped, calFor, snapshot, scope, entries, date })` → `{ writes, unmapped, notes, ledger }`: a per-width difference no single setting holds is written as an equal-in-effect value, a tier-shaped sibling, the block's own breakpoint setting, or a scoped `@media` rule, in that order.
- `writtenTarget(rec, target, slot, notes)`: the skeleton target of the element the ladder writes: the row's own, or the node's target at that slot; with no target at that slot, the row's own and a note.
- `valuesAt(row, reads, target, home)`: the per-width values for the written element: the row's values when the row is on it or is a state row; otherwise that element's rendered values at the widths it is read at, never mixed with the row element's, keeping a row value declared in a form the computed read loses (`DECLARED_FORM`).

### `lib/fill-step-rungs.mjs`
- `stepStretches(prop, series, tierValue)` → `[ { range: [lo, hi), value } ]`: the runs of the 16px sweep where the draft's value differs from the tier read that stands for that width (`tierValue('mobile' | 'tablet' | 'desktop')`) and touches a real step (a fluid ramp is not one).
- `stepRungWrites({ nodes, flat, refOf, reads, writes, scope, entries, date })` → `{ writes, notes, ledger }`: for every written per-device or single-value property whose sweep steps between the read widths, a scoped `@media` rule on the instance in Additional CSS at the draft's threshold (snapped to a tier within 5%, with a `breakpoint-snap` ledger entry); the nav item padding `w < 1200 ? 10px : 15px` is held from 1024 to 1199.
- `siblingSlot(slot)` → the slot with its sibling position (`:nth-of-type`) removed, so the copies of a repeated element share one key.

### `lib/loop-body.mjs`
- `loopKeyOf`: The innermost `sc-for` a template element sits in, or null.
- `staticIn`: Whether a property is the same in every copy of a template element: no style hole for it (or for a shorthand it belongs to) answers with more than one distinct value across the loop's copies.
- `loopBodyCheck`: The callback for the hop. facts: the fact record; identityOf( ancestor, path ) -> the template key (`<component>/<n>`) of the draft element behind a measured path, or null when the runtime names none (the origin map, lib/fill-skeleton.mjs::originMap).
- `identityFromOrigin`: identityOf for loopBodyCheck from the surface's origin map ({ [cr-ref]: { tpl } }, lib/fill-skeleton.mjs::originMap): the measured path of an ancestor came from the descendant ref the caller recorded in `ancestor.measuredRefs`.

### `lib/motion-behaviours.mjs`
- `withEnds`: A property that states its values only in its formula, as a choice between two literals (`cond ? 'a' : 'b'`, the shape intake's re-derived inventory writes), with `to` the first and `from` the second; a stated `from` or `to` is kept.
- `recogniseBehaviour(record, ctx)` → null for a record that is not a script-driven behaviour, else `{ kind: 'effect' | 'settings' | 'control' | 'site' | 'close' | 'native' | 'covered' | 'unmapped', … }` (the module header lists each shape); the record's formula-only values are filled first (`withEnds`).
- `effectRows`: The review rows of the `effect` row type (lib/intake-review.mjs): one per recognised behaviour a visitor or a reviewer confirms once, and one per behaviour no SGS effect expresses. `ctx` is recogniseBehaviour's.
- `hostsFromTemplate`: The attribute selector -> element keys map of a read template: every element whose attributes name the selector's `data-*` hook. `selectors` are the `[data-x]` strings records name (hostOf).

### `lib/motion-behaviour-settings.mjs`
- `positionsByTemplate({ reads, nodes, rootComponent })` → the draft's computed position of every root element a template element is, by width; `stickyTiers(by)` → `headerSticky` per tier from those positions, or null when no tier pins.
- `headerScrollOf(record, ctx)` → the header's scrolled shadow and threshold and, for a pinned header, its sticky behaviour per tier; `dotPulseOf(record)` → the open-status dot pulse; `accordionScrollOf(record)` → scrolling to the accordion row just opened; `autoplayOf(record)` → the testimonial slider's autoplay; `PINNED` and `pinnedTimelineOf(record)` → the pinned horizontal timeline's track, counter, focus and active year as `sgs/timeline` settings.

### `lib/motion-effect-element.mjs`
- `callPathOf(record)` → the functions an animation record's call path passes through, innermost first; `cssTextDeclarations(formula)` → the declarations a `cssText` formula sets; `effectElementStyle(record, records)` → the effect element's declarations for an animation record, or null.
- `colourOf(value)` → a single colour as `{ hex, alpha }`, or null; `largerSideFactor(decl)` → the multiple of its host's larger side a declared length is, or null.

### `lib/motion-record-text.mjs`
- `props`, `nameOf`, `words`, `blob`, `triggerOf`, `durationOf`, `easingOf`: what a record states; `MINUS`, `TIMES`, `NUMBER`, `number`, `round`, `compact`: the number patterns and helpers its formulas are read with.

### `lib/motion-tilt.mjs`
- `fromPointer(property)` → whether a pointer handler wrote the property from the pointer's position (its own formula and value only). `tiltOf(record, ctx)` → a script-written rotation from the pointer's position as a tilt (the hover state's `followPointer`) with a glare layer when the same handler writes the pointer position into custom properties; `tiltGlareHostOf(record, ctx)` → the id of the tilt record whose glare a handler of its own writes, or null; `tiltResetOf(record)` → the spring back of a tilt.

### `lib/trace-match.mjs`
- `names`: A record names its element by template_tpl_id, by nearest_tpl_id, or (structural targets) inside element/trace text.
- `durOk`: duration_ms is a number, or text: "per property" (each properties[] entry carries its own duration_ms) or a formula.
- `eventKey`: An event with no template id or no nearest one is keyed '-', never the text "null".
- `matchFor(key, recs, shift)` → the first record whose duration fits the event key's (`durOk`, with `shift`) and which names the key's template or nearest element (`names`), else undefined.

### `tests/fixtures/motion-fixture-db.mjs`
- `motionFixtureDb`: blocks: { 'sgs/x': [ { target, css_element } ] } (the root is always added).

### `lib/calibrate-basket.mjs`
- `addItemBody(basket)` → the add-item request body for a basket line.
- `setBasketInPage([root, body])` → in the page: empties the basket, then adds `body` when given; returns the line count after, or `{ error }`.
- `setBasket(page, siteUrl, basket)` → fills (basket given) or empties (null) the basket of the site at `siteUrl` from a page on that site; throws on a failed request or a line that did not take.
- `markerShows(reads, n, d)` → whether instance n's marker shows against default d in a read pass: any element or property differing at any width.

### `lib/census-conservation.mjs`
- `layerTimings(element)` → the timing values one sgsMotion element's layers give each longhand, `{ longhand: [value] }`; a layer that sets no delay runs at 0ms.
- `rootLayerTimings(doc)` → `layerTimings` of the root element only: a part's layer times the part, not the write's element.
- `motionLonghands(w)` → the longhands one motion-pass write sets: the root layer's timing longhands, the write's own property when it names one, else none.
- `layerGives(prop, raw, given, tokens)` → whether a timing longhand's read value is what the layer gives it at every entry of the list (times by ms, easings by canonical spelling, `token:` values through the client's tokens).

### `lib/fill-motion-settle.mjs`
- `motionSettled({ unmapped, writes, nodes, flat, refOf, mdb, db, calFor, reads, rawSnapshot })` → settles by `motion` each raised resting row whose every width equals what the written sgsMotion hover state carries; returns `{ unmapped, answered }`. The surface's rows first pass through `lib/fill-timing.mjs::timingDuplicates`.
- `motionRejudged({ settled, writes, nodes, flat, refOf, mdb, db, calFor, rawSnapshot })` → rejudges a resting timing the element pass settled against the stylesheet by the written layer instead: settled `motion` where every width is a value the layer gives, else an UNMAPPED timing-conflict row; returns `{ settled, unmapped }`.
- `HOVER_PAINT` → the colour and shadow properties every sgsMotion hover state transitions beside its movement.
- `baseTransitions()` → the block parts' own base transitions, which a part's motion `transition` keeps, or null where the generated table is absent.
- `transitionListOutcomes({ nodes, flat, refOf, reads, writes, mdb, db, calFor, base })` → settles a resting transition-property or transition-behavior read where live transitions the draft's properties (the calibrated default paint in order, or the written hover state as a set), else an UNMAPPED row naming the difference; returns `{ settled, unmapped }`. A live list that also transitions properties the draft does not is settled too (same reason, the extras named in the detail) when no state look of the element changes any of them at that width, and an inherited one (`lib/calibrate-props.mjs::INHERITED`) also when no look of an ancestor node's targets does; a width with no state read, or a list missing a draft property the movement entries cannot account for, stays raised.

### `lib/fill-state-reach.mjs`
- `stateDrafts` → the draft's state looks per target, grouped by state, pseudo-element and property across the read widths.
- `ancestorLook(reads, rec, target, state, prop)` → the ancestor target whose pointer-state look an inherited property follows, when the element declares no `prop` of its own at any read width; null otherwise.
- `ancestorHolds({ cal, state, slot, prop, ancestor, aprop, taken })` → whether a state setting already written paints the ancestor's `aprop` at its slot, so the element paints no state rule of its own.
- `motionHolds(mdb, rec, target, home)` → whether the block's sgsMotion hover layer holds this target's hover transform, so the state pass leaves it there; a DB without motion tables holds none.

### `lib/form-submit-run.mjs`
- `formSettingsOf(content)` → the form's `{ successMessage, successRedirect, notifyEmail }` from an `sgs_form` post's content (an unset one is ''), or null when it holds no sgs/form block.
- `formFieldsOf(content)` → each field block in the content, `[ { block, label, required, msgRequired } ]` in document order.
- `testValueFor(c)` → a valid test value for a control `{ tag, type, min, options }`: a string, `'check'` to tick it, or null to leave it.
- `successVerdict({ settings, finalUrl, successText })` → whether a submission ended in the form's own success state: the redirect target when it has one, else a visible success message.
- `originalRecipient({ subject, to })` → the address a mail was meant for: the one a test site's redirect names in the subject, else the mail's own `to`.

### `lib/form-submit-page.mjs`
- `FORM_SELECTOR` → `form.sgs-form__inner`; `REQUIRED` → its required controls.
- `markedValueFor(info, marker)` → `testValueFor`'s value, with every free-text control carrying the marker when one is given.
- `fillVisible(page, skip, { marker, form })` → fills the reachable required controls of `form` (a selector; a visually hidden tile through its visible label; never the honeypot), leaving the first `skip` empty; with a marker also its empty free-text fields; returns the required controls it cannot fill (a file field).
- `stepInPage(form)` → `{ at, of, label, decoys }`: the shown `.sgs-form-step` and the visible Next/Continue buttons that are not the form's own.
- `submitForm(page, skip, { marker, form, settleMs })` → walks the form's steps with its own Next (`.sgs-form__button--next`, else a button reading Next or Continue) and clicks its submit button; returns `{ steps, decoys }`; throws `stuck on step N of M ("label")…` when a Next click leaves the shown step unchanged, and when neither a submit nor a next button shows.

### `lib/site-check-report.mjs`
- `CHECKS` → the eight checks `{ id, n, title }`; `parseOnly(raw)` → `{ ids }` or `{ error }`.
- `verdictOf(items)` → `fail` when an item failed or carries an unknown status, `pass` when one passed, else `n-a`; `checkRecord(id, items, naReason)` → the check's record; `exitCodeOf(checks)` → 1 when one failed.
- `skippedRecord(id)` → the record of a check `--only` left out; `evidenceOf(item)` → an item's evidence line (url, selector, screenshot, error, detail).
- `renderMarkdown(result)` → `result.md`: the table by check, the failures with evidence, then every item.

### `lib/site-check-site.mjs`
- `targetOf(site, targets, roots)`, `siteBase(target, roots)` → the target and its `WP_URL_*` (never another secret); `candidateRoots` → the repo, then a worktree's main checkout (re-exported from `scripts/parity/flows/lib/browser.mjs`).
- `STORE_EXAMPLES` → the Store API list a product or product-category row opens its example from.
- `rowsFor(rows, idsSite, target)`, `readRows(repo, client, target)` → the page map with its ids kept for the site that owns them or its mirror.
- `pageTargets(rows, base)` → `{ pages, examples, skipped }`; `flowsClient(outRoot)` → the client `scripts/parity/flows` belongs to, from `lib/flow.mjs::flowsOutRoot`.

### `lib/site-check-nav.mjs`
- `MARK` → the `data-site-check` attribute the in-page readers mark triggers and targets with.
- `shownInPage(el)` → rendered and not hidden (below the fold counts); `onScreenInPage(el)` → also overlapping the viewport (an opened panel); `stateInPage([mark, trigger, target, src])` → `{ expanded, shown }` of a marked pair.
- `pathKey(href, base)` → origin and path without the trailing slash, for comparing links.
- `readHeaderLinksInPage()` → the header's links with their menu-bar flag; `readDraftLinksInPage()` → the page's links to a `.dc.html` draft file.
- `markMegaInPage(mark)`, `markDrawerInPage(mark)` → mark the visible mega triggers and their panels, the visible burger and its drawer.
- `menuItems({ header, links, pages, examples, headerRows, draftLinks, megaTriggers, base, url })` → the menu check's items; a `sgs_mega_menu` header row passes on the trigger in `megaTriggers` (from `markMegaInPage`) whose panel id ends `-mega-<id>` and whose panel is on the page.
- `headerRowsOf({ rows, loaded, menuLinks })` → the page-map rows in the header menu as `menuItems` takes them; a product or category row carries the draft's link `text` and the resolved `url` (with its query) instead of a page address, so it is matched by them, never by its title. `addressKey(href, base)` → origin, path and query, for comparing a link with a query.
- `megaItems(page, { url, shoot })`, `drawerItem(page, { width, url, shoot })` → one item per mega trigger, one per width.
- `openDisclosure(page, trigger, target)`, `closeDisclosure(page, trigger, target, closer)` → open by hover, focus and ArrowDown, then a click; close by the close button, Escape, then moving away.

### `lib/site-check-parts.mjs`
- `readPartsInPage(mark)` → the page's marked modals, choice flows and forms; `FLOW_ROOT` → a choice flow's root selector; `flowStateInPage` → its shown step, step count, Back and Continue.
- `missingFlowRows(rows, flows)` → the page-map `sgs_choice_flow` rows no rendered flow shows: a flow naming a slug (`data-flow-slug`) is the row of that slug; a flow naming none is the row its `aria-label` matches.
- `WOO_PAGES` → what the cart, checkout and account pages must show; `wooStateInPage` → whether it shows, else the page's first words.
- `modalItem`, `flowItem`, `formItem`, `wooItem` → one item each; `addOneLine(page, base)` → one basket line through the Store API for `--with-cart`.

### `lib/intake-palette-declared.mjs`
- `ROLE_WORDS` → the role vocabulary read from `data/palette-role-words.json`.
- `namespaceOf(names)` → the namespace word most colour tokens share (`mm` in `--mm-cocoa`), or null; kept off alias names before matching.
- `declarations({ tokens, folder, slugs, words })` → every declared role, `[ { slug, hex, token, strength, source: 'alias'|'comment'|'readme', why } ]`.
- `declaredRoles(list)` → the winning (strongest) declaration per slug as a Map, and `conflicts` for ties between different colours, which drop the slug.

### `lib/intake-palette-into.mjs`
- `PALETTE_ONLY_NOTE` → the note a palette-only `intake.json` carries saying the full intake must run for the other stages.
- `rowRoles(r)` → the slugs a palette row proposes: its roles, else its one role, else none.
- `markRoles(mark, oldRow)` → the slugs a mark names on a row, or null when it names none that can be compared.
- `intoIntake({ intake, reviewMd, palette, client, takenOn })` → `{ intake, reviewMd, created, kept, dropped }`: the new palette block put into the current record and review text, keeping a mark only when its slugs equal the new proposal's.

### `lib/intake-palette-run.mjs`
- `writeIntoIntake(at, out, say)` → `--into-intake`: the proposal written into `intake.json` and `review.md` through `intoIntake`.
- `PALETTE_STAGES` → the stages a palette-only run executes: routing, replay, tokens and palette.
- `runPaletteOnly(o, { log })` → runs only those stages for `intake.mjs --palette-only` and returns the palette result.

### `lib/intake-palette-usage.mjs`
- `isBackground(p)` → whether a CSS property is `background` or `background-color`.
- `isBorder(p)` → whether a CSS property is a border or outline property, side and colour forms included.
- `usageRoles(groups, uses)` → the roles the painted usage gives each colour group, from the normalised usage rows; with no text flags every colour row counts as text.

### `lib/origin-file.mjs`
- `draftHashOf(manifest)` → sha256 over every `.dc.html` file's path and hash in a handoff manifest, in path order; null when it lists no template.
- `stampOrigin(origin, hash)` → the origin with every entry stamped with the draft hash; the input is not changed.
- `originVerdict(origin, hash)` → `{ ok, stamped, why }`: ok false when any entry names another hash, stamped false when no entry carries one.
- `readOrigin(originFile, manifestFile)` → reads an origin file and checks it against `MANIFEST.json`; throws when it is from another export; returns `{ origin, verdict }`.

### `lib/provision-product.mjs`
- `PRODUCT_SPEC` → the calibration product's spec: its attributes, terms and variations.
- `PRODUCT_SPEC_KEY` → a short hash over the spec and the PHP that applies it, so a product completed by an older spec or apply is completed again.
- `BASKET_VARIATION` → the basket line calibration adds before reading sgs/cart: the first term of each variation attribute.
- `productStatePhp()` → read-only PHP: whether the found product is at this spec.
- `productApplyPhp()` → PHP that finds the product by title or creates it and completes it to the spec.

### `lib/resolve-nearest.mjs`
- `encloses(home, slot)` → whether the element at path `home` is the element at `slot` or one of its ancestors; an undefined home encloses nothing.
- `nearestHome(rows, homeOf, slot)` → the tied rows whose home is nearest the read element, or null when no row's home can be compared.

### `lib/resolve-side-effects.mjs`
- `sideEffectFree(rows, { effectsOf, slot, same, siblings, paintOf, equal, isLength })` → the tied rows a side effect does not rule out, or `rows` unchanged when none would remain.

### `lib/fill-list-content.mjs`

- `listClaims(block, target)` → `[{ block, attr }]` for the settings a list target takes the draft's items into (the host's one array setting, or each content setting of the single child block it allows); `lib/content-coverage.mjs` claims these for the list route.

### `lib/content-coverage.mjs`
- `NO_ELEMENT_SHAPES` → the `emit_shape` values that mean the page holds no element a draft could be read from (`script-rendered`, `editor-only`, `output-only`, `context`); every other shape names an element the draft draws.
- `ROUTES` → `measured`, `list`, `role-writer`, `no-element`, `gap`: the ways a content setting is filled or accounted for.
- `contentRows(db)` → every `sgs/` block setting whose role the `roles` table classifies content-bearing.
- `gapRowsOf(coverage)` → the per-setting gap rows of a coverage map, by `<block>.<setting>`.
- `claimsOf({ db, cacheDir, schemaOf, facts, blocks })` → a map from `<block>.<setting>` to the `{ route, writer }` claims the writers' own selection functions make.
- `contentCoverage({ db, cacheDir, schemaOf, facts, coverage })` → `{ rows, counts, writers, failing, staleGaps, badGaps, ok }`: each content row's route, the rows with none (with why each route missed), the gap rows a writer now fills, and the gap rows naming a setting the DB no longer holds.
- `reportLines(result)` → the check's report as lines.

### `shop-fixtures.mjs`
- `FIXTURE_SITES` → the fresh test sites and local mirrors the shop fixtures may be written to (`mamas-test`, `eye-care-clone-test`, `local-mamas`, `local-eye-care`).

### `shop-parts.mjs`
- `themeFiles(vocab, repo)` → the theme files the shop parts vocabulary names, read from the repo.
- `draftSurfaces(client, repo)` → one client's draft surfaces (`lib/shop-parts-read.mjs::readShopSurfaces`), or null when its export has no `surfaces.json`.

### `tests/fixtures/frozen-db/frozen-db.mjs`
- `BLOCKS` → the blocks the frozen tests build or plan.
- `frozenDb()` → builds the frozen slice as a read-only fixture DB and returns its path.
- `sliceOf(db)` → the `blocks`, `block_attributes` and `property_equivalents` rows of a DB, each row's non-null columns only, in a stable order.

### `tests/palette-keys.test.mjs`
- `slugValues(roles, hexOf)` → slug → hex from token → slugs (framework slugs only).
- `compareClient(client)` → one client's proposal compared with its answer key.
- `REVIEW` → every remaining difference, slug → `{ key, proposal, class, check }`; each is a choice of the key that no rendered or code fact reproduces.
- `NAME_ONLY` → the slugs the proposal takes from a token name alone, each a review class.
- `KEY_ONLY` → the key's own slugs that are not in theme.json's palette, slug → the token it names.

### `tools/palette-keys.mjs`
- `keyFile(client)` → the path of a client's `palette-key.json`.
- `fromMarks(client)` → token → slugs from Bean's palette marks in `review.md`.
- `fromSnapshot(client)` → token → the snapshot slugs whose colour equals the token's value.
- `derive(client, source)` → `fromMarks` or `fromSnapshot` by the `source` name, else null.
